import type { Slide } from '../../domain/entities/Slide';

export interface SlideStore {

   * Get current slides
   */
  getSlides(): Slide[];

  /**
   * Get active slide
   */
  getActiveSlide(): Slide | null;

  /**
   * Get active slide ID
   */
  getActiveSlideId(): string | null;

  /**
   * Set slides
   */
  setSlides(slides: Slide[]): void;

  /**
   * Set active slide
   */
  setActiveSlideId(id: string): void;

  /**
   * Add slide
   */
  addSlide(slide: Slide): void;

  /**
   * Remove slide
   */
  removeSlide(id: string): void;

  /**
   * Update slide
   */
  updateSlide(id: string, patch: Partial<Slide>): void;

  /**
   * Add element to slide
   */
  addElement(slideId: string, element: CanvasElement): void;

  /**
   * Update element in slide
   */
  updateElement(slideId: string, elementId: string, patch: Partial<CanvasElement>): void;

  /**
   * Remove element from slide
   */
  removeElement(slideId: string, elementId: string): void;

  /**
   * Subscribe to changes
   */
  subscribe(callback: () => void): () => void;
}

/**
 * Slide Use Cases - Application Layer
 * Business logic orchestration
 */

import { Slide } from '../../domain/entities/Slide';
import type { SlideStore } from '../ports/SlideStore';
import type { DocFormat, Orientation, CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';

export interface CreateSlideInput {
  name?: string;
  format?: DocFormat;
  orientation?: Orientation;
}

export interface UpdateElementInput {
  slideId: string;
  elementId: string;
  patch: Partial<CanvasElement>;
}

export class SlideUseCases {
  constructor(private readonly slideStore: SlideStore) {}

  /**
   * Create new slide
   */
  createSlide(input: CreateSlideInput = {}): Slide {
    const slide = new Slide({
      name: input.name,
      format: input.format,
      orientation: input.orientation,
    });

    this.slideStore.addSlide(slide);
    return slide;
  }

  /**
   * Remove slide
   */
  removeSlide(id: string): void {
    const slides = this.slideStore.getSlides();
    if (slides.length <= 1) {
      throw new Error('Cannot remove the last slide');
    }
    this.slideStore.removeSlide(id);
  }

  /**
   * Change slide format
   */
  changeFormat(slideId: string, format: DocFormat, customSize?: { w: number; h: number }): void {
    const slide = this.slideStore.getSlides().find((s) => s.id === slideId);
    if (!slide) {
      throw new Error(`Slide not found: ${slideId}`);
    }

    slide.changeFormat(format, customSize);
    this.slideStore.updateSlide(slideId, slide);
  }

  /**
   * Change slide orientation
   */
  changeOrientation(slideId: string, orientation: Orientation): void {
    const slide = this.slideStore.getSlides().find((s) => s.id === slideId);
    if (!slide) {
      throw new Error(`Slide not found: ${slideId}`);
    }

    slide.changeOrientation(orientation);
    this.slideStore.updateSlide(slideId, slide);
  }

  /**
   * Add element to slide
   */
  addElement(slideId: string, element: CanvasElement): void {
    this.slideStore.addElement(slideId, element);
  }

  /**
   * Update element
   */
  updateElement(input: UpdateElementInput): void {
    this.slideStore.updateElement(input.slideId, input.elementId, input.patch);
  }

  /**
   * Remove element
   */
  removeElement(slideId: string, elementId: string): void {
    this.slideStore.removeElement(slideId, elementId);
  }

  /**
   * Move element layer
   */
  moveElementLayer(slideId: string, elementId: string, direction: 1 | -1): void {
    const slide = this.slideStore.getSlides().find((s) => s.id === slideId);
    if (!slide) return;

    slide.moveElementLayer(elementId, direction);
    this.slideStore.updateSlide(slideId, slide);
  }

  /**
   * Duplicate slide
   */
  duplicateSlide(slideId: string): Slide | null {
    const slide = this.slideStore.getSlides().find((s) => s.id === slideId);
    if (!slide) return null;

    const clone = slide.clone();
    this.slideStore.addSlide(clone);
    return clone;
  }

  /**
   * Set active slide
   */
  setActiveSlide(id: string): void {
    this.slideStore.setActiveSlideId(id);
  }

  /**
   * Get slides
   */
  getSlides(): Slide[] {
    return this.slideStore.getSlides();
  }

  /**
   * Get active slide
   */
  getActiveSlide(): Slide | null {
    return this.slideStore.getActiveSlide();
  }
}

// Factory function
export function createSlideUseCases(slideStore: SlideStore): SlideUseCases {
  return new SlideUseCases(slideStore);
}

FILE: domain\document\DocumentStore.ts
SIZE: 2044 bytes

import { produce } from 'immer';
import type { Slide, DataRoot } from '../../shared/types';
import { SimpleStore } from '../../shared/store/store';
import { Slide as SlideClass } from '../entities/Slide';

export interface DocumentState {
  slides: Slide[];
  activeId: string;
  data: DataRoot[];
}

interface HistoryEntry {
  state: DocumentState;
  mergeKey?: string;
  timestamp: number;
}

const MAX_HISTORY = 100;
const MERGE_TIMEOUT = 600;

export class DocumentStore extends SimpleStore<DocumentState> {
  private history: HistoryEntry[] = [];
  private historyIndex = -1;

  constructor() {
    const initial = new SlideClass({ name: 'РЎР»Р°Р№Рґ 1' }).toPlainObject();
    super({
      slides: [initial],
      activeId: initial.id,
      data: [],
    });
    this.pushHistory();
  }

  commit(recipe: (draft: DocumentState) => void, mergeKey?: string) {
    const nextState = produce(this.state, recipe);
    this.setState(nextState);
    this.pushHistory(mergeKey);
  }

  private pushHistory(mergeKey?: string) {
    const now = Date.now();
    const last = this.history[this.historyIndex];

    if (mergeKey && last?.mergeKey === mergeKey && now - last.timestamp < MERGE_TIMEOUT) {
      this.history[this.historyIndex] = { state: this.state, mergeKey, timestamp: now };
      return;
    }

    this.history = this.history.slice(0, this.historyIndex + 1);
    this.history.push({ state: this.state, mergeKey, timestamp: now });
    if (this.history.length > MAX_HISTORY) this.history.shift();
    else this.historyIndex++;
  }

  undo() {
    if (this.historyIndex > 0) {
      this.historyIndex--;
      this.setState(this.history[this.historyIndex].state);
    }
  }

  redo() {
    if (this.historyIndex < this.history.length - 1) {
      this.historyIndex++;
      this.setState(this.history[this.historyIndex].state);
    }
  }

  get canUndo() { return this.historyIndex > 0; }
  get canRedo() { return this.historyIndex < this.history.length - 1; }
}

/* ============================================================
   domain/elements/registry.ts
   ============================================================ */

import React from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';

export interface ElementStrategy<T extends CanvasElement = CanvasElement> {
  type: T['type'];
  KonvaView: React.FC<{
    element: T;
    isSelected: boolean;
    onSelect: () => void;
    onUpdate: (patch: Partial<T>) => void;
    onDblClick?: () => void;
  }>;
  toSvg: (element: T) => string;
}

const registry = new Map<string, ElementStrategy>();

export function registerElement<T extends CanvasElement>(strategy: ElementStrategy<T>) {
  registry.set(strategy.type, strategy as ElementStrategy);
}

export function getElementStrategy(type: string): ElementStrategy {
  const strategy = registry.get(type);
  if (!strategy) throw new Error(`[registry] no strategy for type: ${type}`);
  return strategy;
}

/**
 * Slide Entity - Domain Layer
 * Core business logic for slides
 */

import type { Slide as SlideType, CanvasElement, Padding, DocFormat, Orientation } from '../../shared/types';
import { FORMATS, DEFAULT_PADDING } from '../../shared/constants';


export interface SlideProps {
  readonly id?: string;
  readonly name?: string;
  readonly format?: DocFormat;
  readonly orientation?: Orientation;
  readonly width?: number;
  readonly height?: number;
  readonly background?: string;
  readonly backgroundImage?: string;
  readonly padding?: Padding;
  readonly elements?: readonly CanvasElement[];
}

export class Slide {
  readonly id: string;
  name: string;
  format: DocFormat;
  orientation: Orientation;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  background: string;
  backgroundImage?: string;
  padding: Padding;
  elements: CanvasElement[];

  constructor(props: SlideProps = {}) {
    const format = props.format ?? 'a4';
    const orientation = props.orientation ?? 'portrait';
    const formatSize = FORMATS[format].portrait;
    
    this.id = props.id ?? generateId();
    this.name = props.name ?? 'Slide';
    this.format = format;
    this.orientation = orientation;
    this.width = props.width ?? (orientation === 'portrait' ? formatSize.w : formatSize.h);
    this.height = props.height ?? (orientation === 'portrait' ? formatSize.h : formatSize.w);
    this.background = props.background ?? '#ffffff';
    this.backgroundImage = props.backgroundImage;
    this.padding = props.padding ?? { ...DEFAULT_PADDING };
    this.elements = props.elements ? [...props.elements] : [];
  }

  /**
   * Get printable area dimensions
   */
  get printableArea(): { width: number; height: number } {
    return {
      width: Math.max(0, this.width - this.padding.left - this.padding.right),
      height: Math.max(0, this.height - this.padding.top - this.padding.bottom),
    };
  }

  /**
   * Add element to slide
   */
  addElement(element: CanvasElement): void {
    this.elements = [...this.elements, element];
  }

  /**
   * Remove element by ID
   */
  removeElement(elementId: string): void {
    this.elements = this.elements.filter(el => el.id !== elementId);
  }

  /**
   * Update element by ID
   */
  updateElement(elementId: string, patch: Partial<CanvasElement>): void {
    this.elements = this.elements.map(el =>
      el.id === elementId ? { ...el, ...patch } as CanvasElement : el
    );
  }

  /**
   * Get element by ID
   */
  getElementById(elementId: string): CanvasElement | undefined {
    return this.elements.find(el => el.id === elementId);
  }

  /**
   * Move element in z-order
   */
  moveElementLayer(elementId: string, direction: 1 | -1): void {
    const index = this.elements.findIndex(el => el.id === elementId);
    if (index < 0) return;
    
    const newIndex = index + direction;
    if (newIndex < 0 || newIndex >= this.elements.length) return;
    
    const newElements = [...this.elements];
    [newElements[index], newElements[newIndex]] = [newElements[newIndex], newElements[index]];
    this.elements = newElements;
  }

  /**
   * Change format and recalculate dimensions
   */
  changeFormat(format: DocFormat, customSize?: { w: number; h: number }): void {
    const formatSize = FORMATS[format].portrait;
    const baseSize = format === 'custom' && customSize ? customSize : formatSize;
    
    const newWidth = this.orientation === 'portrait' ? baseSize.w : baseSize.h;
    const newHeight = this.orientation === 'portrait' ? baseSize.h : baseSize.w;
    
    this.reflowElements(newWidth, newHeight);
    
    this.format = format;
    this.width = newWidth;
    this.height = newHeight;
  }

  /**
   * Change orientation and swap dimensions
   */
  changeOrientation(orientation: Orientation): void {
    if (this.orientation === orientation) return;
    
    const formatSize = FORMATS[this.format].portrait;
    const baseSize = this.format === 'custom' 
      ? { w: this.height, h: this.width } // Swap current
      : formatSize;
    
    const newWidth = orientation === 'portrait' ? baseSize.w : baseSize.h;
    const newHeight = orientation === 'portrait' ? baseSize.h : baseSize.w;
    
    this.reflowElements(newWidth, newHeight);
    
    this.orientation = orientation;
    this.width = newWidth;
    this.height = newHeight;
  }

  /**
   * Reflow elements when slide dimensions change
   */
  private reflowElements(newWidth: number, newHeight: number): void {
    const printableWidth = Math.max(0, newWidth - this.padding.left - this.padding.right);
    const printableHeight = Math.max(0, newHeight - this.padding.top - this.padding.bottom);

    this.elements = this.elements.map(el => {
      // Footer: stick to bottom
      if (el.type === 'footer') {
        return {
          ...el,
          x: this.padding.left,
          y: newHeight - this.padding.bottom - el.height,
          width: printableWidth,
        };
      }

      // Placeholder: fill printable area
      if (el.type === 'placeholder') {
        return {
          ...el,
          x: this.padding.left,
          y: this.padding.top,
          width: printableWidth,
          height: printableHeight,
        };
      }

      // Other elements: clamp to new bounds
      const maxX = Math.max(0, newWidth - el.width);
      const maxY = Math.max(0, newHeight - el.height);

      return {
        ...el,
        x: Math.min(el.x, maxX),
        y: Math.min(el.y, maxY),
        width: Math.min(el.width, printableWidth),
      };
    });
  }

  /**
   * Clone slide with new ID
   */
  clone(): Slide {
    return new Slide({
      name: `${this.name} (Copy)`,
      format: this.format,
      orientation: this.orientation,
      width: this.width,
      height: this.height,
      background: this.background,
      backgroundImage: this.backgroundImage,
      padding: { ...this.padding },
      elements: this.elements.map(el => ({ ...el, id: generateId() })),
    });
  }

  /**
   * Convert to plain object
   */
  toPlainObject(): SlideType {
    return {
      id: this.id,
      name: this.name,
      format: this.format,
      orientation: this.orientation,
      width: this.width,
      height: this.height,
      background: this.background,
      backgroundImage: this.backgroundImage,
      padding: { ...this.padding },
      elements: [...this.elements],
    };
  }

  /**
   * Create from plain object
   */
  static fromPlainObject(obj: SlideType): Slide {
    return new Slide({
      id: obj.id,
      name: obj.name,
      format: obj.format,
      orientation: obj.orientation,
      width: obj.width,
      height: obj.height,
      background: obj.background,
      backgroundImage: obj.backgroundImage,
      padding: obj.padding,
      elements: obj.elements,
    });
  }
}

// Import at the end to avoid circular dependency
import { generateId } from '../../shared/utils/id';

/**
 * Slide Repository Interface - Domain Layer
 * Abstract interface for slide persistence
 */

import type { Slide } from '../entities/Slide';

export interface SlideRepository {
  /**
   * Get all slides
   */
  getAll(): Promise<Slide[]>;

  /**
   * Get slide by ID
   */
  getById(id: string): Promise<Slide | null>;

  /**
   * Save slide
   */
  save(slide: Slide): Promise<void>;

  /**
   * Delete slide
   */
  delete(id: string): Promise<void>;

  /**
   * Save all slides
   */
  saveAll(slides: Slide[]): Promise<void>;
}

/**
 * In-memory implementation for testing
 */
export class InMemorySlideRepository implements SlideRepository {
  private slides = new Map<string, Slide>();

  async getAll(): Promise<Slide[]> {
    return Array.from(this.slides.values());
  }

  async getById(id: string): Promise<Slide | null> {
    return this.slides.get(id) ?? null;
  }

  async save(slide: Slide): Promise<void> {
    this.slides.set(slide.id, slide);
  }

  async delete(id: string): Promise<void> {
    this.slides.delete(id);
  }

  async saveAll(slides: Slide[]): Promise<void> {
    for (const slide of slides) {
      this.slides.set(slide.id, slide);
    }
  }

  clear(): void {
    this.slides.clear();
  }
}

/**
 * Slide Store Implementation - Infrastructure Layer
 * Concrete implementation using our Store
 */

import { Slide } from '../../domain/entities/Slide';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';
import type { SlideStore } from '../../application/ports/SlideStore';
import { createStore } from './Store';

interface SlideStoreState {
  slides: Slide[];
  activeSlideId: string | null;
}

const initialState: SlideStoreState = {
  slides: [new Slide({ name: 'Slide 1' })],
  activeSlideId: '',
};

// Initialize with first slide
initialState.activeSlideId = initialState.slides[0].id;

const store = createStore({ initialState });

/**
 * Concrete implementation of SlideStore
 */
export class SlideStoreImpl implements SlideStore {
  getSlides(): Slide[] {
    return store.select((s) => s.slides);
  }

  getActiveSlide(): Slide | null {
    const { slides, activeSlideId } = store.getState();
    return slides.find((s) => s.id === activeSlideId) ?? null;
  }

  getActiveSlideId(): string | null {
    return store.select((s) => s.activeSlideId);
  }

  setSlides(slides: Slide[]): void {
    store.setStatePartial({ slides });
  }

  setActiveSlideId(id: string): void {
    store.setStatePartial({ activeSlideId: id });
  }

  addSlide(slide: Slide): void {
    store.updateState((state) => ({
      ...state,
      slides: [...state.slides, slide],
      activeSlideId: slide.id,
    }));
  }

  removeSlide(id: string): void {
    store.updateState((state) => {
      const newSlides = state.slides.filter((s) => s.id !== id);
      const newActiveId =
        state.activeSlideId === id
          ? newSlides.length > 0
            ? newSlides[0].id
            : null
          : state.activeSlideId;

      return {
        ...state,
        slides: newSlides,
        activeSlideId: newActiveId,
      };
    });
  }

  updateSlide(id: string, patch: Partial<Slide>): void {
    store.updateState((state) => ({
      ...state,
      slides: state.slides.map((s) => {
        if (s.id !== id) return s;
        // Preserve Slide prototype by creating new instance
        return Slide.fromPlainObject({ ...s, ...patch });
      }),
    }));
  }

  addElement(slideId: string, element: CanvasElement): void {
    store.updateState((state) => ({
      ...state,
      slides: state.slides.map((s) =>
        s.id === slideId
          ? { ...s, elements: [...s.elements, element] }
          : s
      ),
    }));
  }

  updateElement(slideId: string, elementId: string, patch: Partial<CanvasElement>): void {
    store.updateState((state) => ({
      ...state,
      slides: state.slides.map((s) =>
        s.id === slideId
          ? {
              ...s,
              elements: s.elements.map((e) =>
                e.id === elementId ? { ...e, ...patch } as CanvasElement : e
              ),
            }
          : s
      ),
    }));
  }

  removeElement(slideId: string, elementId: string): void {
    store.updateState((state) => ({
      ...state,
      slides: state.slides.map((s) =>
        s.id === slideId
          ? {
              ...s,
              elements: s.elements.filter((e) => e.id !== elementId),
            }
          : s
      ),
    }));
  }

  subscribe(callback: () => void): () => void {
    return store.subscribe(callback);
  }
}

// Singleton instance
export const slideStore = new SlideStoreImpl();

// React hook
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

export function useSlideStore(): SlideStoreState {
  const [state, setState] = useState(store.getState());

  useEffect(() => {
    return store.subscribe(setState);
  }, []);

  return state;
}

export function useActiveSlide(): Slide | null {
  const [slide, setSlide] = useState<Slide | null>(slideStore.getActiveSlide());

  useEffect(() => {
    return store.subscribe(() => {
      setSlide(slideStore.getActiveSlide());
    });
  }, []);

  return slide;
}

/**
 * Simple reactive store - Infrastructure Layer
 * Lightweight alternative to external state management
 */

export type Listener<T> = (state: T) => void;
export type Selector<T, R> = (state: T) => R;

export interface StoreConfig<T> {
  initialState: T;
}

export class Store<T extends Record<string, unknown>> {
  private state: T;
  private listeners = new Set<Listener<T>>();

  constructor(config: StoreConfig<T>) {
    this.state = config.initialState;
  }

  /**
   * Get current state
   */
  getState(): T {
    return this.state;
  }

  /**
   * Get derived value via selector
   */
  select<R>(selector: Selector<T, R>): R {
    return selector(this.state);
  }

  /**
   * Subscribe to state changes
   */
  subscribe(listener: Listener<T>): () => void {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => this.listeners.delete(listener);
  }

  /**
   * Subscribe to specific value changes
   */
  subscribeTo<R>(selector: Selector<T, R>, listener: (value: R, prevValue: R) => void): () => void {
    let prevValue = selector(this.state);
    
    return this.subscribe((state) => {
      const newValue = selector(state);
      if (newValue !== prevValue) {
        listener(newValue, prevValue);
        prevValue = newValue;
      }
    });
  }

  /**
   * Set state (full replacement)
   */
  setState(newState: T): void {
    this.state = newState;
    this.notify();
  }

  /**
   * Update state (partial merge)
   */
  setStatePartial(partial: Partial<T>): void {
    this.state = { ...this.state, ...partial } as T;
    this.notify();
  }

  /**
   * Update state with function
   */
  updateState(updater: (state: T) => T): void {
    this.state = updater(this.state);
    this.notify();
  }

  /**
   * Notify all listeners
   */
  private notify(): void {
    for (const listener of this.listeners) {
      try {
        listener(this.state);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Store listener error:', error);
      }
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Create store factory
 */
export function createStore<T extends Record<string, unknown>>(config: StoreConfig<T>): Store<T> {
  return new Store(config);
}

import type { CanvasElement, AnchorSide, ConnectorRouting } from '../shared/types';
import { uid } from './uid';

export const ANCHOR_OFFSET = 8;
/** РћС‚СЃС‚СѓРї, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Р№ РїСЂРёР±Р°РІР»СЏРµС‚СЃСЏ Рє bbox РёСЃС‚РѕС‡РЅРёРєР° РїСЂРё РѕС‚С‚Р°Р»РєРёРІР°РЅРёРё ghost. */
export const GHOST_MIN_GAP = 40;

export const GHOST_MIN_W = 80;
export const GHOST_MIN_H = 40;
export const GHOST_MAX_W = 320;
export const GHOST_MAX_H = 240;

export function getAnchorPoint(
  el: CanvasElement,
  side: AnchorSide,
): { x: number; y: number } {
  const cx = el.x + el.width / 2;
  const cy = el.y + el.height / 2;
  const rot = ((el.rotation || 0) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const cos = Math.cos(rot);
  const sin = Math.sin(rot);

  let lx = 0, ly = 0;
  if (side === 'top') { lx = 0; ly = -el.height / 2 - ANCHOR_OFFSET; }
  else if (side === 'right') { lx = el.width / 2 + ANCHOR_OFFSET; ly = 0; }
  else if (side === 'bottom') { lx = 0; ly = el.height / 2 + ANCHOR_OFFSET; }
  else { lx = -el.width / 2 - ANCHOR_OFFSET; ly = 0; }

  return {
    x: cx + lx * cos - ly * sin,
    y: cy + lx * sin + ly * cos,
  };
}

export function getAnchorDirection(side: AnchorSide): { dx: number; dy: number } {
  if (side === 'top') return { dx: 0, dy: -1 };
  if (side === 'right') return { dx: 1, dy: 0 };
  if (side === 'bottom') return { dx: 0, dy: 1 };
  return { dx: -1, dy: 0 };
}

/* ============================================================
   РњР°СЂС€СЂСѓС‚РёР·Р°С†РёСЏ
   ------------------------------------------------------------
   Р‘РµР· ROUTE_PADDING: СЃРЅР°С‡Р°Р»Р° Р»РѕРјР°Р»Р°СЃСЊ РіРµРѕРјРµС‚СЂРёСЏ, РєРѕРіРґР° СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚С‹
   СЃС‚РѕСЏС‚ Р±Р»РёР·РєРѕ. РўРµРїРµСЂСЊ РјР°СЂС€СЂСѓС‚ РёРґС‘С‚ СЂРѕРІРЅРѕ РѕС‚ anchor Рє anchor,
   Р° В«РєСЂСЋС‡РѕРєВ» РІ Z-РѕР±СЂР°Р·РЅРѕР№ С„РѕСЂРјРµ РґРµР»Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ РІ СЃРµСЂРµРґРёРЅРµ.
   ============================================================ */

function routeStraight(
  from: CanvasElement, fromAnchor: AnchorSide,
  to: CanvasElement, toAnchor: AnchorSide,
): number[] {
  const a = getAnchorPoint(from, fromAnchor);
  const b = getAnchorPoint(to, toAnchor);
  return [a.x, a.y, b.x, b.y];
}

function routeOrthogonal(
  from: CanvasElement, fromAnchor: AnchorSide,
  to: CanvasElement, toAnchor: AnchorSide,
): number[] {
  const a = getAnchorPoint(from, fromAnchor);
  const b = getAnchorPoint(to, toAnchor);

  const isFromHoriz = fromAnchor === 'left' || fromAnchor === 'right';
  const isToHoriz = toAnchor === 'left' || toAnchor === 'right';

  if (isFromHoriz && isToHoriz) {
    const midX = (a.x + b.x) / 2;
    return [a.x, a.y, midX, a.y, midX, b.y, b.x, b.y];
  }
  if (!isFromHoriz && !isToHoriz) {
    const midY = (a.y + b.y) / 2;
    return [a.x, a.y, a.x, midY, b.x, midY, b.x, b.y];
  }
  if (isFromHoriz && !isToHoriz) {
    return [a.x, a.y, b.x, a.y, b.x, b.y];
  }
  return [a.x, a.y, a.x, b.y, b.x, b.y];
}

export function routeConnector(
  from: CanvasElement, fromAnchor: AnchorSide,
  to: CanvasElement, toAnchor: AnchorSide,
  routing: ConnectorRouting,
): number[] {
  if (routing === 'straight') return routeStraight(from, fromAnchor, to, toAnchor);
  return routeOrthogonal(from, fromAnchor, to, toAnchor);
}

/* ============================================================
   РџРѕРёСЃРє
   ============================================================ */

export function findNearestAnchor(
  el: CanvasElement,
  px: number,
  py: number,
): { side: AnchorSide; distance: number } {
  const sides: AnchorSide[] = ['top', 'right', 'bottom', 'left'];
  let best: { side: AnchorSide; distance: number } = { side: 'top', distance: Infinity };
  for (const side of sides) {
    const p = getAnchorPoint(el, side);
    const d = Math.hypot(p.x - px, p.y - py);
    if (d < best.distance) best = { side, distance: d };
  }
  return best;
}

export function findElementAt(
  elements: CanvasElement[],
  px: number,
  py: number,
  excludeIds: string[] = [],
): CanvasElement | null {
  for (let i = elements.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const el = elements[i];
    if (el.type === 'connector') continue;
    if (el.type === 'line') continue;
    if (excludeIds.includes(el.id)) continue;
    if (el.locked) continue;
    if (px >= el.x && px <= el.x + el.width && py >= el.y && py <= el.y + el.height) {
      return el;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export function findElementWithPadding(
  elements: CanvasElement[],
  px: number,
  py: number,
  padding: number,
  excludeIds: string[] = [],
): CanvasElement | null {
  for (let i = elements.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const el = elements[i];
    if (el.type === 'connector') continue;
    if (el.type === 'line') continue;
    if (excludeIds.includes(el.id)) continue;
    if (el.locked) continue;
    if (
      px >= el.x - padding && px <= el.x + el.width + padding &&
      py >= el.y - padding && py <= el.y + el.height + padding
    ) {
      return el;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export function resolveConnectorEnds(
  conn: { fromId: string; toId: string },
  elements: CanvasElement[],
): { from: CanvasElement | null; to: CanvasElement | null } {
  let from: CanvasElement | null = null;
  let to: CanvasElement | null = null;
  for (const el of elements) {
    if (el.id === conn.fromId) from = el;
    if (el.id === conn.toId) to = el;
    if (from && to) break;
  }
  return { from, to };
}

export function midpointOfPath(points: number[]): { x: number; y: number } {
  let totalLen = 0;
  const segs: { x1: number; y1: number; x2: number; y2: number; len: number }[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < points.length - 2; i += 2) {
    const x1 = points[i], y1 = points[i + 1];
    const x2 = points[i + 2], y2 = points[i + 3];
    const len = Math.hypot(x2 - x1, y2 - y1);
    segs.push({ x1, y1, x2, y2, len });
    totalLen += len;
  }
  let target = totalLen / 2;
  for (const s of segs) {
    if (target <= s.len) {
      const t = s.len === 0 ? 0 : target / s.len;
      return { x: s.x1 + (s.x2 - s.x1) * t, y: s.y1 + (s.y2 - s.y1) * t };
    }
    target -= s.len;
  }
  return { x: points[0], y: points[1] };
}

/* ============================================================
   Pull-to-create
   ============================================================ */

export interface Rect {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

/** Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ ghost-РїСЂСЏРјРѕСѓРіРѕР»СЊРЅРёРєР°: РєР°Рє Сѓ source, РЅРѕ РІ СЂР°Р·СѓРјРЅС‹С… РїСЂРµРґРµР»Р°С…. */
export function ghostSizeFor(source: CanvasElement): { w: number; h: number } {
  return {
    w: Math.max(GHOST_MIN_W, Math.min(GHOST_MAX_W, source.width)),
    h: Math.max(GHOST_MIN_H, Math.min(GHOST_MAX_H, source.height)),
  };
}

/**
 * РџРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ ghost-РїСЂСЏРјРѕСѓРіРѕР»СЊРЅРёРєР° pull-to-create.
 * РЎС‚Р°СЂС‚СѓРµС‚ РѕС‚ РєСѓСЂСЃРѕСЂР°, РЅРѕ РµСЃР»Рё РїРµСЂРµСЃРµРєР°РµС‚СЃСЏ СЃ bbox РёСЃС‚РѕС‡РЅРёРєР°
 * (РїР»СЋСЃ GHOST_MIN_GAP), РІС‹С‚Р°Р»РєРёРІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ РїРѕ РѕСЃРё anchor-РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёСЏ.
 */
export function computeGhostRect(
  source: CanvasElement,
  fromAnchor: AnchorSide,
  mouseX: number,
  mouseY: number,
): Rect {
  const { w, h } = ghostSizeFor(source);

  let left = mouseX - w / 2;
  let top = mouseY - h / 2;

  const srcLeft = source.x - GHOST_MIN_GAP;
  const srcRight = source.x + source.width + GHOST_MIN_GAP;
  const srcTop = source.y - GHOST_MIN_GAP;
  const srcBottom = source.y + source.height + GHOST_MIN_GAP;

  const overlapsX = left < srcRight && left + w > srcLeft;
  const overlapsY = top < srcBottom && top + h > srcTop;

  if (overlapsX && overlapsY) {
    const dir = getAnchorDirection(fromAnchor);
    if (dir.dx > 0) {
      left = srcRight;
    } else if (dir.dx < 0) {
      left = srcLeft - w;
    } else if (dir.dy > 0) {
      top = srcBottom;
    } else {
      top = srcTop - h;
    }
  }

  return {
    x: Math.round(left),
    y: Math.round(top),
    width: w,
    height: h,
  };
}

/**
 * Р‘Р»РёР¶Р°Р№С€Р°СЏ СЃС‚РѕСЂРѕРЅР° РЅРѕРІРѕРіРѕ РїСЂСЏРјРѕСѓРіРѕР»СЊРЅРёРєР°, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂР°СЏ В«СЃРјРѕС‚СЂРёС‚В» РЅР° РёСЃС‚РѕС‡РЅРёРє.
 * РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ РєР°Рє toAnchor РґР»СЏ РєРѕРЅРЅРµРєС‚РѕСЂР°.
 */
export function nearestAnchorForSource(
  rect: Rect,
  source: CanvasElement,
): AnchorSide {
  const cx = rect.x + rect.width / 2;
  const cy = rect.y + rect.height / 2;
  const srcCx = source.x + source.width / 2;
  const srcCy = source.y + source.height / 2;

  // Р’РµРєС‚РѕСЂ РѕС‚ С†РµРЅС‚СЂР° РЅРѕРІРѕРіРѕ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚Р° Рє С†РµРЅС‚СЂСѓ РёСЃС‚РѕС‡РЅРёРєР°.
  const dx = srcCx - cx;
  const dy = srcCy - cy;

  if (Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) {
    // РСЃС‚РѕС‡РЅРёРє СЃРїСЂР°РІР° в†’ Р±Р»РёР¶Р°Р№С€Р°СЏ СЃС‚РѕСЂРѕРЅР° РЅРѕРІРѕРіРѕ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚Р° вЂ” РїСЂР°РІР°СЏ.
    return dx > 0 ? 'right' : 'left';
  }
  // РСЃС‚РѕС‡РЅРёРє СЃРЅРёР·Сѓ в†’ Р±Р»РёР¶Р°Р№С€Р°СЏ СЃС‚РѕСЂРѕРЅР° вЂ” РЅРёР¶РЅСЏСЏ.
  return dy > 0 ? 'bottom' : 'top';
}

/* ============================================================
   РљР»РѕРЅРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ СЃС‚РёР»СЏ РёСЃС‚РѕС‡РЅРёРєР° РґР»СЏ РЅРѕРІРѕРіРѕ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚Р°
   ============================================================ */

export function cloneForConnector(
  rect: Rect,
  source: CanvasElement,
): CanvasElement {
  const newId = uid();
  const base = {
    id: newId,
    x: rect.x,
    y: rect.y,
    width: rect.width,
    height: rect.height,
    rotation: 0,
  };

  switch (source.type) {
    case 'shape':
      return { ...source, ...base, text: '' };

    case 'text':
      return { ...source, ...base, text: '', html: undefined };

    case 'image':
      return { ...source, ...base };

    case 'stamp':
      return { ...source, ...base };

    case 'table':
      return {
        ...source,
        ...base,
        data: source.data.map((row) => row.map(() => '')),
      };

    case 'footer':
      return { ...source, ...base, text: '' };

    case 'placeholder':
      return { ...source, ...base, sample: '' };

    default:
      // Fallback вЂ” РѕР±С‹С‡РЅС‹Р№ СЃРєСЂСѓРіР»С‘РЅРЅС‹Р№ РїСЂСЏРјРѕСѓРіРѕР»СЊРЅРёРє
      return {
        id: newId,
        type: 'shape',
        shape: 'roundRect',
        ...base,
        fill: '#eef2ff',
        stroke: '#6366f1',
        strokeWidth: 2,
        text: '',
        fontSize: 14,
        textFill: '#1e293b',
        radius: 8,
        textAlign: 'center',
        textVerticalAlign: 'middle',
        textPadding: { top: 8, right: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8 },
        textFontFamily: 'Arial',
        textLineHeight: 1.3,
        textFontStyle: 'normal',
        textDecoration: '',
      };
  }
}

export function download(content: string, name: string, mime: string): void {
  const blob = new Blob([content], { type: mime });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = name;
  a.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

export function newFooter(slide?: Slide, footerHeight = 60): FooterElement {
  const width = slide?.width ?? A4.width;
  const height = slide?.height ?? A4.height;
  const padding = slide?.padding ?? DEFAULT_PADDING;
  const printableWidth = Math.max(0, width - padding.left - padding.right);
  return {
    id: uid(), type: 'footer',

    width: printableWidth, height: footerHeight, rotation: 0,
    text: 'РћРћРћ В«РљРѕРјРїР°РЅРёСЏВ» В· www.example.com В· +7 (495) 000-00-00',

export function makePlaceholder(
  x: number, y: number, fieldPath: string, fieldType: DataFieldType, sample: string,

    id: uid(), type: 'placeholder',
    x: Math.max(0, x - 100), y: Math.max(0, y - 20),
    width: 220,
    height: fieldType === 'image' ? 200 : 60,
    rotation: 0, fieldPath, fieldType, sample, label: fieldPath,
    fontSize: 16, fontFamily: 'Arial', fill: '#1a1a1a',
    fontStyle: 'normal', textDecoration: '',
    align: 'left', verticalAlign: 'top', lineHeight: 1.2,
    padding: { top: 4, right: 4, bottom: 4, left: 4 },
    background: 'transparent', imageMode: 'fit',
  };
}

export function makeArrayTable(
  x: number, y: number,
  payload: {
    path: string;
    arrayName: string;
    sampleCount?: number;
    arrayChildren: { name: string; type: string }[];
  },
): TableElement {
  const children = payload.arrayChildren || [];
  const count = Math.max(1, payload.sampleCount ?? 3);
  const header: string[] = ['РђС‚СЂРёР±СѓС‚'];
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) header.push(`#${i + 1}`);
  const data: string[][] = [header];
  children.forEach((child) => {
    const row: string[] = [child.name];
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) row.push(`{{${payload.path}[${i}].${child.name}}}`);
    data.push(row);
  });
  const rows = data.length;
  const cols = 1 + count;
  return {
    id: uid(), type: 'table',
    x: Math.max(0, x - 100), y: Math.max(0, y - 20),
    width: 40 + cols * 120, height: 20 + rows * 32, rotation: 0,
    rows, cols, data,
    borderColor: '#cbd5e1', headerBg: '#eef2ff', fontSize: 13,
  };
}

import type { CanvasElement, Slide } from '../shared/types';
import { GRID_SIZE } from '../shared/constants';

export interface BBox { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; }

export function getBBox(el: CanvasElement): BBox {
  if ((el.type === 'line' || el.type === 'fill') && el.points && el.points.length >= 4) {
    const xs: number[] = [];
    const ys: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < el.points.length; i += 2) {
      xs.push(el.points[i]);
      ys.push(el.points[i + 1]);
    }
    const minX = Math.min(...xs);
    const minY = Math.min(...ys);
    const maxX = Math.max(...xs);
    const maxY = Math.max(...ys);
    return { x: el.x + minX, y: el.y + minY, w: maxX - minX, h: maxY - minY };
  }
  return { x: el.x, y: el.y, w: el.width, h: el.height };
}

export function hitShapeEl(el: CanvasElement, px: number, py: number): boolean {
  if (el.type === 'shape') {
    if (px < el.x || py < el.y || px > el.x + el.width || py > el.y + el.height) return false;
    if (el.shape === 'circle') {
      const cx = el.x + el.width / 2;
      const cy = el.y + el.height / 2;
      const r = Math.min(el.width, el.height) / 2;
      return Math.hypot(px - cx, py - cy) <= r;
    }
    return true;
  }
  if (el.type === 'fill' && el.points && el.points.length >= 6) {
    const pts = el.points;
    const n = pts.length / 2;
    let inside = false;
    for (let i = 0, j = n - 1; i < n; j = i++) {
      const xi = el.x + pts[i * 2];
      const yi = el.y + pts[i * 2 + 1];
      const xj = el.x + pts[j * 2];
      const yj = el.y + pts[j * 2 + 1];
      const intersect =
        yi > py !== yj > py && px < ((xj - xi) * (py - yi)) / (yj - yi + 0.000001) + xi;
      if (intersect) inside = !inside;
    }
    return inside;
  }
  return false;
}

export function findElementAt(slide: Slide, x: number, y: number): CanvasElement | null {
  for (let i = slide.elements.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const el = slide.elements[i];
    if (el.type === 'line') {
      const pts = el.points || [0, el.height / 2, el.width, el.height / 2];
      const xs: number[] = [];
      const ys: number[] = [];
      for (let j = 0; j < pts.length; j += 2) {
        xs.push(el.x + pts[j]);
        ys.push(el.y + pts[j + 1]);
      }
      const bx = Math.min(...xs);
      const by = Math.min(...ys);
      const bw = Math.max(...xs) - bx;
      const bh = Math.max(...ys) - by;
      if (x >= bx - 6 && x <= bx + bw + 6 && y >= by - 6 && y <= by + bh + 6) return el;
      continue;
    }
    if (x >= el.x && x <= el.x + el.width && y >= el.y && y <= el.y + el.height) return el;
  }
  return null;
}

export const gridRound = (v: number): number => Math.round(v / GRID_SIZE) * GRID_SIZE;

/* ============================================================
   Р Р°Р±РѕС‚Р° СЃ РёР·РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёСЏРјРё: СѓРґР°Р»РµРЅРёРµ РѕРґРЅРѕС‚РѕРЅРЅРѕРіРѕ С„РѕРЅР° (chroma-key),
   РІС‹С‡РёСЃР»РµРЅРёРµ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂРѕРІ РїРѕРґ РІСЃС‚Р°РІРєСѓ.
   ============================================================ */

export interface TransparentOptions {
  tolerance?: number;
  keyColor?: string | 'auto';
  maxSize?: number;
}

const hexToRgb = (hex: string): { r: number; g: number; b: number } => {
  const m = hex.replace('#', '').match(/.{2}/g);
  if (!m || m.length < 3) return { r: 255, g: 255, b: 255 };
  return {
    r: parseInt(m[0], 16),
    g: parseInt(m[1], 16),
    b: parseInt(m[2], 16),
  };
};

export function loadImage(src: string): Promise<HTMLImageElement> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const img = new Image();
    img.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
    img.onload = () => resolve(img);
    img.onerror = (e) => reject(e);
    img.src = src;
  });
}

export async function makeImageTransparent(
  src: string,
  options: TransparentOptions = {},
): Promise<string> {
  const { tolerance = 30, keyColor = 'auto', maxSize = 2048 } = options;

  const img = await loadImage(src);

  let w = img.naturalWidth || img.width;
  let h = img.naturalHeight || img.height;
  const k = Math.min(1, maxSize / Math.max(w, h));
  w = Math.max(1, Math.round(w * k));
  h = Math.max(1, Math.round(h * k));

  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = w;
  canvas.height = h;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });
  if (!ctx) return src;
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
  ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, w, h);

  let data: ImageData;
  try {
    data = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, w, h);
  } catch {
    return src;
  }
  const px = data.data;

  let kr: number, kg: number, kb: number;
  if (keyColor === 'auto') {
    const idxs = [
      0,
      (w - 1) * 4,
      (h - 1) * w * 4,
      ((h - 1) * w + (w - 1)) * 4,
    ];
    let r = 0, g = 0, b = 0;
    for (const i of idxs) {
      r += px[i];
      g += px[i + 1];
      b += px[i + 2];
    }
    kr = r / 4;
    kg = g / 4;
    kb = b / 4;
  } else {
    const rgb = hexToRgb(keyColor);
    kr = rgb.r;
    kg = rgb.g;
    kb = rgb.b;
  }

  const tol = tolerance;
  const tol2 = tol * tol * 3;
  const softEdge2 = tol2 * 4;

  for (let i = 0; i < px.length; i += 4) {
    const dr = px[i] - kr;
    const dg = px[i + 1] - kg;
    const db = px[i + 2] - kb;
    const dist2 = dr * dr + dg * dg + db * db;

    if (dist2 <= tol2) {
      px[i + 3] = 0;
    } else if (dist2 < softEdge2) {
      const d = Math.sqrt(dist2);
      const t = (d - tol) / tol;
      px[i + 3] = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(px[i + 3] * t)));
    }
  }

  ctx.putImageData(data, 0, 0);
  return canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
}

export function fitImageSize(
  naturalW: number,
  naturalH: number,
  slideW: number,
  slideH: number,
  maxRatio = 0.6,
): { width: number; height: number } {
  const maxW = Math.max(40, slideW * maxRatio);
  const maxH = Math.max(40, slideH * maxRatio);
  const k = Math.min(1, maxW / naturalW, maxH / naturalH);
  return {
    width: Math.max(20, Math.round(naturalW * k)),
    height: Math.max(20, Math.round(naturalH * k)),
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   lib/polyline.ts

   РЈС‚РёР»РёС‚С‹ РґР»СЏ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚С‹ СЃ Р»РёРЅРёСЏРјРё: СЃРµСЂРёР°Р»РёР·Р°С†РёСЏ, РїРѕРёСЃРє Р±Р»РёР¶Р°Р№С€РµРіРѕ
   СЃРµРіРјРµРЅС‚Р°, РІСЃС‚Р°РІРєР°/СѓРґР°Р»РµРЅРёРµ С‚РѕС‡РєРё, РјР°СЃС€С‚Р°Р±РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ, bbox.

   РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ Рё РЅР° РєР°РЅРІР°СЃРµ (LineEl), Рё РІ С€С‚Р°РјРїР°С… (LineView),
   Рё РІ РїР°РЅРµР»СЏС… СЃРІРѕР№СЃС‚РІ.
   ============================================================ */

export interface PolylinePoint {
  x: number;
  y: number;
}

/** Р Р°Р·Р±РѕСЂ РїР»РѕСЃРєРѕРіРѕ РјР°СЃСЃРёРІР° [x1, y1, x2, y2, ...] РІ РјР°СЃСЃРёРІ С‚РѕС‡РµРє. */
export function parsePoints(flat: number[] | undefined): PolylinePoint[] {
  if (!flat || flat.length < 4) return [];
  const out: PolylinePoint[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i + 1 < flat.length; i += 2) {
    out.push({ x: flat[i], y: flat[i + 1] });
  }
  return out;
}

/** РЎРµСЂРёР°Р»РёР·Р°С†РёСЏ РјР°СЃСЃРёРІР° С‚РѕС‡РµРє РІ РїР»РѕСЃРєРёР№ РјР°СЃСЃРёРІ. */
export function serializePoints(pts: PolylinePoint[]): number[] {
  const out: number[] = [];
  for (const p of pts) out.push(p.x, p.y);
  return out;
}

/**
 * РќР°Р№С‚Рё Р±Р»РёР¶Р°Р№С€РёР№ СЃРµРіРјРµРЅС‚ Рє С‚РѕС‡РєРµ (px, py).
 * Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ РёРЅРґРµРєСЃ СЃРµРіРјРµРЅС‚Р°, РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂ t (0..1) РЅР° РЅС‘Рј,
 * СЂР°СЃСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ РґРѕ СЃРµРіРјРµРЅС‚Р° Рё РєРѕРѕСЂРґРёРЅР°С‚С‹ РїСЂРѕРµРєС†РёРё.
 */
export function findNearestSegment(
  points: PolylinePoint[],
  px: number,
  py: number,
): { segmentIndex: number; t: number; distance: number; pointX: number; pointY: number } {
  let best = {
    segmentIndex: 0, t: 0, distance: Infinity, pointX: 0, pointY: 0,
  };
  for (let i = 0; i + 1 < points.length; i++) {
    const a = points[i];
    const b = points[i + 1];
    const dx = b.x - a.x;
    const dy = b.y - a.y;
    const len2 = dx * dx + dy * dy || 1;
    const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ((px - a.x) * dx + (py - a.y) * dy) / len2));
    const qx = a.x + t * dx;
    const qy = a.y + t * dy;
    const d = Math.hypot(px - qx, py - qy);
    if (d < best.distance) {
      best = { segmentIndex: i, t, distance: d, pointX: qx, pointY: qy };
    }
  }
  return best;
}

/**
 * Р’СЃС‚Р°РІРёС‚СЊ С‚РѕС‡РєСѓ РІ Р±Р»РёР¶Р°Р№С€РёР№ СЃРµРіРјРµРЅС‚.
 *  - snapRadius вЂ” РЅРµ РґСѓР±Р»РёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ С‚РѕС‡РєСѓ, РµСЃР»Рё РєР»РёРє РїРѕРїР°Р» РїРѕС‡С‚Рё РЅР° СЃСѓС‰РµСЃС‚РІСѓСЋС‰СѓСЋ.
 *  - Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ РЅРѕРІС‹Р№ РјР°СЃСЃРёРІ; РµСЃР»Рё РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёР№ РЅРµС‚, РІРµСЂРЅС‘С‚ РёСЃС…РѕРґРЅС‹Р№.
 */
export function insertPoint(
  points: PolylinePoint[],
  px: number,
  py: number,
  snapRadius = 5,
): PolylinePoint[] {
  for (const p of points) {
    if (Math.hypot(p.x - px, p.y - py) < snapRadius) return points;
  }

  const { segmentIndex, pointX, pointY } = findNearestSegment(points, px, py);
  const next = points.slice();
  next.splice(segmentIndex + 1, 0, { x: Math.round(pointX), y: Math.round(pointY) });
  return next;
}

/** РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ С‚РѕС‡РєСѓ РїРѕ РёРЅРґРµРєСЃСѓ. РњРёРЅРёРјСѓРј 2 С‚РѕС‡РєРё РґРѕР»Р¶РЅРѕ РѕСЃС‚Р°С‚СЊСЃСЏ. */
export function removePoint(points: PolylinePoint[], index: number): PolylinePoint[] {
  if (points.length <= 2) return points;
  const next = points.slice();
  next.splice(index, 1);
  return next;
}

/** РњР°СЃС€С‚Р°Р±РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ С‚РѕС‡РµРє РѕС‚РЅРѕСЃРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ С‚РѕС‡РєРё РїСЂРёРІСЏР·РєРё (РІ С‚РµС… Р¶Рµ РєРѕРѕСЂРґРёРЅР°С‚Р°С…). */
export function scalePoints(
  points: PolylinePoint[],
  sx: number,
  sy: number,
  ox = 0,
  oy = 0,
): PolylinePoint[] {
  return points.map((p) => ({
    x: (p.x - ox) * sx + ox,
    y: (p.y - oy) * sy + oy,
  }));
}

/** BBox РЅР°Р±РѕСЂР° С‚РѕС‡РµРє. */
export function getPointsBBox(points: PolylinePoint[]): {
  minX: number; minY: number; maxX: number; maxY: number;
  width: number; height: number;
} {
  if (!points.length) {
    return { minX: 0, minY: 0, maxX: 0, maxY: 0, width: 0, height: 0 };
  }
  let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
  for (const p of points) {
    if (p.x < minX) minX = p.x;
    if (p.y < minY) minY = p.y;
    if (p.x > maxX) maxX = p.x;
    if (p.y > maxY) maxY = p.y;
  }
  return { minX, minY, maxX, maxY, width: maxX - minX, height: maxY - minY };
}

/** Р“Р°СЂР°РЅС‚РёСЂСѓРµС‚ РјРёРЅРёРјСѓРј 2 С‚РѕС‡РєРё: РµСЃР»Рё РїСЂРёС€С‘Р» РїСѓСЃС‚РѕР№ РјР°СЃСЃРёРІ вЂ” РІРµСЂРЅС‘С‚ РіРѕСЂРёР·РѕРЅС‚Р°Р»СЊ. */
export function ensureMinimalPoints(
  flat: number[] | undefined,
  width: number,
  height: number,
): number[] {
  if (flat && flat.length >= 4) return flat;
  return [0, height / 2, width, height / 2];
}

import type { DataRoot } from '../shared/types';

export function resolveSample(data: DataRoot[], path: string): string | null {
  if (!path) return null;
  const clean = path.replace(/\[\d+\]/g, '');
  const parts = clean.split('.');
  const rootName = parts.shift();
  if (!rootName) return null;
  const root = data.find((d) => d.name === rootName);
  if (!root) return null;

  let fields = root.fields;
  let current: any = null;
  for (const p of parts) {
    current = fields.find((f) => f.name === p);
    if (!current) return null;
    fields = current.children || [];
  }
  return current ? current.sample : null;
}

/* ============================================================
   Р Р°СЃРєР»Р°РґРєР° runs РїРѕ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєР°Рј.

   Р”РІР° РїСЂР°РІРёР»Р°:
   1) lineHeight вЂ” per-line (max fontSize РІ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРµ * coef).
   2) Р’РµРґСѓС‰РёРµ РїСѓСЃС‚С‹Рµ Р°Р±Р·Р°С†С‹ (РїРµСЂРµРґ РїРµСЂРІС‹Рј С‚РµРєСЃС‚РѕРј) РїСЂРµРІСЂР°С‰Р°СЋС‚СЃСЏ
      РІ leadingGapY Рё СЃРґРІРёРіР°СЋС‚ Р±Р»РѕРє РІРЅРёР·. Р‘РµР· СЌС‚РѕРіРѕ Enter РІ
      РЅР°С‡Р°Р»Рµ Р±Р»РѕРєР° РІРёР·СѓР°Р»СЊРЅРѕ С‚РµСЂСЏР»СЃСЏ.

import type { TextRun, PositionedRun, LayoutResult } from './types';
import { measureText } from './measure';

type Token = TextRun & { width: number; fsize: number; ffamily: string; style: string };

const PARAGRAPH_GAP_PX = 8;

export function layoutRuns(
  runs: TextRun[],
  maxWidth: number,
  lineHeightCoef: number,
): LayoutResult {
  if (!runs.length || maxWidth <= 0) {
    return { lines: [], totalHeight: 0, lineHeight: 0 };
  }

  let baseFontSize = 16;
  let baseFontFamily = 'Arial';
  for (const run of runs) {
    if (run.fontSize) baseFontSize = run.fontSize;
    if (run.fontFamily) baseFontFamily = run.fontFamily;
  }

  const tokens = tokenize(runs, baseFontSize, baseFontFamily);
  const paragraphGap = PARAGRAPH_GAP_PX;

  const lines: PositionedRun[][] = [];
  let currentLine: PositionedRun[] = [];
  let currentLineMaxFontSize = 0;
  let cursorX = 0;
  let cursorY = 0;
  let pendingNewlines = 0;

  /* Р’РµРґСѓС‰РёРµ \n РїРµСЂРµРґ РїРµСЂРІС‹Рј С‚РµРєСЃС‚РѕРј. РџСЂРёРјРµРЅСЏРµС‚СЃСЏ РћР”РРќ СЂР°Р·
     РїСЂРё РїРµСЂРІРѕРј pushToken: СЃРґРІРёРіР°РµС‚ РІРµСЃСЊ Р±Р»РѕРє РІРЅРёР·. */
  let leadingGapY = 0;

  const flushLine = () => {
    if (!currentLine.length) return;
    lines.push(currentLine);

    const lh = Math.max(
      currentLineMaxFontSize,
      Math.round(currentLineMaxFontSize * lineHeightCoef),
    );
    cursorY += lh;
    cursorX = 0;
    currentLine = [];
    currentLineMaxFontSize = 0;
  };

  /**
   * pendingNewlines = 2 в†’ РѕРґРёРЅ Р°Р±Р·Р°С† (gap).
   * pendingNewlines = 4 в†’ Р°Р±Р·Р°С† + РїСѓСЃС‚Р°СЏ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєР°.
   * pendingNewlines = 6 в†’ Р°Р±Р·Р°С† + 2 РїСѓСЃС‚С‹Рµ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРё.
   *
   * Р•СЃР»Рё РµС‰С‘ РЅРёС‡РµРіРѕ РЅРµ РЅР°СЂРёСЃРѕРІР°РЅРѕ вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ Р’Р•Р”РЈР©РР• РїСѓСЃС‚С‹Рµ Р°Р±Р·Р°С†С‹,
   * СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏРµРј РёС… РєР°Рє leadingGapY.
   */
  const applyPendingNewlines = (isEnd: boolean) => {
    if (pendingNewlines === 0) return;

    const approxLh = Math.round(baseFontSize * lineHeightCoef);

    if (lines.length === 0 && currentLine.length === 0) {
      /* Р•С‰С‘ РЅРёС‡РµРіРѕ РЅРµ СЂРёСЃРѕРІР°Р»Рё. */
      if (!isEnd) {
        const pairs = Math.floor(pendingNewlines / 2);
        if (pairs > 0) {
          leadingGapY += paragraphGap;
          for (let p = 1; p < pairs; p++) {
            leadingGapY += approxLh + paragraphGap;
          }
        } else {
          /* РћРґРёРЅРѕС‡РЅС‹Р№ \n РІ СЃР°РјРѕРј РЅР°С‡Р°Р»Рµ вЂ” РѕРґРЅР° РїСѓСЃС‚Р°СЏ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєР°. */
          leadingGapY += approxLh;
        }
      }
      pendingNewlines = 0;
      return;
    }

    flushLine();

    if (!isEnd) {
      const pairs = Math.floor(pendingNewlines / 2);
      if (pairs > 0) {
        cursorY += paragraphGap;
        for (let p = 1; p < pairs; p++) {
          cursorY += approxLh + paragraphGap;
        }
      }
    }
    pendingNewlines = 0;
  };

  const pushToken = (tk: Token, text: string, width: number) => {
    /* РџРµСЂРІС‹Р№ С‚РѕРєРµРЅ вЂ” РїСЂРёРјРµРЅСЏРµРј РІРµРґСѓС‰РёР№ РѕС‚СЃС‚СѓРї. */
    if (leadingGapY > 0 && currentLine.length === 0 && lines.length === 0) {
      cursorY += leadingGapY;
      leadingGapY = 0;
    }
    if (tk.fsize > currentLineMaxFontSize) currentLineMaxFontSize = tk.fsize;
    currentLine.push({
      text,
      bold: tk.bold,
      italic: tk.italic,
      underline: tk.underline,
      strike: tk.strike,
      color: tk.color,
      bgColor: tk.bgColor,
      href: tk.href,
      fontSize: tk.fsize,
      fontFamily: tk.ffamily,
      x: cursorX,
      y: cursorY,
      width,
      actualFontSize: tk.fsize,
    });
    cursorX += width;
  };

  for (const tk of tokens) {
    if (tk.text === '\n') {
      pendingNewlines++;
      continue;
    }

    const isSpace = /^\s+$/.test(tk.text);
    if (isSpace && cursorX === 0 && pendingNewlines === 0) continue;

    applyPendingNewlines(false);

    if (cursorX + tk.width <= maxWidth) {
      pushToken(tk, tk.text, tk.width);
      continue;
    }

    if (cursorX > 0) flushLine();

    if (isSpace) continue;

    if (tk.width > maxWidth) {
      const chars = Array.from(tk.text);
      let chunk = '';
      let chunkWidth = 0;

      for (const ch of chars) {
        const cw = measureText(ch, tk.fsize, tk.ffamily, tk.style);
        if (chunkWidth + cw > maxWidth && chunk.length > 0) {
          pushToken(tk, chunk, chunkWidth);
          flushLine();
          chunk = ch;
          chunkWidth = cw;
        } else {
          chunk += ch;
          chunkWidth += cw;
        }
      }

      if (chunk.length) pushToken(tk, chunk, chunkWidth);
      continue;
    }

    pushToken(tk, tk.text, tk.width);
  }

  applyPendingNewlines(true);
  flushLine();

  let totalHeight = 0;
  for (const line of lines) {
    for (const r of line) {
      const bottom = r.y + Math.max(r.actualFontSize, r.actualFontSize * lineHeightCoef);
      if (bottom > totalHeight) totalHeight = bottom;
    }
  }

  return {
    lines,
    totalHeight,
    lineHeight: 0,
  };
}

/* ============================================================ */

function tokenize(
  runs: TextRun[],
  baseFontSize: number,
  baseFontFamily: string,
): Token[] {
  const tokens: Token[] = [];

  for (const run of runs) {
    const fs = run.fontSize ?? baseFontSize;
    const ff = run.fontFamily ?? baseFontFamily;
    const style =
      run.bold && run.italic ? 'italic bold'
      : run.bold ? 'bold'
      : run.italic ? 'italic'
      : 'normal';

    for (const part of run.text.split(/(\n)/)) {
      if (part === '') continue;
      if (part === '\n') {
        tokens.push({ ...run, text: '\n', width: 0, fsize: fs, ffamily: ff, style });
        continue;
      }
      for (const w of part.split(/(\s+)/)) {
        if (w === '') continue;
        tokens.push({
          ...run,
          text: w,
          width: measureText(w, fs, ff, style),
          fsize: fs,
          ffamily: ff,
          style,
        });
      }
    }
  }

  return tokens;
}

/* ============================================================
   РР·РјРµСЂРµРЅРёРµ С€РёСЂРёРЅС‹ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р° С‡РµСЂРµР· offscreen canvas.
   ============================================================ */

let measureCtx: CanvasRenderingContext2D | null | undefined;

function getCtx(): CanvasRenderingContext2D | null {
  if (measureCtx === undefined) {
    measureCtx =
      typeof document !== 'undefined'
        ? document.createElement('canvas').getContext('2d')
        : null;
  }
  return measureCtx;
}

export function measureText(
  text: string,
  fontSize: number,
  fontFamily: string,
  fontStyle: string,
): number {
  const ctx = getCtx();
  if (!ctx) return text.length * fontSize * 0.55;
  const style = fontStyle || 'normal';
  ctx.font = `${style} ${fontSize}px ${fontFamily}, Arial, sans-serif`;
  return ctx.measureText(text).width;
}

import type { BaseStyle, RichBlock } from '../types';
import { parseHtmlToRuns } from './runs';
import { parseTableEl } from './table';

/* ============================================================
   HTML в†’ RichBlock[]  (С‚РµРєСЃС‚ + С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†С‹ РІРµСЂС…РЅРµРіРѕ СѓСЂРѕРІРЅСЏ)
   ============================================================ */

export function parseHtmlToBlocks(html: string, base: BaseStyle): RichBlock[] {
  if (!html) return [];
  if (typeof DOMParser === 'undefined') {
    const runs = parseHtmlToRuns(html, base);
    return runs.length ? [{ kind: 'text', runs }] : [];
  }

  const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
  return parseNodesToBlocks(doc.body.childNodes, base);
}

/**
 * Р Р°Р·Р±РѕСЂ СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјРѕРіРѕ СЏС‡РµР№РєРё С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†С‹.
 *
 * Р’РђР–РќРћ: РїСѓСЃС‚С‹Рµ <p></p> РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё СЏС‡РµР№РєРё вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ РѕСЃРѕР·РЅР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ РІРёР·СѓР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№
 * СЂР°Р·РґРµР»РёС‚РµР»СЊ (РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЊ РЅР°Р¶Р°Р» Enter РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°). РС… РЅРµР»СЊР·СЏ
 * СѓРґР°Р»СЏС‚СЊ. РЈРґР°Р»СЏРµРј С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РїСѓСЃС‚С‹Рµ <p> РЅР° РЎРђРњР«РҐ РљР РђРЇРҐ СЏС‡РµР№РєРё вЂ”
 * РѕРЅРё РґР°СЋС‚ Р»РёС€РЅРёР№ РѕС‚СЃС‚СѓРї СЃРІРµСЂС…Сѓ/СЃРЅРёР·Сѓ.
 */
export function parseCellBlocks(cellEl: HTMLElement, base: BaseStyle): RichBlock[] {
  const clone = cellEl.cloneNode(true) as HTMLElement;

  /* РЎРЅРёРјР°РµРј РїСѓСЃС‚С‹Рµ <p> С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РїРѕ РєСЂР°СЏРј вЂ” СЃРІРµСЂС…Сѓ Рё СЃРЅРёР·Сѓ. */
  const directPs = Array.from(clone.querySelectorAll(':scope > p')) as HTMLElement[];

  while (directPs.length) {
    const p = directPs[0];
    if (p.textContent?.trim() || p.querySelector('br, table')) break;
    p.remove();
    directPs.shift();
  }
  while (directPs.length) {
    const p = directPs[directPs.length - 1];
    if (p.textContent?.trim() || p.querySelector('br, table')) break;
    p.remove();
    directPs.pop();
  }

  /* Р’РµРґСѓС‰РёРµ/Р·Р°РјС‹РєР°СЋС‰РёРµ <br> РІ СЏС‡РµР№РєРµ вЂ” СѓР±РёСЂР°РµРј. */
  while (clone.firstChild && (clone.firstChild as Element).nodeName === 'BR') {
    clone.removeChild(clone.firstChild);
  }
  while (clone.lastChild && (clone.lastChild as Element).nodeName === 'BR') {
    clone.removeChild(clone.lastChild);
  }

  return parseNodesToBlocks(clone.childNodes, base);
}

/* ============================================================
   Р’РЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРµРµ
   ============================================================ */

const CONTAINER_TAGS = new Set([
  'div', 'section', 'article', 'main', 'aside', 'figure',
  'blockquote', 'header', 'footer', 'nav', 'span',
]);

function parseNodesToBlocks(
  nodes: NodeListOf<ChildNode> | ChildNode[],
  base: BaseStyle,
): RichBlock[] {
  const blocks: RichBlock[] = [];
  let pendingHtml = '';

  const flushText = () => {
    if (!pendingHtml.trim()) { pendingHtml = ''; return; }
    const runs = parseHtmlToRuns(pendingHtml, base);
    if (runs.length) blocks.push({ kind: 'text', runs });
    pendingHtml = '';
  };

  const visit = (node: Node) => {
    if (node.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE) {
      pendingHtml += (node.textContent ?? '')
        .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
        .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
        .replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
      return;
    }
    if (node.nodeType !== Node.ELEMENT_NODE) {
      return;
    }

    const el = node as HTMLElement;
    const tag = el.tagName.toLowerCase();

    if (tag === 'table') {
      flushText();
      try {
        const data = parseTableEl(el, base, parseCellBlocks);
        if (data && data.cells.length) {
          blocks.push({ kind: 'table', data });
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[richText] parseTableEl failed:', err);
        const runs = parseHtmlToRuns(el.textContent ?? '', base);
        if (runs.length) blocks.push({ kind: 'text', runs });
      }
      return;
    }

    if (CONTAINER_TAGS.has(tag) && el.querySelector('table')) {
      for (const child of Array.from(el.childNodes)) {
        visit(child);
      }
      return;
    }

    pendingHtml += el.outerHTML;
  };

  for (const node of Array.from(nodes)) {
    visit(node);
  }
  flushText();

  return blocks;
}

export { parseHtmlToBlocks, parseCellBlocks } from './blocks';
export { parseTableEl } from './table';

FILE: lib\richText\parser\runs.ts

import type { TextRun, BaseStyle } from '../types';

/* ============================================================
   HTML в†’ TextRun[]

   Р’РђР–РќРћ:
   - Р’РµРґСѓС‰РёРµ Рё РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёРµ РїСѓСЃС‚С‹Рµ Р°Р±Р·Р°С†С‹ РЎРћРҐР РђРќРЇР®РўРЎРЇ РєР°Рє '\n\n'.
     РС… РїРѕС‚РѕРј РѕР±СЂР°Р±Р°С‚С‹РІР°РµС‚ layoutRuns РєР°Рє leading gap / gap РјРµР¶РґСѓ
     Р°Р±Р·Р°С†Р°РјРё. Р‘РµР· СЌС‚РѕРіРѕ Enter РІ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂРµ РІРёР·СѓР°Р»СЊРЅРѕ В«С‚РµСЂСЏР»СЃСЏВ».
   - РћР±СЂРµР·Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ РјР°РєСЃРёРјСѓРј РћР”РРќ Р·Р°РІРµСЂС€Р°СЋС‰РёР№ '\n\n' вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ
     С‚РµС…РЅРёС‡РµСЃРєР°СЏ РїСѓСЃС‚Р°СЏ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєР° РїРѕСЃР»Рµ РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРµРіРѕ Р°Р±Р·Р°С†Р°.
     Р’СЃС‘, С‡С‚Рѕ Р±РѕР»СЊС€Рµ, вЂ” РѕСЃРѕР·РЅР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РїСѓСЃС‚С‹Рµ Р°Р±Р·Р°С†С‹.
   ============================================================ */

export function parseHtmlToRuns(html: string, base: BaseStyle): TextRun[] {
  if (!html) return [];
  if (typeof DOMParser === 'undefined') {
    return [{ ...baseToRun(base), text: html.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '') }];
  }

  const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
  const runs: TextRun[] = [];

  const pushLine = (style: Partial<TextRun>) => {
    if (runs.length && runs[runs.length - 1].text.endsWith('\n')) return;
    runs.push({ ...baseToRun(base), ...style, text: '\n' });
  };

  /**
   * РџР°СЂР°РіСЂР°С„РЅС‹Р№ СЂР°Р·СЂС‹РІ. force=true вЂ” РґР»СЏ РїСѓСЃС‚РѕРіРѕ Р±Р»РѕРєР°
   * (<p></p> / <p><br></p>): РґРѕР±Р°РІР»СЏРµРј '\n\n' РґР°Р¶Рµ РµСЃР»Рё
   * РїСЂРµРґС‹РґСѓС‰РёР№ СѓР¶Рµ РЅР° '\n\n'. Р­С‚Рѕ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏРµС‚ В«РґС‹СЂРєРёВ» РјРµР¶РґСѓ
   * Р°Р±Р·Р°С†Р°РјРё, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Рµ РёРЅР°С‡Рµ СЃС…Р»РѕРїС‹РІР°СЋС‚СЃСЏ.
   */
  const pushParagraphBreak = (style: Partial<TextRun>, force = false) => {
    const last = runs[runs.length - 1];
    if (!force) {
      if (last && last.text.endsWith('\n\n')) return;
      if (last && last.text.endsWith('\n')) {
        runs.push({ ...baseToRun(base), ...style, text: '\n' });
        return;
      }
    }
    runs.push({ ...baseToRun(base), ...style, text: '\n\n' });
  };

  const makeListMarker = (depth: number, ordered: boolean, index: number): string => {
    const indent = '      '.repeat(Math.max(0, depth));
    const bullet = ordered ? `${index + 1}. ` : 'вЂў ';
    return indent + bullet;
  };

  function walk(node: Node, style: Partial<TextRun>, listDepth = 0) {
    if (node.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE) {
      const text = node.textContent || '';
      if (text) runs.push({ ...baseToRun(base), ...style, text });
      return;
    }
    if (node.nodeType !== Node.ELEMENT_NODE) return;

    const el = node as HTMLElement;
    const tag = el.tagName.toLowerCase();

    if (tag === 'br') {
      pushLine(style);
      return;
    }
    if (tag === 'table') return;

    /* ---------- РЎРїРёСЃРєРё: ul / ol ---------- */
    if (tag === 'ul' || tag === 'ol') {
      const ordered = tag === 'ol';
      const liList = Array.from(el.children).filter(
        (c) => (c as HTMLElement).tagName.toLowerCase() === 'li',
      );
      liList.forEach((li, idx) => {
        const marker = makeListMarker(listDepth, ordered, idx);
        runs.push({ ...baseToRun(base), ...style, text: marker });

        li.childNodes.forEach((child) => {
          if (child.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE) {
            const ct = (child as HTMLElement).tagName.toLowerCase();
            if (ct === 'p') {
              child.childNodes.forEach((c) => walk(c, style, listDepth));
              return;
            }
            if (ct === 'ul' || ct === 'ol') {
              pushLine(style);
              walk(child, style, listDepth + 1);
              return;
            }
          }
          walk(child, style, listDepth);
        });

        pushLine(style);
      });
      return;
    }

    /* ---------- Р—Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРєРё ---------- */
    const next: Partial<TextRun> = { ...style };
    const baseFontSize = base.fontSize || 16;
    if (tag === 'h1') { next.fontSize = Math.round(baseFontSize * 1.75); next.bold = true; }
    else if (tag === 'h2') { next.fontSize = Math.round(baseFontSize * 1.4); next.bold = true; }
    else if (tag === 'h3') { next.fontSize = Math.round(baseFontSize * 1.15); next.bold = true; }

    /* ---------- РРЅР»Р°Р№РЅ-СЃС‚РёР»Рё С‚РµРіР°РјРё ---------- */
    if (tag === 'b' || tag === 'strong') next.bold = true;
    if (tag === 'i' || tag === 'em') next.italic = true;
    if (tag === 'u') next.underline = true;
    if (tag === 's' || tag === 'strike' || tag === 'del') next.strike = true;

    if (tag === 'a') {
      const href = el.getAttribute('href') || '';
      if (href) next.href = href;
      next.underline = true;
      if (!next.color) next.color = '#2563eb';
    }
    if (tag === 'mark') {
      const bg = el.style.backgroundColor || el.getAttribute('data-color');
      if (bg) next.bgColor = bg;
    }

    /* ---------- РРЅР»Р°Р№РЅ-СЃС‚РёР»Рё С‡РµСЂРµР· style="..." ---------- */
    const s = el.style;
    if (s) {
      if (s.color) next.color = s.color;
      if (s.backgroundColor) next.bgColor = s.backgroundColor;
      if (s.fontSize) {
        const n = parseFloat(s.fontSize);
        if (!Number.isNaN(n) && n > 0) next.fontSize = n;
      }
      if (s.fontFamily) {
        next.fontFamily = s.fontFamily.replace(/["']/g, '').split(',')[0].trim();
      }
      const fw = s.fontWeight;
      if (fw === 'bold' || (fw && parseInt(fw, 10) >= 600)) next.bold = true;
      if (s.fontStyle === 'italic') next.italic = true;
      const td = s.textDecoration || '';
      if (td.includes('underline')) next.underline = true;
      if (td.includes('line-through')) next.strike = true;
    }

    const isBlock = tag === 'p' || tag === 'div' || tag === 'h1' || tag === 'h2' || tag === 'h3';

    if (isBlock) {
      /* <p></p> Рё <p><br></p> СЃС‡РёС‚Р°СЋС‚СЃСЏ РїСѓСЃС‚С‹РјРё вЂ” РґР»СЏ РЅРёС…
         РїР°СЂР°РіСЂР°С„РЅС‹Р№ СЂР°Р·СЂС‹РІ РґРѕР±Р°РІР»СЏРµРј РїСЂРёРЅСѓРґРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹
         СЃС…Р»РѕРїС‹РІР°РЅРёРµ РЅРµ СЃСЉРµР»Рѕ РїСѓСЃС‚СѓСЋ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєСѓ. */
      const wasEmpty = !(el.textContent || '').trim();

      el.childNodes.forEach((child) => walk(child, next, listDepth));
      pushParagraphBreak(style, wasEmpty);
      return;
    }

    el.childNodes.forEach((child) => walk(child, next, listDepth));
  }

  walk(doc.body, {});

  /* Р’РµРґСѓС‰РёРµ \n РќР• РѕР±СЂРµР·Р°РµРј вЂ” РѕРЅРё РґР°СЋС‚ РѕС‚СЃС‚СѓРї СЃРІРµСЂС…Сѓ Р±Р»РѕРєР°,
     РµСЃР»Рё РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЊ РѕСЃС‚Р°РІРёР» РїСѓСЃС‚С‹Рµ Р°Р±Р·Р°С†С‹ РїРµСЂРµРґ С‚РµРєСЃС‚РѕРј. */

  /* Trailing вЂ” РјР°РєСЃРёРјСѓРј РѕРґРёРЅ \n\n (С‚РµС…РЅРёС‡РµСЃРєРёР№). */
  if (runs.length) {
    const last = runs[runs.length - 1];
    if (last.text === '\n\n' || last.text === '\n') runs.pop();
  }

  return runs;
}

/* ============================================================ */

export function baseToRun(base: BaseStyle): Omit<TextRun, 'text'> {
  return {
    bold: base.fontStyle.includes('bold'),
    italic: base.fontStyle.includes('italic'),
    underline: base.textDecoration.includes('underline'),
    strike: base.textDecoration.includes('line-through'),
    color: base.fill,
    fontSize: base.fontSize,
    fontFamily: base.fontFamily,
  };
}

import type {
  BaseStyle,
  CellAlign,
  CellVerticalAlign,
  RichTableCell,
  RichTableCellPadding,
  RichTableData,
  RichBlock,
} from '../types';

export type ParseCellBlocksFn = (cellEl: HTMLElement, base: BaseStyle) => RichBlock[];

export function parseTableEl(
  table: HTMLElement,
  base: BaseStyle,
  parseCellBlocks: ParseCellBlocksFn,
): RichTableData | null {
  if (!table) return null;

  const trList = Array.from(
    table.querySelectorAll(
      ':scope > thead > tr, :scope > tbody > tr, :scope > tfoot > tr, :scope > tr',
    ),
  );

  const cells: RichTableCell[] = [];
  const grid: (RichTableCell | null)[][] = [];

  const ensureRow = (r: number) => { while (grid.length <= r) grid.push([]); };
  const isOccupied = (r: number, c: number): boolean => {
    const row = grid[r];
    if (!row) return false;
    return row[c] !== undefined && row[c] !== null;
  };

  trList.forEach((tr, r) => {
    ensureRow(r);
    let c = 0;
    while (isOccupied(r, c)) c++;

    const cellEls = Array.from(tr.childNodes).filter(
      (ch): ch is HTMLElement =>
        ch.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE &&
        ((ch as HTMLElement).tagName.toLowerCase() === 'td' ||
          (ch as HTMLElement).tagName.toLowerCase() === 'th'),
    );

    for (const cellEl of cellEls) {
      while (isOccupied(r, c)) c++;

      const colspan = Math.max(1, parseInt(cellEl.getAttribute('colspan') || '1', 10) || 1);
      const rowspan = Math.max(1, parseInt(cellEl.getAttribute('rowspan') || '1', 10) || 1);
      const isHeader = cellEl.tagName.toLowerCase() === 'th';

      const cell: RichTableCell = {
        blocks: parseCellBlocks(cellEl, base),
        isHeader,
        colspan,
        rowspan,
        rowIndex: r,
        colIndex: c,
        rawWidths: parseRawWidths(cellEl),
        bgColor: cellEl.style.backgroundColor || undefined,
        borderColor: cellEl.style.borderColor || undefined,
        borderWidth: parseFloatOrUndef(cellEl.style.borderWidth),
        borderStyle: cellEl.style.borderStyle || undefined,
        padding: parsePadding(cellEl),
        align: readCellAlign(cellEl, isHeader),
        verticalAlign: readCellVerticalAlign(cellEl),
      };
      cells.push(cell);

      for (let rr = 0; rr < rowspan; rr++) {
        ensureRow(r + rr);
        for (let cc = 0; cc < colspan; cc++) {
          grid[r + rr][c + cc] = cell;
        }
      }
      c += colspan;
    }
  });

  let colCount = 0;
  for (const row of grid) {
    if (!row) continue;
    for (let i = row.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      if (row[i] !== undefined && row[i] !== null) {
        if (i + 1 > colCount) colCount = i + 1;
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  if (colCount === 0) colCount = 1;

  const colWidths = resolveColWidths(table, cells, colCount);

  return { cells, rowCount: grid.length, colCount, colWidths };
}

/* ... (РѕСЃС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ С„СѓРЅРєС†РёРё resolveColWidths, parseRawWidths, parsePadding, readCellAlign, readCellVerticalAlign РѕСЃС‚Р°РІР»СЏРµРј Р±РµР· РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёР№, РѕРЅРё РІ dump1 Р±С‹Р»Рё РїСЂР°РІРёР»СЊРЅС‹РјРё) ... */

const ALIGN_VALUES: CellAlign[] = ['left', 'center', 'right', 'justify'];

function normalizeAlign(v: string | undefined): CellAlign | null {
  const s = (v || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (s === 'start') return 'left';
  if (s === 'end') return 'right';
  if (ALIGN_VALUES.includes(s as CellAlign)) return s as CellAlign;
  return null;
}

function readCellAlign(cellEl: HTMLElement, _isHeader: boolean): CellAlign {
  const onCell = normalizeAlign(cellEl.style.textAlign);
  if (onCell) return onCell;

  const firstBlock = cellEl.querySelector(':scope > p, :scope > h1, :scope > h2, :scope > h3, :scope > div, :scope > blockquote, :scope > li') as HTMLElement | null;
  if (firstBlock) {
    const fromBlock = normalizeAlign(firstBlock.style.textAlign);
    if (fromBlock) return fromBlock;
  }

  const anyAligned = cellEl.querySelector('[style*="text-align"]') as HTMLElement | null;
  if (anyAligned) {
    const fromAny = normalizeAlign(anyAligned.style.textAlign);
    if (fromAny) return fromAny;
  }

  return 'left';
}

function readCellVerticalAlign(cellEl: HTMLElement): CellVerticalAlign {
  const onCell = (cellEl.style.verticalAlign || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (onCell === 'middle' || onCell === 'center') return 'middle';
  if (onCell === 'bottom') return 'bottom';
  if (onCell === 'top' || onCell === 'baseline') return 'top';

  const firstBlock = cellEl.firstElementChild as HTMLElement | null;
  if (firstBlock) {
    const v = (firstBlock.style.verticalAlign || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    if (v === 'middle' || v === 'center') return 'middle';
    if (v === 'bottom') return 'bottom';
  }
  return 'top';
}

function resolveColWidths(table: HTMLElement, cells: RichTableCell[], colCount: number): number[] | null {
  const fromCells = readCellWidths(cells, colCount);
  if (fromCells) return fromCells;

  const fromColgroup = readColgroup(table, colCount);
  if (fromColgroup && fromColgroup.hasRealWidth) return fromColgroup.widths;
  if (fromColgroup) return fromColgroup.widths;

  return null;
}

function findColgroup(table: HTMLElement): Element | null {
  for (const child of Array.from(table.children)) {
    if (child.tagName.toLowerCase() === 'colgroup') return child;
  }
  return null;
}

function readColgroup(table: HTMLElement, colCount: number): { widths: number[]; hasRealWidth: boolean } | null {
  const colgroup = findColgroup(table);
  if (!colgroup) return null;

  const colEls = Array.from(colgroup.children).filter(c => c.tagName.toLowerCase() === 'col') as HTMLElement[];
  if (colEls.length !== colCount) return null;

  const widths: number[] = new Array(colCount).fill(0);
  let hasRealWidth = false;

  colEls.forEach((colEl, i) => {
    const w = parseFloat(colEl.style.width);
    const aw = parseFloat(colEl.getAttribute('width') || '');
    if (!Number.isNaN(w) && w > 0) { widths[i] = w; hasRealWidth = true; }
    else if (!Number.isNaN(aw) && aw > 0) { widths[i] = aw; hasRealWidth = true; }
  });

  return { widths, hasRealWidth };
}

function readCellWidths(cells: RichTableCell[], colCount: number): number[] | null {
  const widths: number[] = new Array(colCount).fill(0);
  let any = false;

  for (const cell of cells) {
    if (!cell.rawWidths || !cell.rawWidths.length) continue;
    if (cell.rawWidths.length === cell.colspan) {
      for (let i = 0; i < cell.colspan; i++) {
        const col = cell.colIndex + i;
        if (col < colCount && widths[col] === 0) { widths[col] = cell.rawWidths[i]; any = true; }
      }
    } else if (cell.rawWidths.length === 1) {
      if (widths[cell.colIndex] === 0) { widths[cell.colIndex] = cell.rawWidths[0]; any = true; }
    }
  }
  return any ? widths : null;
}

function parseFloatOrUndef(v: string | null | undefined): number | undefined {
  if (!v) return undefined;
  const n = parseFloat(v);
  return Number.isNaN(n) ? undefined : n;
}

export function parsePadding(el: HTMLElement): RichTableCellPadding | undefined {
  const s = el.style;
  if (!s) return undefined;
  const top = parseFloat(s.paddingTop), right = parseFloat(s.paddingRight), bottom = parseFloat(s.paddingBottom), left = parseFloat(s.paddingLeft);
  const anySide = [top, right, bottom, left].some((n) => !Number.isNaN(n));
  let p: RichTableCellPadding | null = null;
  if (anySide) {
    p = { top: Number.isNaN(top) ? 0 : top, right: Number.isNaN(right) ? 0 : right, bottom: Number.isNaN(bottom) ? 0 : bottom, left: Number.isNaN(left) ? 0 : left };
  }
  const shorthand = s.padding;
  if (shorthand) {
    const parts = shorthand.trim().split(/\s+/).map((x) => parseFloat(x) || 0);
    if (parts.length === 1) p = { top: parts[0], right: parts[0], bottom: parts[0], left: parts[0] };
    else if (parts.length === 2) p = { top: parts[0], right: parts[1], bottom: parts[0], left: parts[1] };
    else if (parts.length === 3) p = { top: parts[0], right: parts[1], bottom: parts[2], left: parts[1] };
    else if (parts.length >= 4) p = { top: parts[0], right: parts[1], bottom: parts[2], left: parts[3] };
  }
  return p ?? undefined;
}

function parseRawWidths(cellEl: HTMLElement): number[] | null {
  const fromAttr = cellEl.getAttribute('colwidth');
  if (fromAttr && fromAttr !== 'null') {
    const arr = fromAttr.split(',').map((x) => parseFloat(x.trim())).filter((n) => !Number.isNaN(n) && n > 0);
    if (arr.length) return arr;
  }
  const sw = cellEl.style?.width;
  if (sw) {
    const n = parseFloat(sw);
    if (!Number.isNaN(n) && n > 0) return [n];
  }
  return null;
}

/* ============================================================
   Fallback: plain text в†’ HTML РґР»СЏ РµРґРёРЅРѕРѕР±СЂР°Р·РЅРѕРіРѕ РїР°СЂСЃРёРЅРіР°.
   ============================================================ */

export function plainToHtml(text: string): string {
  const esc = (text || '')
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
  return `<p>${esc.replace(/\n/g, '<br>')}</p>`;
}

  borderWidth?: number;

  /** text-align СЏС‡РµР№РєРё. Р”РµС„РѕР»С‚: <th> в†’ center, <td> в†’ left. */

}

}

  | { kind: 'text'; runs: TextRun[] }

import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem } from '../../shared/types';
import { uid } from '../uid';
import type { StampTemplate } from './library';

const INK = '#0a2540';
const SOFT = '#374151';
const WHITE = '#ffffff';
const GREEN = '#15803d';
const GREEN_LIGHT = '#dcfce7';
const RED = '#b91c1c';
const RED_LIGHT = '#fecaca';
const BLUE = '#1e40af';
const BLUE_LIGHT = '#dbeafe';
const YELLOW = '#ca8a04';
const YELLOW_LIGHT = '#fef9c3';

/* ============================================================
   Р¤Р°Р±СЂРёРєРё
   ============================================================ */

const shape = (
  o: Partial<StampShapeItem> & { kind: StampShapeItem['kind'] },
): StampShapeItem => ({
  id: uid(),
  x: 0, y: 0, width: 100, height: 100,
  rotation: 0, cornerRadius: 0,
  stroke: INK, strokeWidth: 1, dash: 'solid',
  fill: undefined,
  ...o,
});

const text = (
  o: Partial<StampTextItem> & { text: string; mode: 'straight' | 'arc' },
): StampTextItem => ({
  id: uid(),
  x: 0, y: 0, width: 200, align: 'center',
  arcCx: 100, arcCy: 100, arcRadius: 60,
  arcStartDeg: -90, arcEndDeg: 90, arcFlip: false,
  arcOffset: 6, arcSpanDeg: 140,
  fontSize: 12, fontFamily: 'Arial', fontStyle: 'normal',
  textDecoration: '', fill: INK,
  ...o,
});

/**
 * Anchor-Р·Р°Р»РёРІРєР°: РїРѕР»РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊСЋ РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂСЏРµС‚ РіРµРѕРјРµС‚СЂРёСЋ С†РµР»РµРІРѕРіРѕ РєРѕРЅС‚РµР№РЅРµСЂР°.
 * bbox = РІРµСЃСЊ С€С‚Р°РјРї, seed Р·Р°РґР°С‘С‚СЃСЏ РІ РґРѕР»СЏС… РѕС‚ bbox РєРѕРЅС‚РµР№РЅРµСЂР°.
 */
function anchorFill(
  container: StampShapeItem,
  color: string,
  anchorLocal: { x: number; y: number },
  stampW: number,
  stampH: number,
  name = 'Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР°',
): StampShapeItem {
  return {
    id: uid(),
    kind: 'fill',
    x: 0, y: 0,
    width: stampW, height: stampH,
    rotation: 0,
    cornerRadius: 0,
    stroke: '', strokeWidth: 0, dash: 'solid',
    fill: color,
    name,
    fillTargetShapeId: container.id,
    fillAnchorLocal: anchorLocal,
  };
}

   ============================================================ */

/* ============================================================
   7. РџРћР›РћРЎРђРўР«Р™ РЁРўРђРњРџ (РґРёР°РіРѕРЅР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»РѕСЃС‹)
   ============================================================ */

function stripedStamp(): StampTemplate {
  const W = 260, H = 90;

  const frame = shape({
    kind: 'rect', x: 2, y: 2, width: W - 4, height: H - 4,
    cornerRadius: 4, strokeWidth: 2,
  });
  /* Р–С‘Р»С‚Р°СЏ РїРѕРґР»РѕР¶РєР° */
  const inner = shape({
    kind: 'rect', x: 6, y: 6, width: W - 12, height: H - 12,
    cornerRadius: 3, strokeWidth: 0, stroke: undefined,
  });
  const innerFill = anchorFill(
    inner, YELLOW_LIGHT,
    { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 },
    W, H, 'Р–С‘Р»С‚С‹Р№ С„РѕРЅ',
  );

  /* 3 С‚РѕРЅРєРёРµ РґРёР°РіРѕРЅР°Р»Рё РІ РїСЂР°РІРѕР№ С‚СЂРµС‚Рё */
  const angle = 55;
  const makeDiagonal = (x0: number): StampShapeItem => {
    const dx = Math.tan((angle * Math.PI) / 180) * H;
    return shape({
      kind: 'line',
      x: x0, y: 6,
      width: Math.abs(dx), height: H - 12,
      points: [0, H - 12, dx, 0],
      stroke: YELLOW, strokeWidth: 1.5, dash: 'solid',
    });
  };

  const d1 = makeDiagonal(W - 100);
  const d2 = makeDiagonal(W - 70);
  const d3 = makeDiagonal(W - 40);

  return {
    id: 'bank-striped',
    name: 'РџРѕР»РѕСЃР°С‚С‹Р№ РєР»РёС€-С€С‚Р°РјРї',
    description: 'РћС„РѕСЂРјРёС‚РµР»СЊСЃРєРёР№ С€С‚Р°РјРї СЃ РґРёР°РіРѕРЅР°Р»СЊРЅС‹РјРё РїРѕР»РѕСЃР°РјРё',
    icon: 'рџЋ—',
    width: W, height: H,
    shapes: [innerFill, frame, inner, d1, d2, d3],
    texts: [
      text({
        text: 'РџР РРќРЇРўРћ Рљ РРЎРџРћР›РќР•РќРР®', mode: 'straight',
        x: 14, y: 30, width: W - 130,
        fontSize: 15, fontStyle: 'bold', fill: INK, align: 'left',
      }),
      text({
        text: 'Р”Р°С‚Р° Рё РїРѕРґРїРёСЃСЊ РѕРїРµСЂР°С†РёРѕРЅРёСЃС‚Р°', mode: 'straight',
        x: 14, y: 58, width: W - 130,
        fontSize: 8, fill: SOFT, align: 'left',
      }),
    ],
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   lib/stamps/bankingStampsWithImages.ts

   Р‘Р°РЅРєРѕРІСЃРєРёРµ С€С‚Р°РјРїС‹, РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓСЋС‰РёРµ StampImageItem.

   Р’СЃРµ SVG-РёРєРѕРЅРєРё РІС€РёС‚С‹ РІ РєРѕРґ РєР°Рє data URL вЂ” РЅРµ С‚СЂРµР±СѓСЋС‚
   РІРЅРµС€РЅРёС… С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ. РљР°Р¶РґРѕРµ РёР·РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёРµ вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ РѕР±С‹С‡РЅС‹Р№ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚
   shapes/texts/images, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Р№ РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ РїРѕРґРІРёРЅСѓС‚СЊ, Р·Р°РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ РёР»Рё
   СѓРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ РІ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂРµ С€С‚Р°РјРїР°.

   РўРµС…РЅРёС‡РµСЃРєРё StampImageItem СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёС‚:
     - src: data URL РёР»Рё http(s)
     - x, y, width, height
     - fit: 'contain' | 'cover' | 'fill'
     - rotation, name, locked, hidden
   ============================================================ */

import type {
  StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem,
} from '../../shared/types';
import { uid } from '../uid';
import type { StampTemplate } from './library';

const INK = '#0a2540';
const SOFT = '#374151';
const WHITE = '#ffffff';
const GOLD = '#c8a951';
const GOLD_LIGHT = '#fef3c7';
const BLUE = '#1e40af';
const BLUE_LIGHT = '#dbeafe';
const RED = '#b91c1c';
const GREEN = '#15803d';

/* ============================================================
   Р’СЃС‚СЂРѕРµРЅРЅС‹Рµ SVG-РёРєРѕРЅРєРё
   ============================================================ */

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   Р СѓР±Р»СЊ вЂ” СЃРёРјРІРѕР» Р±Р°РЅРєРѕРІСЃРєРѕР№ СЃРёСЃС‚РµРјС‹
   ------------------------------------------------------------ */

const RUBLE_SVG = `
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 100 100">
  <circle cx="50" cy="50" r="48" fill="${BLUE}" stroke="${WHITE}" stroke-width="2"/>
  <text x="50" y="72" font-family="Arial, sans-serif" font-size="64"
        font-weight="bold" fill="${WHITE}" text-anchor="middle">в‚Ѕ</text>
</svg>`;

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   Р“РµСЂР± Р Р¤ вЂ” РґРІСѓРіР»Р°РІС‹Р№ РѕСЂС‘Р» (СЃС‚РёР»РёР·РѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Р№)
   ------------------------------------------------------------ */
