import React from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Circle, Line, Text, Star } from 'react-konva';
import type { ShapeElement } from '../../../shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';

interface Props extends CommonProps {
  el: ShapeElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<ShapeElement>) => void;
  onDblClick: (e?: any) => void;
  isEditing?: boolean;
}

export const ShapeEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate, onDblClick,
  onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu,
  isEditing,
}) => {
  const w = el.width, h = el.height;
  const common = {
    stroke: el.stroke,
    strokeWidth: el.strokeWidth,
    fill: el.fill === 'transparent' ? undefined : el.fill,
  };
  const hasText = !!el.text && !isEditing;
  const fontSize = el.fontSize || 16;

  const textPad = el.textPadding || { top: 8, right: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8 };
  const textAlign = el.textAlign || 'center';
  const textVerticalAlign = el.textVerticalAlign || 'middle';
  const textFontFamily = el.textFontFamily || 'Arial';
  const textLineHeight = el.textLineHeight || 1.3;
  const textFontStyle = el.textFontStyle || 'normal';
  const textDecoration = el.textDecoration || '';

  const innerW = Math.max(1, w - textPad.left - textPad.right);
  const innerH = Math.max(1, h - textPad.top - textPad.bottom);

  let tx = textPad.left, ty = textPad.top, tw = innerW, th = innerH;
  if (el.shape === 'circle') {
    const d = Math.min(w, h);
    tw = d * 0.7; th = d * 0.7;
    tx = (w - tw) / 2; ty = (h - th) / 2;
  } else if (el.shape === 'triangle') {
    tw = w * 0.55; th = h * 0.45;
    tx = (w - tw) / 2; ty = h * 0.5;
  } else if (el.shape === 'star') {
    tw = w * 0.5; th = h * 0.5;
    tx = (w - tw) / 2; ty = (h - th) / 2;
  }

  const lineBoxOffset = textVerticalAlign === 'top'
    ? ((textLineHeight - 1) * fontSize) / 2
    : 0;

  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id}
      name="element"
      x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
      draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDblClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(e); }}
      onDblTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart}
      onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
      onTransformEnd={(e) => {
        const n = e.target;
        const sx = n.scaleX(); const sy = n.scaleY();
        n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
        onUpdate({
          x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
          width: Math.max(10, el.width * sx),
          height: Math.max(10, el.height * sy),
        });
      }}
    >
      {el.shape === 'rect' && <Rect width={w} height={h} cornerRadius={el.radius || 0} {...common} />}
      {el.shape === 'roundRect' && <Rect width={w} height={h} cornerRadius={el.radius ?? 16} {...common} />}
      {el.shape === 'circle' && <Circle x={w / 2} y={h / 2} radius={Math.min(w, h) / 2} {...common} />}
      {el.shape === 'triangle' && <Line points={[w / 2, 0, w, h, 0, h, w / 2, 0]} closed {...common} lineJoin="round" />}
      {el.shape === 'star' && (
        <Star x={w / 2} y={h / 2} numPoints={5}
          innerRadius={Math.min(w, h) / 4}
          outerRadius={Math.min(w, h) / 2}
          {...common} />
      )}
      {el.shape === 'arrow' && (
        <Line
          points={[0, h / 2, w - 20, h / 2, w - 20, h / 2 - 15, w, h / 2, w - 20, h / 2 + 15, w - 20, h / 2]}
          {...common}
          lineJoin="round"
        />
      )}
      {hasText && (
        <Text
          x={tx} y={ty + lineBoxOffset} width={tw} height={th}
          text={el.text}
          fontSize={fontSize}
          fontFamily={textFontFamily}
          fontStyle={textFontStyle}
          textDecoration={textDecoration}
          fill={el.textFill}
          align={textAlign}
          verticalAlign={textVerticalAlign}
          lineHeight={textLineHeight}
          wrap="word"
          listening={false}
        />
      )}
    </Group>
  );
};


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: presentation\components\elements\StampEl.tsx
SIZE: 10066 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Group, Rect, Circle, Ellipse, Line,
  Text as KonvaText, Shape as KonvaShape, Image as KImage,
} from 'react-konva';
import type {
  StampElement, StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem,
} from '../../../shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';
import {
  drawArcText, canvasFont, konvaStyle, STAMP_DASH_MAP,
} from '../../../lib/stamps/render';
import { CompositeFillLayer } from '../stamps/CompositeFillLayer';

interface Props extends CommonProps {
  el: StampElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<StampElement>) => void;
}

export const StampEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate, onDblClick,
  onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu,
  isSelected,
}) => {
  const shapes = el.shapes ?? [];
  const texts = el.texts ?? [];
  const images = el.images ?? [];

  const regularShapes = useMemo(
    () => shapes.filter((s) => s.kind !== 'fill'),
    [shapes],
  );
  const legacyFills = useMemo(
    () => shapes.filter((s) => s.kind === 'fill' && !s.fillTargetShapeId),
    [shapes],
  );

  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id}
      name="element"
      x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
      draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDblClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick?.(e); }}
      onDblTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick?.(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart}
      onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
      onTransformEnd={(e) => {
        const n = e.target;
        const sx = n.scaleX(); const sy = n.scaleY();
        n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
        onUpdate({
          x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
          width: Math.max(40, el.width * sx),
          height: Math.max(40, el.height * sy),
        });
      }}
    >
      <Rect
        width={Math.max(1, el.width)}
        height={Math.max(1, el.height)}
        fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)"
        listening
      />

      <CompositeFillLayer
        width={el.width}
        height={el.height}
        shapes={shapes}
      />

      {legacyFills.map((s) => s.hidden ? null : (
        <LegacyFillShape key={s.id} shape={s} allShapes={shapes} />
      ))}

      {regularShapes.map((s) => s.hidden ? null : (
        <ShapeNode key={s.id} shape={s} allShapes={shapes} />
      ))}

      {images.map((im) => im.hidden ? null : <ImageNode key={im.id} im={im} />)}

      {texts.map((t) => t.hidden ? null : (
        <TextNode key={t.id} t={t} shapes={shapes} />
      ))}

      {isSelected && (
        <Rect
          width={el.width} height={el.height}
          stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5} dash={[6, 4]}
          listening={false}
        />
      )}
    </Group>
  );
};

/* ---------- shape nodes ---------- */

const ShapeNode: React.FC<{ shape: StampShapeItem; allShapes: StampShapeItem[] }> = ({ shape }) => {
  const cx = shape.x + shape.width / 2;
  const cy = shape.y + shape.height / 2;
  const common = {
    stroke: shape.stroke,
    strokeWidth: shape.strokeWidth,
    dash: STAMP_DASH_MAP[shape.dash],
    fill: shape.fill || 'transparent',
    listening: false,
  };

  if (shape.kind === 'rect') {
    return <Rect x={shape.x} y={shape.y} width={shape.width} height={shape.height}
      cornerRadius={shape.cornerRadius || 0} {...common} />;
  }
  if (shape.kind === 'circle') {
    const r = Math.min(shape.width, shape.height) / 2;
    return <Ellipse x={cx} y={cy} radiusX={r} radiusY={r} {...common} />;
  }
  if (shape.kind === 'ellipse') {
    return <Ellipse x={cx} y={cy} radiusX={shape.width / 2} radiusY={shape.height / 2} {...common} />;
  }
  if (shape.kind === 'triangle') {
    return <Line
      points={[cx, shape.y, shape.x + shape.width, shape.y + shape.height, shape.x, shape.y + shape.height]}
      closed lineJoin="round" {...common} />;
  }
  if (shape.kind === 'line') {
    const pts = shape.points && shape.points.length >= 4
      ? [...shape.points]
      : [0, shape.height / 2, shape.width, shape.height / 2];
    return <Line x={shape.x} y={shape.y} points={pts} lineCap="round" lineJoin="round" {...common} />;
  }
  return null;
};

/* ---------- legacy path fill ---------- */

const LegacyFillShape: React.FC<{ shape: StampShapeItem; allShapes: StampShapeItem[] }> = ({
  shape, allShapes,
}) => {
  const outer = shape.fillOuterId ? allShapes.find((x) => x.id === shape.fillOuterId) : null;
  if (!outer) return null;
  const holes = (shape.fillHoleIds || [])
    .map((id) => allShapes.find((x) => x.id === id))
    .filter((x): x is StampShapeItem => !!x && !x.hidden);
  const mode = shape.fillMode || 'subtract';

  return (
    <KonvaShape
      listening={false}
      sceneFunc={(c) => {
        const raw = (c as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
        const all = [outer, ...holes];
        const minX = Math.min(...all.map((p) => p.x));
        const minY = Math.min(...all.map((p) => p.y));
        const maxX = Math.max(...all.map((p) => p.x + p.width));
        const maxY = Math.max(...all.map((p) => p.y + p.height));

        raw.save();
        if (mode === 'intersect' && holes.length > 0) {
          const op = (s: StampShapeItem) => {
            const path = new Path2D();
            const { x, y, width: w, height: h } = s;
            if (s.kind === 'rect') path.rect(x, y, w, h);
            else if (s.kind === 'circle' || s.kind === 'ellipse') {
              path.ellipse(x + w / 2, y + h / 2, w / 2, h / 2, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            } else if (s.kind === 'triangle') {
              path.moveTo(x + w / 2, y);
              path.lineTo(x + w, y + h);
              path.lineTo(x, y + h);
              path.closePath();
            }
            return path;
          };
          raw.clip(op(outer));
          for (const h of holes) raw.clip(op(h));
        } else {
          const combined = new Path2D();
          const addPath = (s: StampShapeItem) => {
            const { x, y, width: w, height: h } = s;
            if (s.kind === 'rect') combined.rect(x, y, w, h);
            else if (s.kind === 'circle' || s.kind === 'ellipse') {
              combined.ellipse(x + w / 2, y + h / 2, w / 2, h / 2, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            } else if (s.kind === 'triangle') {
              combined.moveTo(x + w / 2, y);
              combined.lineTo(x + w, y + h);
              combined.lineTo(x, y + h);
              combined.closePath();
            }
          };
          addPath(outer);
          for (const h of holes) addPath(h);
          raw.clip(combined, 'evenodd');
        }
        raw.fillStyle = shape.fill || '#bfdbfe';
        raw.fillRect(minX, minY, maxX - minX, maxY - minY);
        raw.restore();
      }}
    />
  );
};

/* ---------- image node ---------- */

const ImageNode: React.FC<{ im: StampImageItem }> = ({ im }) => {
  const [img, setImg] = useState<HTMLImageElement | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!im.src) { setImg(null); return; }
    const i = new window.Image();
    i.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
    i.onload = () => setImg(i);
    i.onerror = () => setImg(null);
    i.src = im.src;
  }, [im.src]);
  if (!img) return null;
  const preserve =
    im.fit === 'cover' ? 'xMidYMid slice'
    : im.fit === 'fill' ? 'none'
    : 'xMidYMid meet';
  return (
    <KImage
      x={im.x} y={im.y}
      width={im.width} height={im.height}
      image={img}
      listening={false}
    />
  );
};

/* ---------- text node ---------- */

const TextNode: React.FC<{ t: StampTextItem; shapes: StampShapeItem[] }> = ({ t, shapes }) => {
  if (t.mode === 'straight') {
    return (
      <KonvaText
        x={t.x} y={t.y} width={t.width}
        text={t.text}
        fontSize={t.fontSize}
        fontFamily={t.fontFamily}
        fontStyle={konvaStyle(t.fontStyle || 'normal')}
        textDecoration={t.textDecoration}
        fill={t.fill}
        align={t.align}
        listening={false}
      />
    );
  }

  const host = t.arcHostId
    ? shapes.find((s) => s.id === t.arcHostId) || null
    : shapes.find((s) => {
        if (s.hidden || s.kind === 'line' || s.kind === 'fill') return false;
        const ccx = s.x + s.width / 2;
        const ccy = s.y + s.height / 2;
        return Math.abs(ccx - t.arcCx) < 1 && Math.abs(ccy - t.arcCy) < 1;
      }) || null;

  const rx = t.arcRx ?? t.arcRadius;
  const ry = t.arcRy ?? t.arcRadius;

  if (host && (host.kind === 'rect' || host.kind === 'triangle')) {
    return (
      <KonvaShape
        listening={false}
        sceneFunc={(ctx) => {
          const c = (ctx as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
          drawArcText(c, {
            text: t.text,
            cx: t.arcCx,
            cy: t.arcCy,
            radius: t.arcRadius,
            radiusX: rx,
            radiusY: ry,
            startDeg: t.arcStartDeg,
            endDeg: t.arcEndDeg,
            flip: t.arcFlip,
            font: canvasFont(t.fontFamily, t.fontStyle, t.fontSize),
            fill: t.fill,
          });
        }}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <KonvaShape
      listening={false}
      sceneFunc={(ctx) => {
        const c = (ctx as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
        drawArcText(c, {
          text: t.text,
          cx: t.arcCx,
          cy: t.arcCy,
          radius: t.arcRadius,
          radiusX: rx,
          radiusY: ry,
          startDeg: t.arcStartDeg,
          endDeg: t.arcEndDeg,
          flip: t.arcFlip,
          font: canvasFont(t.fontFamily, t.fontStyle, t.fontSize),
          fill: t.fill,
        });
      }}
    />
  );
};

import React, { useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Text } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { TableElement } from '../../../shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';

interface Props extends CommonProps {
  el: TableElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<TableElement>) => void;
  onCellDblClick?: (r: number, c: number) => void;
}

export const TableEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate, onCellDblClick, onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu,
}) => {
  const groupRef = useRef<Konva.Group>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const node = groupRef.current;
    if (!node) return;
    const fixed = () => ({ x: 0, y: 0, width: el.width, height: el.height });
    (node as any).getClientRect = fixed;
    (node as any).getSelfRect = fixed;
  }, [el.width, el.height]);

  const cellW = el.width / el.cols;
  const cellH = el.height / el.rows;
  const cells: React.ReactNode[] = [];
  const texts: React.ReactNode[] = [];

  for (let r = 0; r < el.rows; r++) {
    for (let c = 0; c < el.cols; c++) {
      const bg = r === 0 ? el.headerBg : '#fff';
      cells.push(
        <Rect key={`r-${r}-${c}`} x={c * cellW} y={r * cellH} width={cellW} height={cellH}
          fill={bg} stroke={el.borderColor} strokeWidth={1}
          onDblClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onCellDblClick?.(r, c); }}
        />
      );
      const cellText = el.data[r]?.[c] ?? '';
      const isPh = /\{\{[^}]+\}\}/.test(cellText);
      texts.push(
        <Text key={`t-${r}-${c}`} x={c * cellW + 6} y={r * cellH + 6}
          width={Math.max(1, cellW - 12)} height={Math.max(1, cellH - 12)}
          text={cellText} fontSize={el.fontSize}
          fill={isPh ? '#2563eb' : '#111'} fontStyle={isPh ? 'italic' : 'normal'}
          wrap="word" ellipsis={true} listening={false}
        />
      );
    }
  }

  return (
    <Group
      ref={groupRef}
      id={el.id} name="element" x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
      draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
      onTransformEnd={(e) => {
        const n = e.target;
        const sx = n.scaleX(); const sy = n.scaleY();
        n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
        onUpdate({
          x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
          width: Math.max(100, el.width * sx), height: Math.max(60, el.height * sy),
        });
      }}
    >
      {cells}
      {texts}
    </Group>
  );
};

/* ============================================================
   presentation/components/elements/tableLayout.ts
   Р Р°СЃРєР»Р°РґРєР° rich-С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†С‹. Р§РёСЃС‚Р°СЏ Р»РѕРіРёРєР°, Р±РµР· React.
   ============================================================ */

import type {
  RichTableData,
  RichTableCell,
  RichTableCellPadding,
  RichBlock,
} from '../../../lib/richText/types';
import { layoutRuns } from '../../../lib/richText/layout';

export const CELL_PADDING_DEFAULT: RichTableCellPadding =
  { top: 8, right: 10, bottom: 8, left: 10 };

const MIN_ROW_HEIGHT = 12;
export const CELL_BLOCK_MARGIN = 8;
export const MAX_NEST_DEPTH = 4;

export type CellBlockItem =
  | { kind: 'text'; layout: ReturnType<typeof layoutRuns>; height: number }
  | { kind: 'table'; data: RichTableData; height: number };

export interface TableLayout {
  colOffsets: number[];
  rowOffsets: number[];
  cellItems: Map<RichTableCell, CellBlockItem[]>;
  totalHeight: number;
  totalWidth: number;
}

export function cellPad(cell: RichTableCell): RichTableCellPadding {
  return cell.padding ?? CELL_PADDING_DEFAULT;
}

export function lineWidth(line: { width: number }[]): number {
  let w = 0;
  for (const r of line) w += r.width;
  return w;
}

export function computeTableLayout(
  data: RichTableData,
  width: number,
  fallbackFontSize: number,
  lineHeightCoef: number,
  depth: number,
): TableLayout {
  const { cells, rowCount, colCount } = data;

  const colPixelWidths = resolveColPixelWidths(data, width, colCount);
  const colOffsets = buildOffsets(colPixelWidths);

  const { cellItems, cellNaturalHeights } = measureCells(
    cells, colOffsets, colCount, fallbackFontSize, lineHeightCoef,
  );

  const rowHeights = resolveRowHeights(cells, rowCount, cellNaturalHeights);
  const rowOffsets = buildOffsets(rowHeights);

  return {
    colOffsets,
    rowOffsets,
    cellItems,
    totalHeight: rowOffsets[rowOffsets.length - 1] ?? 0,
    totalWidth: colOffsets[colOffsets.length - 1] ?? 0,
  };
}

export function measureTableHeight(
  data: RichTableData,
  width: number,
  fallbackFontSize: number,
  lineHeightCoef: number,
  depth = 0,
): number {
  if (!data.cells.length) return 0;
  return computeTableLayout(data, width, fallbackFontSize, lineHeightCoef, depth).totalHeight;
}

function resolveColPixelWidths(
  data: RichTableData,
  totalWidth: number,
  colCount: number,
): number[] {
  const { colWidths } = data;

  if (!colWidths || colWidths.length !== colCount) {
    return Array(colCount).fill(totalWidth / colCount);
  }

  const explicit = colWidths.map((w) => (typeof w === 'number' && w > 0 ? w : 0));
  const totalExplicit = explicit.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  const autoCount = explicit.filter((w) => w === 0).length;

  if (totalExplicit === 0) return Array(colCount).fill(totalWidth / colCount);
  if (autoCount === 0) return explicit.map((w) => (w / totalExplicit) * totalWidth);

  const remaining = Math.max(0, totalWidth - totalExplicit);
  const autoWidth = remaining / autoCount;
  return explicit.map((w) => (w > 0 ? w : autoWidth));
}

function buildOffsets(widths: number[]): number[] {
  const offsets = [0];
  for (let i = 0; i < widths.length; i++) offsets.push(offsets[i] + widths[i]);
  return offsets;
}

function measureCells(
  cells: RichTableCell[],
  colOffsets: number[],
  colCount: number,
  fallbackFontSize: number,
  lineHeightCoef: number,
): {
  cellItems: Map<RichTableCell, CellBlockItem[]>;
  cellNaturalHeights: Map<RichTableCell, number>;
} {
  const cellItems = new Map<RichTableCell, CellBlockItem[]>();
  const cellNaturalHeights = new Map<RichTableCell, number>();

  for (const cell of cells) {
    const p = cellPad(cell);
    const cw = colOffsets[Math.min(cell.colIndex + cell.colspan, colCount)]
             - colOffsets[cell.colIndex];
    const innerW = Math.max(10, cw - p.left - p.right);
    const items = measureCellContent(cell, innerW, fallbackFontSize, lineHeightCoef);
    const contentHeight = items.reduce((sum, it) => sum + it.height, 0)
      + Math.max(0, items.length - 1) * CELL_BLOCK_MARGIN;
    const minCellHeight = fallbackFontSize * lineHeightCoef + p.top + p.bottom;

    cellItems.set(cell, items);
    cellNaturalHeights.set(
      cell,
      Math.max(MIN_ROW_HEIGHT, minCellHeight, contentHeight + p.top + p.bottom),
    );
  }
  return { cellItems, cellNaturalHeights };
}

function measureCellContent(
  cell: RichTableCell,
  innerWidth: number,
  fallbackFontSize: number,
  lineHeightCoef: number,
): CellBlockItem[] {
  const items: CellBlockItem[] = [];
  for (const block of cell.blocks as RichBlock[]) {
    if (block.kind === 'text') {
      const layout = layoutRuns(block.runs, innerWidth, lineHeightCoef);
      items.push({ kind: 'text', layout, height: layout.totalHeight });
    } else {
      const h = measureTableHeight(block.data, innerWidth, fallbackFontSize, lineHeightCoef, 1);
      items.push({ kind: 'table', data: block.data, height: h });
    }
  }
  return items;
}

function resolveRowHeights(
  cells: RichTableCell[],
  rowCount: number,
  cellNaturalHeights: Map<RichTableCell, number>,
): number[] {
  const rowHeights: number[] = new Array(rowCount).fill(MIN_ROW_HEIGHT);

  for (const cell of cells) {
    if (cell.rowspan !== 1) continue;
    const h = cellNaturalHeights.get(cell) ?? 0;
    if (h > rowHeights[cell.rowIndex]) rowHeights[cell.rowIndex] = h;
  }

  for (let iter = 0; iter < 10; iter++) {
    let changed = false;
    for (const cell of cells) {
      if (cell.rowspan <= 1) continue;
      const need = cellNaturalHeights.get(cell) ?? 0;
      let have = 0;
      for (let r = cell.rowIndex; r < cell.rowIndex + cell.rowspan; r++) {
        have += rowHeights[r] ?? 0;
      }
      if (have + 0.5 < need) {
        const delta = (need - have) / cell.rowspan;
        for (let r = cell.rowIndex; r < cell.rowIndex + cell.rowspan; r++) {
          rowHeights[r] = (rowHeights[r] ?? 0) + delta;
        }
        changed = true;
      }
    }
    if (!changed) break;
  }
  return rowHeights;
}

import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Text as KonvaText } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { TextElement } from '../../../shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';
import { parseHtmlToBlocks, layoutRuns, plainToHtml, type RichTableData, type PositionedRun } from '../../../lib/richText';
import { RichTable, measureTableHeight } from './RichTable';
import { TEXT_PLACEHOLDER } from '../../../shared/constants';

const BLOCK_MARGIN = 0;

interface Props extends CommonProps {
  el: TextElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<TextElement>) => void;
  onDblClick: (e?: any) => void;
  isEditing?: boolean;
}

type PreparedBlock = { kind: 'text'; layout: any; height: number } | { kind: 'table'; data: RichTableData; height: number };

export const TextEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate, onDblClick, onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu, isEditing,
}) => {
  const padding = el.padding || { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 };
  const innerWidth = Math.max(1, el.width - padding.left - padding.right);
  const innerHeight = Math.max(1, el.height - padding.top - padding.bottom);

  const groupRef = useRef<Konva.Group>(null);

  // РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј bbox С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РїСЂРё РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРё СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂРѕРІ, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ Transformer РјРѕРі С‚СЏРЅСѓС‚СЊ
  useEffect(() => {
    const node = groupRef.current;
    if (!node) return;
    const fixed = () => ({ x: 0, y: 0, width: el.width, height: el.height });
    (node as any).getClientRect = fixed;
    (node as any).getSelfRect = fixed;
  }, [el.width, el.height]);

  const isPlaceholder = useMemo(() => !el.html && (el.text === TEXT_PLACEHOLDER || !el.text?.trim()), [el.html, el.text]);

  const blocks = useMemo(() => {
    const baseFontStyle = el.fontStyle || 'normal';
    const resolvedFontStyle = isPlaceholder ? `${baseFontStyle} italic` : baseFontStyle;
    const resolvedFill = isPlaceholder ? '#9ca3af' : el.fill;
    const sourceHtml = el.html && el.html.trim() ? el.html : plainToHtml(el.text || '');
    return parseHtmlToBlocks(sourceHtml, { fontSize: el.fontSize, fontFamily: el.fontFamily, fontStyle: resolvedFontStyle, textDecoration: el.textDecoration || '', fill: resolvedFill });
  }, [el.html, el.text, el.fontSize, el.fontFamily, el.fontStyle, el.textDecoration, el.fill, isPlaceholder]);

  const lineHeightCoef = el.lineHeight || 1.5;

  const prepared = useMemo(() => {
    const items: PreparedBlock[] = [];
    let totalHeight = 0;
    for (const block of blocks) {
      if (block.kind === 'text') {
        const layout = layoutRuns(block.runs, innerWidth, lineHeightCoef);
        if (layout.lines.length === 0) continue;
        items.push({ kind: 'text', layout, height: layout.totalHeight });
        totalHeight += layout.totalHeight;
      } else {
        const h = measureTableHeight(block.data, innerWidth, el.fontSize, lineHeightCoef);
        items.push({ kind: 'table', data: block.data, height: h });
        totalHeight += h;
      }
    }
    if (items.length > 1 && BLOCK_MARGIN > 0) totalHeight += (items.length - 1) * BLOCK_MARGIN;
    return { items, totalHeight };
  }, [blocks, innerWidth, lineHeightCoef, el.fontSize]);

  let offsetY = padding.top;
  if (el.verticalAlign === 'middle') offsetY = padding.top + Math.max(0, (innerHeight - prepared.totalHeight) / 2);
  else if (el.verticalAlign === 'bottom') offsetY = padding.top + Math.max(0, innerHeight - prepared.totalHeight);

  const effectiveFill = isPlaceholder ? '#9ca3af' : el.fill;
  const bg = el.background && el.background !== 'transparent' ? el.background : 'transparent';
  const hitFill = bg === 'transparent' ? 'rgba(0,0,0,0.001)' : bg;
  let cursorY = offsetY;

  return (
    <Group
      ref={groupRef}
      id={el.id}
      name="element"
      x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
      width={el.width} height={el.height}
      draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDblClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(e); }}
      onDblTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
      onTransformEnd={(e) => {
        const n = e.target;
        const sx = n.scaleX(); const sy = n.scaleY();
        n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
        onUpdate({
          x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
          width: Math.max(100, el.width * sx), // РњРёРЅРёРјСѓРј 100 РїРёРєСЃРµР»РµР№ РґР»СЏ RichText
          height: Math.max(40, el.height * sy),
        });
      }}
    >
      <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} fill={hitFill} listening={true} />

      {!isEditing && prepared.items.map((item, bi) => {
        const blockTop = cursorY;
        cursorY += item.height;
        if (bi < prepared.items.length - 1) cursorY += BLOCK_MARGIN;

        if (item.kind === 'text') {
          return (
            <React.Fragment key={`b-${bi}`}>
              {item.layout.lines.map((line: PositionedRun[], i: number) => {
                const lw = line.reduce((acc: number, r: PositionedRun) => acc + r.width, 0);
                let lineOffsetX = 0;
                if (el.align === 'center') lineOffsetX = Math.max(0, (innerWidth - lw) / 2);
                else if (el.align === 'right') lineOffsetX = Math.max(0, innerWidth - lw);
                return line.map((run, j) => {
                  const x = padding.left + lineOffsetX + run.x;
                  const y = blockTop + run.y;
                  const resolvedFontStyle = run.bold && run.italic ? 'italic bold' : run.bold ? 'bold' : run.italic ? 'italic' : isPlaceholder ? 'italic' : 'normal';
                  return (
                    <React.Fragment key={`${i}-${j}`}>
                      {run.bgColor && <Rect x={x} y={y} width={run.width} height={run.actualFontSize * 1.2} fill={run.bgColor} listening={false} />}
                      <KonvaText x={x} y={y} text={run.text} fontSize={run.actualFontSize} fontFamily={run.fontFamily || el.fontFamily} fontStyle={resolvedFontStyle} textDecoration={run.underline && run.strike ? 'underline line-through' : run.underline ? 'underline' : run.strike ? 'line-through' : ''} fill={run.color || effectiveFill} listening={false} />
                    </React.Fragment>
                  );
                });
              })}
            </React.Fragment>
          );
        }

        return <RichTable key={`table-${bi}`} data={item.data} x={padding.left} y={blockTop} width={innerWidth} fontFamily={el.fontFamily} fallbackFontSize={el.fontSize} fallbackFill={el.fill} lineHeightCoef={lineHeightCoef} />;
      })}
    </Group>
  );
};

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';

interface FloatingPropsProps {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  element: CanvasElement;
  onUpdate: (patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
  onBringForward: () => void;
  onSendBackward: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
}

const normalizeColor = (c: string | undefined | null): string => {
  if (!c || c === 'transparent') return '#ffffff';
  if (/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{3}$/.test(c)) {
    return '#' + c[1] + c[1] + c[2] + c[2] + c[3] + c[3];
  }
  return c;
};

export const FloatingProps: React.FC<FloatingPropsProps> = ({
  x, y, element, onUpdate, onClose, onBringForward, onSendBackward, onDelete,
}) => {
  const panelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [position, setPosition] = useState({ x, y });

  useEffect(() => {
    const panel = panelRef.current;
    if (!panel) return;
    const rect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
    let newX = x, newY = y - 50;
    if (newX + rect.width > window.innerWidth) newX = window.innerWidth - rect.width - 10;
    if (newX < 10) newX = 10;
    if (newY < 10) newY = y + element.height + 10;
    if (newY + rect.height > window.innerHeight) newY = window.innerHeight - rect.height - 10;
    setPosition({ x: newX, y: newY });
  }, [x, y, element.height]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const down = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (panelRef.current && !panelRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) onClose();
    };
    setTimeout(() => document.addEventListener('mousedown', down), 0);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', down);
  }, [onClose]);

  return (
    <div ref={panelRef} className="floating-props" style={{ left: position.x, top: position.y }}>
      <div className="fp-group">
        <button onClick={onBringForward} title="Р’С‹С€Рµ">в¬†</button>
        <button onClick={onSendBackward} title="РќРёР¶Рµ">в¬‡</button>
        <button onClick={onDelete} title="РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ">рџ—‘</button>
      </div>
      {element.type === 'shape' && (
        <>
          <div className="fp-group">
            <label>Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР°</label>
            <input type="color" value={normalizeColor(element.fill)} onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ fill: e.target.value })} />
            <button onClick={() => onUpdate({ fill: 'transparent' })}>в€…</button>
          </div>
          <div className="fp-group">
            <label>РћР±РІРѕРґРєР°</label>
            <input type="color" value={normalizeColor(element.stroke)} onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ stroke: e.target.value })} />
          </div>
        </>
      )}
      {element.type === 'text' && (
        <>
          <div className="fp-group">
            <label>Р¦РІРµС‚</label>
            <input type="color" value={normalizeColor(element.fill)} onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ fill: e.target.value })} />
          </div>
          <div className="fp-group">
            <label>Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ</label>
            <input type="number" value={element.fontSize} onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ fontSize: Number(e.target.value) })} />
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

/**
 * Grid Layer Component - Presentation Layer
 * Renders canvas grid
 */

import React from 'react';
import { Shape } from 'react-konva';
import { GRID_SIZE } from '../../shared/constants';

interface GridLayerProps {
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

export const GridLayer: React.FC<GridLayerProps> = ({ width, height }) => {
  return (
    <Shape
      listening={false}
      sceneFunc={(ctx, shape) => {
        const context = ctx._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
        context.beginPath();

        // Vertical lines
        for (let x = 0; x <= width; x += GRID_SIZE) {
          context.moveTo(x, 0);
          context.lineTo(x, height);
        }

        // Horizontal lines
        for (let y = 0; y <= height; y += GRID_SIZE) {
          context.moveTo(0, y);
          context.lineTo(width, y);
        }

        context.strokeStyle = '#eef2f7';
        context.lineWidth = 1;
        context.stroke();

        ctx.strokeShape(shape);
      }}
    />
  );
};

GridLayer.displayName = 'GridLayer';

/**
 * Inline Editor Component - Presentation Layer
 * Contenteditable overlay for canvas text editing
 */

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState, useCallback } from 'react';

interface InlineEditorProps {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  value: string;
  html?: string;
  fontSize: number;
  fontFamily: string;
  fontStyle: string;
  textDecoration: string;
  fill: string;
  align: 'left' | 'center' | 'right' | 'justify';
  verticalAlign: 'top' | 'middle' | 'bottom';
  lineHeight: number;
  padding: { top: number; right: number; bottom: number; left: number };
  background: string;
  isOpen: boolean;
  onChange: (value: { value?: string; html?: string }) => void;
  onCommit: () => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}

export const InlineEditor: React.FC<InlineEditorProps> = ({
  x,
  y,
  width,
  height,
  value,
  html,
  fontSize,
  fontFamily,
  fontStyle,
  textDecoration,
  fill,
  align,
  verticalAlign,
  lineHeight,
  padding,
  background,
  isOpen,
  onChange,
  onCommit,
  onCancel,
}) => {
  const editorRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [isComposing, setIsComposing] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen || !editorRef.current) return;

    const editor = editorRef.current;
    editor.innerHTML = html || value || '<p><br></p>';
    editor.focus();

    // Select all content
    const range = document.createRange();
    range.selectNodeContents(editor);
    const selection = window.getSelection();
    if (selection) {
      selection.removeAllRanges();
      selection.addRange(range);
    }
  }, [isOpen, html, value]);

  const handleInput = useCallback(() => {
    if (!editorRef.current) return;
    const newHtml = editorRef.current.innerHTML;
    const newText = editorRef.current.innerText;
    onChange({ value: newText, html: newHtml });
  }, [onChange]);

  const handleKeyDown = useCallback(
    (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (isComposing) return;

      if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
        e.preventDefault();
        onCommit();
      } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        e.preventDefault();
        onCancel();
      }
    },
    [isComposing, onCommit, onCancel]
  );

  const handleBlur = useCallback(() => {
    // Small delay to allow click events to process
    setTimeout(() => {
      onCommit();
    }, 100);
  }, [onCommit]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const verticalAlignMap = {
    top: 'flex-start',
    middle: 'center',
    bottom: 'flex-end',
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="inline-editor-overlay"
      style={{
        position: 'fixed',
        left: x,
        top: y,
        width,
        height,
        zIndex: 1000,
      }}
    >
      <div
        ref={editorRef}
        className="inline-editor"
        contentEditable
        suppressContentEditableWarning
        onInput={handleInput}
        onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
        onBlur={handleBlur}
        onCompositionStart={() => setIsComposing(true)}
        onCompositionEnd={() => setIsComposing(false)}
        style={{
          width: '100%',
          height: '100%',
          padding: `${padding.top}px ${padding.right}px ${padding.bottom}px ${padding.left}px`,
          fontSize: `${fontSize}px`,
          fontFamily,
          fontStyle,
          textDecoration,
          color: fill,
          backgroundColor: background === 'transparent' ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)' : background,
          textAlign: align,
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          justifyContent: verticalAlignMap[verticalAlign],
          lineHeight,
          outline: 'none',
          border: '2px solid #3b82f6',
          borderRadius: '4px',
          boxShadow: '0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.15)',
          overflow: 'auto',
          wordWrap: 'break-word',
          whiteSpace: 'pre-wrap',
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
};

InlineEditor.displayName = 'InlineEditor';

import React from 'react';
import { RichTextEditor } from './RichTextEditor';
import type { EditingState } from '../stores/ModalsStore';

interface Props {
  editing: EditingState;
  onClose: () => void;
  onCommit: (html: string | undefined, text: string) => void;
}

export const InlineEditorHost: React.FC<Props> = ({ editing, onClose, onCommit }) => {
  if (editing.isRich) {
    return (
      <RichTextEditor
        isOpen
        initialHtml={editing.html ?? `<p>${editing.value ?? ''}</p>`}
        onClose={onClose}
        onSave={(html) => {
          const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
          const text = doc.body.textContent ?? '';
          onCommit(html, text);
        }}
      />
    );
  }

  // ... (РѕР±С‹С‡РЅС‹Р№ PlainOverlayInline, РѕРЅ РЅРµ РјРµРЅСЏРµС‚СЃСЏ)
  return null; // Р·Р°РіР»СѓС€РєР° РґР»СЏ РєРѕРјРїРёР»СЏС†РёРё, Р·РґРµСЃСЊ РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ Р±С‹С‚СЊ РІР°С€ PlainOverlayInline РёР· РїСЂРѕС€Р»С‹С… С€Р°РіРѕРІ
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import type { DocFormat, Orientation, CanvasElement, Slide } from '../../shared/types';
import { FORMATS } from '../../shared/constants';
import { useStore } from '../../shared/store/store';

const ToolSection: React.FC<{ title: string; icon?: string; defaultOpen?: boolean; children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ title, icon, defaultOpen = false, children }) => {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(defaultOpen);
  return (
    <div className={`tool-section ${open ? 'open' : ''}`}>
      <button type="button" className="section-head" onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}>
        <span className="section-ico">{icon}</span>
        <span className="section-title">{title}</span>
        <span className="section-chev">{open ? 'в–ѕ' : 'в–ё'}</span>
      </button>
      {open && <div className="section-body">{children}</div>}
    </div>
  );
};

const createShapeBase = (): CanvasElement => ({
  id: generateId(),
  type: 'shape',
  shape: 'rect',
  x: 100, y: 100, width: 150, height: 100, rotation: 0,
  fill: '#3b82f6', stroke: '#1d4ed8', strokeWidth: 2,
  text: '', fontSize: 14, textFill: '#ffffff',
});

function computeFormatSize(format: DocFormat, orientation: Orientation, customSize: { w: number; h: number }) {
  const base = format === 'custom' ? customSize : FORMATS[format]?.portrait ?? FORMATS.a4.portrait;
  return orientation === 'portrait' ? { w: base.w, h: base.h } : { w: base.h, h: base.w };
}

function reflowElements(elements: readonly CanvasElement[], slide: Slide, newWidth: number, newHeight: number): CanvasElement[] {
  const printW = Math.max(0, newWidth - slide.padding.left - slide.padding.right);
  const printH = Math.max(0, newHeight - slide.padding.top - slide.padding.bottom);
  return elements.map((el) => {
    if (el.type === 'footer') return { ...el, x: slide.padding.left, y: newHeight - slide.padding.bottom - el.height, width: printW };
    if (el.type === 'placeholder') return { ...el, x: slide.padding.left, y: slide.padding.top, width: printW, height: printH };
    return { ...el, x: Math.min(el.x, Math.max(0, newWidth - el.width)), y: Math.min(el.y, Math.max(0, newHeight - el.height)), width: Math.min(el.width, printW) };
  });
}

export const LeftPanel: React.FC = () => {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const selStore = useSelectionStore();
  const modalsStore = useModalsStore();

  const activeSlide = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.slides.find((x) => x.id === s.activeId));
  const fillMode = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.fillMode);
  const fillColor = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.fillColor);

  const customSize = { w: 794, h: 1123 };

  if (!activeSlide) return null;

  const add = (el: CanvasElement) => {
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (s) (s as any).elements = [...(s.elements as any), el];
    }, 'add-element');
    selStore.select(el.id);
  };

  const changeFormat = (format: DocFormat) => {
    if (format === activeSlide.format) return;
    const { w, h } = computeFormatSize(format, activeSlide.orientation, customSize);
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (!s) return;
      (s as any).format = format;
      (s as any).width = w;
      (s as any).height = h;
      (s as any).elements = reflowElements(s.elements, s, w, h);
    }, 'change-format');
    selStore.clear();
  };

  const changeOrientation = (orientation: Orientation) => {
    if (orientation === activeSlide.orientation) return;
    const { w, h } = computeFormatSize(activeSlide.format, orientation, customSize);
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (!s) return;
      (s as any).orientation = orientation;
      (s as any).width = w;
      (s as any).height = h;
      (s as any).elements = reflowElements(s.elements, s, w, h);
    }, 'change-orientation');
    selStore.clear();
  };

  return (
    <aside className="left-panel">
      <ToolSection title="Р¤РѕСЂРјР°С‚" icon="рџ“ђ" defaultOpen>
        <select className="field" value={activeSlide.format} onChange={(e) => changeFormat(e.target.value as DocFormat)}>
          <option value="a4">A4</option>
          <option value="a5">A5</option>
          <option value="letter">Letter</option>
          <option value="custom">РЎРІРѕР№</option>
        </select>
        <div className="segmented" style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>
          <button type="button" className={activeSlide.orientation === 'portrait' ? 'active' : ''} onClick={() => changeOrientation('portrait')}>РљРЅРёР¶РЅР°СЏ</button>
          <button type="button" className={activeSlide.orientation === 'landscape' ? 'active' : ''} onClick={() => changeOrientation('landscape')}>РђР»СЊР±РѕРјРЅР°СЏ</button>
        </div>
      </ToolSection>

      <ToolSection title="Р­Р»РµРјРµРЅС‚С‹" icon="рџ§©" defaultOpen>
        <div className="tool-grid">
          <button type="button" className="tool-tile" onClick={() => add(createShapeBase())}>
            <span className="tile-ico">в–­</span><span>РџСЂСЏРјРѕСѓРі.</span>
          </button>
          <button type="button" className="tool-tile" onClick={() => add({ ...createShapeBase(), shape: 'circle' } as CanvasElement)}>
            <span className="tile-ico">в—Ї</span><span>РљСЂСѓРі</span>
          </button>
          <button type="button" className="tool-tile" onClick={() =>
            add({
              id: generateId(), type: 'text', x: 100, y: 100, width: 300, height: 100, rotation: 0,
              text: 'Р’РІРµРґРёС‚Рµ С‚РµРєСЃС‚', richText: true, html: '',
              fontSize: 18, fontFamily: 'Arial', fill: '#000', fontStyle: 'normal', textDecoration: '',
              align: 'left', verticalAlign: 'top', lineHeight: 1.5,
              padding: { top: 8, right: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8 }, background: 'transparent',
            } as CanvasElement)
          }>
            <span className="tile-ico">T</span><span>РўРµРєСЃС‚</span>

/**
 * РџСЂРёРІРѕРґРёС‚ Р»СЋР±РѕРµ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРѕРІРѕРµ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРµ С†РІРµС‚Р° Рє С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚Сѓ, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Р№ РїСЂРёРЅРёРјР°РµС‚
 * `<input type="color">`. РџРѕ HTML-СЃРїРµРєРµ СЌС‚Рѕ `#rrggbb` (6 hex-С†РёС„СЂ).
 *
 * В§F-SH-03: `'transparent'` вЂ” РґРѕРїСѓСЃС‚РёРјРѕРµ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРµ РґР»СЏ Р·Р°Р»РёРІРєРё, РЅРѕ РґР»СЏ РїРёРєРµСЂР°
 * РїРѕРєР°Р·С‹РІР°РµРј Р±РµР»С‹Р№.
 */
export function normalizeColor(c: string | undefined | null): string {
  if (!c || c === 'transparent') return '#ffffff';
  if (/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{3}$/.test(c)) {
    return '#' + c[1] + c[1] + c[2] + c[2] + c[3] + c[3];
  }
  return c;
}

const Group: React.FC<{ title: string; children: React.ReactNode }> = ({

  children,

      {title}
    </h4>
    {children}

);

          <input style={inputStyle} type="number" value={Math.round(el.rotation)} onChange={(e) => update({ rotation: +e.target.value })} />

      {el.type === 'text' && <TextProps el={el} update={update} />}

      {el.type === 'line' && <LineProps el={el} update={update} />}
      {el.type === 'image' && <ImageProps el={el} update={update} />}
      {el.type === 'table' && <TableProps el={el} update={update} />}
      {el.type === 'footer' && <FooterProps el={el} update={update} />}
      {el.type === 'placeholder' && <PlaceholderProps el={el} update={update} />}
      {el.type === 'fill' && <FillProps el={el} update={update} />}
      {el.type === 'connector' && <ConnectorProps el={el} update={update} />}
      {el.type === 'stamp' && <StampInfo el={el} />}
      {el.type === 'diagram' && <DiagramInfo el={el} />}
    </div>

      type="button"
      style={{ ...inputStyle, cursor: 'pointer', marginTop: 6 }}
      onClick={() => {
        const input = document.createElement('input');
        input.type = 'file';
        input.accept = 'image/*';
        input.onchange = () => {
          const f = input.files?.[0];
          if (!f) return;
          const r = new FileReader();
          r.onload = () => update({ src: r.result as string });
          r.readAsDataURL(f);
        };
        input.click();
      }}
    >
      Р—Р°РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ

const TableProps: React.FC<{ el: TableElement; update: (p: any) => void }> = ({ el, update }) => (
  <Group title="РўР°Р±Р»РёС†Р°">
    <Row>
      <L label="РЎС‚СЂРѕРє">
        <input style={inputStyle} type="number" min={1} value={el.rows}
          onChange={(e) => {
            const rows = Math.max(1, +e.target.value);
            const data = el.data.slice(0, rows);
            while (data.length < rows) data.push(Array(el.cols).fill(''));
            update({ rows, data });
          }}
        />
      </L>
      <L label="РљРѕР»РѕРЅРѕРє">
        <input style={inputStyle} type="number" min={1} value={el.cols}
          onChange={(e) => {
            const cols = Math.max(1, +e.target.value);
            const data = el.data.map((r) => {
              const nr = r.slice(0, cols);
              while (nr.length < cols) nr.push('');
              return nr;
            });
            update({ cols, data });
          }}
        />
      </L>
    </Row>
    <L label="Р¦РІРµС‚ РіСЂР°РЅРёС†">
      <input style={inputStyle} type="color" value={normalizeColor(el.borderColor)} onChange={(e) => update({ borderColor: e.target.value })} />
    </L>
    <L label="Р¦РІРµС‚ С€Р°РїРєРё">
      <input style={inputStyle} type="color" value={normalizeColor(el.headerBg)} onChange={(e) => update({ headerBg: e.target.value })} />

import React from 'react';

interface Props {
  title: string;
  colors: string[];
  onPick: (color: string) => void;
  onReset?: () => void;
  resetLabel?: string;
}

export const ColorPickerPopover: React.FC<Props> = ({
  title, colors, onPick, onReset, resetLabel = 'РЎР±СЂРѕСЃРёС‚СЊ',
}) => (
  <div className="rt-popover">
    <div className="rt-popover-title">{title}</div>
    <div className="rt-color-grid">
      {colors.map((c) => (
        <button
          key={c}
          type="button"
          className="rt-color-cell"
          style={{ background: c }}
          title={c}
          onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); onPick(c); }}
        />
      ))}
    </div>
    {onReset && (
      <button
        type="button"
        className="rt-popover-action"
        onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); onReset(); }}
      >
        {resetLabel}
      </button>
    )}
  </div>
);

import { Extension } from '@tiptap/core';
import TableCell from '@tiptap/extension-table-cell';
import TableHeader from '@tiptap/extension-table-header';

export const FontSize = Extension.create({
  name: 'fontSize',
  addOptions() {
    return { types: ['textStyle'] };
  },
  addGlobalAttributes() {
    return [{
      types: this.options.types,
      attributes: {
        fontSize: {
          default: null,
          parseHTML: (el) => el.style.fontSize?.replace(/['"]+/g, '') || null,
          renderHTML: (attrs) =>
            attrs.fontSize ? { style: `font-size: ${attrs.fontSize}` } : {},
        },
      },
    }];
  },
  addCommands() {
    return {
      setFontSize: (fontSize: string) => ({ chain }: any) =>
        chain().setMark('textStyle', { fontSize }).run(),
      unsetFontSize: () => ({ chain }: any) =>
        chain().setMark('textStyle', { fontSize: null }).removeEmptyTextStyle().run(),
    } as any;
  },
});

const CELL_ATTRIBUTES = {
  bgColor: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement) => el.style.backgroundColor || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: any) =>
      attrs.bgColor ? { style: `background-color: ${attrs.bgColor}` } : {},
  },
  borderColor: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement) => el.style.borderColor || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: any) =>
      attrs.borderColor ? { style: `border-color: ${attrs.borderColor}` } : {},
  },
  borderWidth: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement) => el.style.borderWidth || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: any) =>
      attrs.borderWidth ? { style: `border-width: ${attrs.borderWidth}` } : {},
  },
  borderStyle: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement) => el.style.borderStyle || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: any) =>
      attrs.borderStyle ? { style: `border-style: ${attrs.borderStyle}` } : {},
  },
  cellPadding: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement) => el.style.padding || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: any) =>
      attrs.cellPadding ? { style: `padding: ${attrs.cellPadding}` } : {},
  },
};

export const CustomTableCell = TableCell.extend({
  addAttributes() {
    return { ...this.parent?.(), ...CELL_ATTRIBUTES };
  },
});

export const CustomTableHeader = TableHeader.extend({
  addAttributes() {
    return { ...this.parent?.(), ...CELL_ATTRIBUTES };
  },
});

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import type { Editor } from '@tiptap/react';
import { TableSizePicker } from './TableSizePicker';
import { CellFillPopover, CellBorderPopover, CellPadPopover } from './TableCellPopovers';

export const TableOperationsToolbar: React.FC<{ editor: Editor }> = ({ editor }) => {
  const [pop, setPop] = useState<null | 'size' | 'fill' | 'border' | 'pad'>(null);
  const toggle = (k: any) => setPop((v) => (v === k ? null : k));
  const insideTable = editor.isActive('table');

  const setCellAttrs = (attrs: Record<string, any>) => {
    editor.chain().focus().updateAttributes('tableCell', attrs).run();
    editor.chain().focus().updateAttributes('tableHeader', attrs).run();
  };

  return (
    <>
      <div className="rt-sep" />
      <div className="rt-popover-wrap">
        <button type="button" className="rt-btn" title="Р’СЃС‚Р°РІРёС‚СЊ С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†Сѓ" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); toggle('size'); }}>
          вЉћ РўР°Р±Р»РёС†Р°
        </button>
        {pop === 'size' && <TableSizePicker onPick={(r, c) => { editor.chain().focus().insertTable({ rows: r, cols: c, withHeaderRow: true }).run(); setPop(null); }} />}
      </div>

      {insideTable && (
        <>
          <div className="rt-sep" />
          <div className="rt-group">
            <button type="button" className="rt-btn" title="Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєСѓ" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); editor.chain().focus().addRowAfter().run(); }}>+РЎС‚СЂ</button>
            <button type="button" className="rt-btn" title="РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєСѓ" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); editor.chain().focus().deleteRow().run(); }}>-РЎС‚СЂ</button>
            <button type="button" className="rt-btn" title="Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ РєРѕР»РѕРЅРєСѓ" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); editor.chain().focus().addColumnAfter().run(); }}>+РљРѕР»</button>
            <button type="button" className="rt-btn" title="РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ РєРѕР»РѕРЅРєСѓ" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); editor.chain().focus().deleteColumn().run(); }}>-РљРѕР»</button>
          </div>
          <div className="rt-sep" />

          <div className="rt-group">

interface Props {
  onPick: (rows: number, cols: number) => void;
}

const MAX = 6;

export const TableSizePicker: React.FC<Props> = ({ onPick }) => {
  const [hover, setHover] = useState({ r: -1, c: -1 });

  return (
    <div className="rt-popover">
      <div className="rt-popover-title">Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†С‹</div>
      <div className="rt-table-grid">
        {Array.from({ length: MAX }, (_, r) =>
          Array.from({ length: MAX }, (_, c) => {
            const active = r <= hover.r && c <= hover.c && hover.r >= 0 && hover.c >= 0;
            return (
              <button
                key={`${r}-${c}`}
                type="button"
                className={`rt-table-cell ${active ? 'active' : ''}`}
                onMouseEnter={() => setHover({ r, c })}
                onMouseLeave={() => setHover({ r: -1, c: -1 })}
                onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); onPick(r + 1, c + 1); }}
              />
            );
          })
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="rt-popover-hint">
        {hover.r >= 0 ? `${hover.r + 1} Г— ${hover.c + 1}` : 'РќР°РІРµРґРёС‚Рµ РєСѓСЂСЃРѕСЂ'}

export const RichTextEditor: React.FC<Props> = ({ isOpen, initialHtml, onClose, onSave }) => {

  const [linkUrl, setLinkUrl] = useState('');

    extensions: [

      TextAlign.configure({ types: ['heading', 'paragraph'] }),
      TextStyle,
      Color,
      FontFamily,
      FontSize,
      Highlight.configure({ multicolor: true }),

      Table.configure({ resizable: true }),

    if (isOpen && editor) {
      editor.commands.setContent(cleanHtml);
      setTimeout(() => { try { editor.commands.focus('end'); } catch {} }, 50);

    const isEmpty = html === '<p></p>' || html === '<p><br></p>';

    onClose();

    if (!editor) return;
    if (showLinkInput) {

      else editor.chain().focus().extendMarkRange('link').unsetLink().run();
      setShowLinkInput(false);
      setLinkUrl('');
    } else {
      const previousUrl = editor.getAttributes('link').href;
      setLinkUrl(previousUrl || '');
      setShowLinkInput(true);
      setTimeout(() => linkInputRef.current?.focus(), 0);
    }
  }, [editor, showLinkInput, linkUrl]);

  if (!isOpen || !editor) return null;

            <Btn onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleItalic().run()} active={editor.isActive('italic')} title="РљСѓСЂСЃРёРІ"><i>I</i></Btn>
            <Btn onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleUnderline().run()} active={editor.isActive('underline')} title="РџРѕРґС‡РµСЂРєРЅСѓС‚С‹Р№"><u>U</u></Btn>
            <Btn onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleStrike().run()} active={editor.isActive('strike')} title="Р—Р°С‡РµСЂРєРЅСѓС‚С‹Р№"><s>S</s></Btn>

              <span className="rt-letter" style={{ borderBottomColor: currentColor }}>A</span>
            </button>
            {pop === 'color' && <ColorPickerPopover title="Р¦РІРµС‚ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°" colors={TEXT_COLORS.flat()} onPick={(c) => { editor.chain().focus().setColor(c).run(); setPop(null); }} onReset={() => { editor.chain().focus().unsetColor().run(); setPop(null); }} />}
          </div>

FILE: presentation\components\shapes\PolylineRenderer.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { Line, Circle } from 'react-konva';
import type { DashType } from '../../../shared/types';

export interface PolylineRendererProps {
  points: number[];
  stroke: string;
  strokeWidth: number;
  dash?: DashType;
  isArrow?: boolean;
  isSelected?: boolean;
  hitStrokeWidth?: number;
  handleRadius?: number;
  onPointMove?: (index: number, x: number, y: number) => void;
}

const DASH_MAP: Record<DashType, number[] | undefined> = {
  solid: undefined,
  dashed: [10, 6],
  dotted: [2, 5],
  dashdot: [10, 5, 2, 5],
  longdash: [20, 8],
};

export const PolylineRenderer: React.FC<PolylineRendererProps> = ({
  points, stroke, strokeWidth, dash = 'solid', isArrow = false, isSelected = false,
  hitStrokeWidth = 20, handleRadius = 6, onPointMove,
}) => {
  if (!points || points.length < 4) return null;

  const dashArr = DASH_MAP[dash];

  const arrowNode = (() => {
    if (!isArrow || points.length < 4) return null;
    const n = points.length;
    const x1 = points[n - 4], y1 = points[n - 3];
    const x2 = points[n - 2], y2 = points[n - 1];
    const angle = Math.atan2(y2 - y1, x2 - x1);
    const len = 14;
    const a1 = angle + Math.PI - 0.35;
    const a2 = angle + Math.PI + 0.35;
    return (
      <Line
        points={[
          x2 + len * Math.cos(a1), y2 + len * Math.sin(a1),
          x2, y2,
          x2 + len * Math.cos(a2), y2 + len * Math.sin(a2),
        ]}
        stroke={stroke}
        strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
        lineCap="round"
        lineJoin="round"
        listening={false}
      />
    );
  })();

  return (
    <>
      <Line points={points} stroke="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" strokeWidth={hitStrokeWidth} listening={true} />
      <Line points={points} stroke={stroke} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} dash={dashArr} lineCap="round" lineJoin="round" listening={false} />
      {arrowNode}
      {isSelected && Array.from({ length: points.length / 2 }).map((_, i) => (
        <Circle
          key={i}
          x={points[i * 2]} y={points[i * 2 + 1]}
          radius={handleRadius} fill="#fbbf24" stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth={1.5} draggable
          onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; }}
          onDragMove={(e) => {
            e.cancelBubble = true;
            onPointMove?.(i, e.target.x(), e.target.y());
          }}
        />
      ))}
    </>
  );
};

FILE: presentation\components\SlidesBar.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../shared/store/store';
import { useDocumentStore, useSelectionStore } from '../providers/EditorProvider';
import { Slide as SlideClass } from '../../domain/entities/Slide';

export const SlidesBar: React.FC = () => {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const selStore = useSelectionStore();
  const slides = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.slides);
  const activeId = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.activeId);

  const addSlide = () => {
    const current = slides.find((s) => s.id === activeId);
    const next = new SlideClass({ name: `РЎР»Р°Р№Рґ ${slides.length + 1}`, format: current?.format ?? 'a4', orientation: current?.orientation ?? 'portrait' }).toPlainObject();
    docStore.commit((draft) => { draft.slides.push(next); draft.activeId = next.id; });
    selStore.clear();
  };

  const removeSlide = (id: string) => {
    if (slides.length <= 1) return;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const i = draft.slides.findIndex((s) => s.id === id);
      if (i < 0) return;
      draft.slides.splice(i, 1);
      if (draft.activeId === id) draft.activeId = draft.slides[0].id;
    });
    selStore.clear();
  };

  const setActive = (id: string) => {
    docStore.setState((s) => ({ ...s, activeId: id }));
    selStore.clear();
  };

  return (
    <footer className="slides-panel">
      {slides.map((s, i) => (
        <div key={s.id} className={`slide-thumb ${s.id === activeId ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setActive(s.id)}>
          {i + 1}
          {slides.length > 1 && <button onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); removeSlide(s.id); }} style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 2, right: 4, border: 'none', background: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', color: '#94a3b8' }}>Г—</button>}
        </div>
      ))}
      <button className="add-slide" onClick={addSlide}>+ РЎР»Р°Р№Рґ</button>
    </footer>
  );
};

FILE: presentation\components\StampEditor.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, useMemo } from 'react';
import { Stage, Layer, Rect, Circle, Ellipse, Line, Text, Shape, Image as KonvaImage, Transformer } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { StampElement, StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '../../shared/types';
import { generateId } from '../../shared/utils/id';
import { useStampFillMode } from '../hooks/useStampFillMode';
import { CompositeFillLayer } from './stamps/CompositeFillLayer';
import { STAMP_DASH_MAP, konvaStyle } from '../../lib/stamps/render';

interface StampEditorProps {
  stamp: StampElement;
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (stamp: StampElement) => void;
}

// ==========================================
// Р’РЎРџРћРњРћР“РђРўР•Р›Р¬РќР«Р• Р¤РЈРќРљР¦РР Р РљРћРњРџРћРќР•РќРўР«
// ==========================================

// РћС‚СЂРёСЃРѕРІРєР° С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р° РїРѕ РґСѓРіРµ
const ArcText = ({ item }: { item: StampTextItem }) => {
  return (
    <Shape
      sceneFunc={(ctx) => {
        const c = (ctx as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
        const fontParts: string[] = [];
        if (item.fontStyle.includes('italic')) fontParts.push('italic');
        if (item.fontStyle.includes('bold')) fontParts.push('bold');
        fontParts.push(`${item.fontSize}px`, item.fontFamily || 'Arial');
        c.font = fontParts.join(' ');
        c.fillStyle = item.fill;
        c.textAlign = 'center';
        c.textBaseline = 'middle';

        const chars = Array.from(item.text);
        const start = (item.arcStartDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
        const end = (item.arcEndDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
        const step = chars.length > 1 ? (end - start) / (chars.length - 1) : 0;

        chars.forEach((ch, i) => {
          const angle = start + i * step;
          const x = item.arcCx + Math.cos(angle) * item.arcRadius;
          const y = item.arcCy + Math.sin(angle) * item.arcRadius;
          
          c.save();
          c.translate(x, y);
          c.rotate(angle + Math.PI / 2); // РџРѕРІРѕСЂРѕС‚ Р±СѓРєРІС‹
          c.fillText(ch, 0, 0);
          c.restore();
        });
      }}
    />
  );
};

// РћС‚СЂРёСЃРѕРІРєР° С„РёРіСѓСЂ
const ShapeNode = ({ shape, onSelect, draggable }: any) => {
  const common = {
    stroke: shape.stroke,
    strokeWidth: shape.strokeWidth,
    dash: STAMP_DASH_MAP[shape.dash],
    fill: shape.fill || 'transparent',
    listening: true,
    draggable,
    onClick: onSelect,
  };
  const cx = shape.x + shape.width / 2;
  const cy = shape.y + shape.height / 2;

  if (shape.kind === 'circle') return <Circle id={shape.id} x={cx} y={cy} radius={Math.min(shape.width, shape.height) / 2} {...common} />;
  if (shape.kind === 'ellipse') return <Ellipse id={shape.id} x={cx} y={cy} radiusX={shape.width / 2} radiusY={shape.height / 2} {...common} />;
  if (shape.kind === 'rect') return <Rect id={shape.id} x={shape.x} y={shape.y} width={shape.width} height={shape.height} cornerRadius={shape.cornerRadius || 0} {...common} />;
  if (shape.kind === 'triangle') return <Line id={shape.id} points={[cx, shape.y, shape.x + shape.width, shape.y + shape.height, shape.x, shape.y + shape.height]} closed {...common} />;
  if (shape.kind === 'line') return <Line id={shape.id} x={shape.x} y={shape.y} points={shape.points || [0, shape.height/2, shape.width, shape.height/2]} {...common} />;
  return null;
};

// ==========================================
// РћРЎРќРћР’РќРћР™ РљРћРњРџРћРќР•РќРў
// ==========================================

export const StampEditor: React.FC<StampEditorProps> = ({ stamp, isOpen, onClose, onSave }) => {
  const [shapes, setShapes] = useState<StampShapeItem[]>(stamp.shapes ?? []);
  const [texts, setTexts] = useState<StampTextItem[]>(stamp.texts ?? []);
  const [images, setImages] = useState<StampImageItem[]>(stamp.images ?? []);
  
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [selectedType, setSelectedType] = useState<'shape' | 'text' | 'image' | null>(null);
  
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
  const [pan, setPan] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const stageRef = useRef<Konva.Stage>(null);
  const trRef = useRef<Konva.Transformer>(null);

  // РРЅРёС†РёР°Р»РёР·Р°С†РёСЏ
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      setShapes(stamp.shapes ?? []);
      setTexts(stamp.texts ?? []);
      setImages(stamp.images ?? []);
      setSelectedId(null);
      setZoom(1);
      setPan({ x: 0, y: 0 });
    }
  }, [isOpen, stamp]);

  // РЎРёРЅС…СЂРѕРЅРёР·Р°С†РёСЏ С‚СЂР°РЅСЃС„РѕСЂРјРµСЂР°
  useEffect(() => {
    const tr = trRef.current, stage = stageRef.current;
    if (!tr || !stage) return;
    const isFill = shapes.find(s => s.id === selectedId)?.kind === 'fill';
    if (!selectedId || isFill) { tr.nodes([]); tr.getLayer()?.batchDraw(); return; }
    const node = stage.findOne('#' + selectedId);
    if (node) tr.nodes([node]);
    tr.getLayer()?.batchDraw();
  }, [selectedId, shapes, texts]);

  const handleWheel = (e: any) => {
    e.evt.preventDefault();
    const dir = e.evt.deltaY > 0 ? -1 : 1;
    setZoom(z => Math.max(0.4, Math.min(6, z + dir * 0.1)));
  };

  // Р”РѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚РѕРІ
  const addShape = (kind: StampShapeItem['kind']) => {
    const s: StampShapeItem = {
      id: generateId(), kind, x: 50, y: 50, width: 100, height: 100,
      cornerRadius: kind === 'rect' ? 8 : 0, stroke: '#000', strokeWidth: 2, dash: 'solid',
    };
    setShapes(p => [...p, s]);
    setSelectedId(s.id); setSelectedType('shape');
  };

  const addText = (mode: 'straight' | 'arc') => {
    const t: StampTextItem = {
      id: generateId(), text: mode === 'arc' ? 'РўР•РљРЎРў РџРћ Р”РЈР“Р•' : 'РќРѕРІС‹Р№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚',
      mode, x: 50, y: 50, width: 150, align: 'center',
      arcCx: stamp.width / 2, arcCy: stamp.height / 2, arcRadius: 80,
      arcStartDeg: -90, arcEndDeg: 90, arcFlip: false,
      fontSize: 16, fontFamily: 'Arial', fontStyle: 'bold', textDecoration: '', fill: '#000',
    };
    setTexts(p => [...p, t]);
    setSelectedId(t.id); setSelectedType('text');
  };

  const fillApi = useStampFillMode({
    shapes, texts, images, width: stamp.width, height: stamp.height,
    commit: (s, t, i) => { setShapes(s); setTexts(t); setImages(i); },
    select: (id) => { setSelectedId(id); setSelectedType('shape'); },
  });

  const selectedShape = selectedType === 'shape' ? shapes.find(s => s.id === selectedId) : null;
  const selectedText = selectedType === 'text' ? texts.find(t => t.id === selectedId) : null;

  const updateShape = (patch: Partial<StampShapeItem>) => { if (selectedId) setShapes(p => p.map(s => s.id === selectedId ? { ...s, ...patch } : s)); };
  const updateText = (patch: Partial<StampTextItem>) => { if (selectedId) setTexts(p => p.map(t => t.id === selectedId ? { ...t, ...patch } : t)); };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="stamp-editor-backdrop">
      <div className="stamp-editor-modal">
        {/* HEADER */}
        <div className="stamp-editor-head">
          <input type="text" defaultValue="Custom Stamp" />
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
            <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={onClose}>РћС‚РјРµРЅР°</button>
            <button className="stamp-btn primary" onClick={() => { onSave({ ...stamp, shapes, texts, images }); onClose(); }}>РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ</button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* TOOLBAR */}
        <div className="stamp-toolbar">
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addShape('circle')}>в—Ї РљСЂСѓРі</button>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addShape('ellipse')}>в¬­ Р­Р»Р»РёРїСЃ</button>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addShape('rect')}>в–­ РџСЂСЏРјРѕСѓРі.</button>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addShape('triangle')}>в–і РўСЂРµСѓРі.</button>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addShape('line')}>в•± Р›РёРЅРёСЏ</button>
          <span style={{ borderLeft: '1px solid #cbd5e1', height: 20, margin: '0 8px' }} />
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addText('straight')}>T РўРµРєСЃС‚</button>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addText('arc')}>в€ РўРµРєСЃС‚ РїРѕ РґСѓРіРµ</button>
          <span style={{ borderLeft: '1px solid #cbd5e1', height: 20, margin: '0 8px' }} />
          <button className={`stamp-btn ${fillApi.fillMode ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => fillApi.setFillMode(!fillApi.fillMode)}>рџ–Њ Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР°</button>
          <span style={{ borderLeft: '1px solid #cbd5e1', height: 20, margin: '0 8px' }} />
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => setZoom(z => z + 0.2)}>+</button>
          <span style={{ fontSize: 12, width: 40, textAlign: 'center' }}>{Math.round(zoom * 100)}%</span>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => setZoom(z => Math.max(0.4, z - 0.2))}>-</button>
        </div>

        {/* BODY */}
        <div className="stamp-editor-body">
          {/* LEFT: LAYERS */}
          <div className="stamp-editor-left">
            <div className="stamp-layers-head">РЎР»РѕРё</div>
            <div className="stamp-layers-list">
              {shapes.map(s => (
                <div key={s.id} className={`stamp-layer ${selectedId === s.id ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => { setSelectedId(s.id); setSelectedType('shape'); }}>
                  <span className="stamp-layer-icon">в—Ї</span>
                  <span className="stamp-layer-name">{s.name || s.kind}</span>
                  <button className="stamp-layer-btn" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); setShapes(p => p.filter(x => x.id !== s.id)); }}>вњ•</button>
                </div>
              ))}
              {texts.map(t => (
                <div key={t.id} className={`stamp-layer ${selectedId === t.id ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => { setSelectedId(t.id); setSelectedType('text'); }}>
                  <span className="stamp-layer-icon">T</span>
                  <span className="stamp-layer-name">{t.text}</span>
                  <button className="stamp-layer-btn" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); setTexts(p => p.filter(x => x.id !== t.id)); }}>вњ•</button>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* CENTER: CANVAS */}
          <div className="stamp-editor-canvas">
            <Stage
              ref={stageRef} width={stamp.width * zoom} height={stamp.height * zoom}
              scaleX={zoom} scaleY={zoom} x={pan.x} y={pan.y}
              onWheel={handleWheel}
              onMouseDown={(e) => {
                if (fillApi.fillMode && e.target === e.target.getStage()) {
                  const pos = e.target.getStage()?.getRelativePointerPosition();
                  if (pos) fillApi.handleCanvasClick(pos.x, pos.y);
                } else if (e.target === e.target.getStage()) {
                  setSelectedId(null);
                }
              }}
              style={{ background: '#fff', boxShadow: '0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.1)' }}
            >
              <Layer>
                <Rect width={stamp.width} height={stamp.height} fill="#fff" listening={false} />
                <CompositeFillLayer width={stamp.width} height={stamp.height} shapes={shapes} />
                
                {shapes.filter(s => s.kind !== 'fill').map(s => (
                  <ShapeNode key={s.id} shape={s} draggable={!fillApi.fillMode} onSelect={() => { setSelectedId(s.id); setSelectedType('shape'); }} />
                ))}
                
                {texts.map(t => t.mode === 'straight' ? (
                  <Text key={t.id} id={t.id} x={t.x} y={t.y} width={t.width} text={t.text} fontSize={t.fontSize} fontFamily={t.fontFamily} fontStyle={konvaStyle(t.fontStyle)} fill={t.fill} align={t.align} draggable={!fillApi.fillMode} onClick={() => { setSelectedId(t.id); setSelectedType('text'); }} />
                ) : (
                  <React.Fragment key={t.id}>
                    <ArcText item={t} />
                    <Circle x={t.arcCx} y={t.arcCy} radius={5} fill="rgba(255,0,0,0.2)" draggable={!fillApi.fillMode} onClick={() => { setSelectedId(t.id); setSelectedType('text'); }} onDragMove={(e) => updateText({ arcCx: e.target.x(), arcCy: e.target.y() })} />
                  </React.Fragment>
                ))}

                {!fillApi.fillMode && <Transformer ref={trRef} rotateEnabled keepRatio={false} anchorSize={8} />}
              </Layer>
            </Stage>
          </div>

          {/* RIGHT: PROPERTIES */}
          <div className="stamp-editor-right">
            {selectedShape && (
              <>
                <h4>РЎРІРѕР№СЃС‚РІР° С„РёРіСѓСЂС‹</h4>
                <label>РћР±РІРѕРґРєР° <input type="color" value={selectedShape.stroke} onChange={(e) => updateShape({ stroke: e.target.value })} /></label>
                <label>РўРѕР»С‰РёРЅР° <input type="number" value={selectedShape.strokeWidth} onChange={(e) => updateShape({ strokeWidth: +e.target.value })} /></label>
                <label>Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР° <input type="color" value={selectedShape.fill || '#ffffff'} onChange={(e) => updateShape({ fill: e.target.value })} /></label>
                {selectedShape.kind === 'fill' && <p style={{ fontSize: 11, color: '#666' }}>Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР° РїСЂРёРІСЏР·Р°РЅР° Рє С„РёРіСѓСЂРµ</p>}
              </>
            )}
            {selectedText && (
              <>
                <h4>РЎРІРѕР№СЃС‚РІР° С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°</h4>
                <label>РЎРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјРѕРµ <textarea value={selectedText.text} onChange={(e) => updateText({ text: e.target.value })} rows={3} /></label>
                <label>Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ <input type="number" value={selectedText.fontSize} onChange={(e) => updateText({ fontSize: +e.target.value })} /></label>
                <label>Р¦РІРµС‚ <input type="color" value={selectedText.fill} onChange={(e) => updateText({ fill: e.target.value })} /></label>
                
                {selectedText.mode === 'arc' && (
                  <>
                    <h4 style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>РќР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєРё РґСѓРіРё</h4>
                    <label>Р Р°РґРёСѓСЃ <input type="number" value={selectedText.arcRadius} onChange={(e) => updateText({ arcRadius: +e.target.value })} /></label>
                    <label>РЈРіРѕР» (РіСЂР°РґСѓСЃС‹) <input type="number" value={selectedText.arcStartDeg} onChange={(e) => updateText({ arcStartDeg: +e.target.value, arcEndDeg: +e.target.value + 180 })} /></label>
                  </>
                )}
              </>
            )}
            {!selectedShape && !selectedText && (
              <p style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 13, textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 40 }}>Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚ РЅР° С…РѕР»СЃС‚Рµ</p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
