import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import type { StampElement } from '../../shared/types';
import {
  getAllStamps,
  addCustomStamp,
  updateCustomStamp,
  removeCustomStamp,
  isBuiltinStamp,
  forkStampAsCustom,
  type StampTemplate,
} from '../../lib/stamps/library';
import { newStampFromTemplate } from '../../lib/stamps/factories';
import { generateId } from '../../shared/utils/id';
import { useDocumentStore, useSelectionStore } from '../providers/EditorProvider';
import { StampEditor } from './StampEditor';

interface EditingState {
  mode: 'create' | 'edit';
  sourceId?: string;
  sourceIsBuiltin?: boolean;
  stamp: StampElement;
}

export const StampLibraryPanel: React.FC = () => {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const selStore = useSelectionStore();
  const [version, setVersion] = useState(0);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<EditingState | null>(null);

  const stamps = useMemo(() => getAllStamps(), [version]);

  const addElement = (el: StampElement) => {
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (!s) return;
      (s as any).elements = [...(s.elements as any), el];
    }, 'add-stamp');
    selStore.select(el.id);
  };

  const insertTemplate = (tpl: StampTemplate) =>
    addElement(newStampFromTemplate(tpl, 200, 200));

  const openCreate = () => {
    setEditing({
      mode: 'create',
      stamp: {
        id: generateId(),
        type: 'stamp',
        x: 200,
        y: 200,
        width: 200,
        height: 200,
        rotation: 0,
        shapes: [],
        texts: [],
        images: [],
      },
    });
  };

  const openEdit = (tpl: StampTemplate) => {
    const builtin = isBuiltinStamp(tpl.id);
    setEditing({
      mode: 'edit',
      sourceId: tpl.id,
      sourceIsBuiltin: builtin,
      stamp: {
        id: generateId(),
        type: 'stamp',
        x: 200,
        y: 200,
        width: tpl.width,
        height: tpl.height,
        rotation: 0,
        shapes: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tpl.shapes)),
        texts: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tpl.texts)),
        images: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tpl.images || [])),
      },
    });
  };

  /* В§F-SLIB-05..08 */
  const handleSave = (saved: StampElement) => {
    if (!editing) return;
    const shapes = saved.shapes ?? [];
    const texts = saved.texts ?? [];
    const images = saved.images ?? [];

    if (editing.mode === 'create') {
      const tpl: StampTemplate = {
        id: `custom-${Date.now()}`,
        name: saved.name || 'РњРѕР№ С€С‚Р°РјРї',
        width: saved.width,
        height: saved.height,
        shapes: [...shapes],
        texts: [...texts],
        images: [...images],
        custom: true,
      };
      addCustomStamp(tpl);
      addElement({ ...newStampFromTemplate(tpl, 200, 200) });
    } else if (editing.sourceIsBuiltin) {
      /* В§F-SLIB-08: builtin РЅРµР»СЊР·СЏ РїРµСЂРµР·Р°РїРёСЃР°С‚СЊ вЂ” С„РѕСЂРєР°РµРј РІ custom. */
      const source = stamps.find((s) => s.id === editing.sourceId);
      if (source) {
        const copy = forkStampAsCustom({
          ...source,
          width: saved.width,
          height: saved.height,
          shapes: [...shapes],
          texts: [...texts],
          images: [...images],
        });
        addCustomStamp(copy);
        addElement({ ...newStampFromTemplate(copy, 200, 200) });
      }
    } else if (editing.sourceId) {
      /* В§F-SLIB-07: РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёРµ СЃСѓС‰РµСЃС‚РІСѓСЋС‰РµРіРѕ custom С€С‚Р°РјРїР°. */
      updateCustomStamp(editing.sourceId, {
        width: saved.width,
        height: saved.height,
        shapes: [...shapes],
        texts: [...texts],
        images: [...images],
      });
    }

    setVersion((v) => v + 1);
    setEditing(null);
  };

  const deleteStamp = (tpl: StampTemplate) => {
    if (!tpl.custom) return;
    if (!window.confirm(`РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ С€С‚Р°РјРї В«${tpl.name}В» РёР· Р±РёР±Р»РёРѕС‚РµРєРё?`)) return;
    removeCustomStamp(tpl.id);
    setVersion((v) => v + 1);
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="tool-btn"
        onClick={openCreate}
        style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}
      >
        + РЎРѕР·РґР°С‚СЊ С€С‚Р°РјРївЂ¦
      </button>

      <div className="tool-grid">
        {stamps.map((s) => (
          <div key={s.id} style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="tool-tile"
              title={s.description || s.name}
              onClick={() => insertTemplate(s)}
              style={{ width: '100%' }}
            >
              <span className="tile-ico">{s.icon || 'рџ–ј'}</span>
              <span>{s.name}</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              title="Р РµРґР°РєС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ"
              onClick={(e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                openEdit(s);
              }}
              style={{
                position: 'absolute',
                top: 2,
                right: s.custom ? 22 : 2,
                width: 18,
                height: 18,
                padding: 0,
                fontSize: 10,
                lineHeight: '16px',
                border: '1px solid var(--border)',
                borderRadius: 3,
                background: 'var(--panel)',
                color: 'var(--text)',
                cursor: 'pointer',
              }}
            >
              вњЋ
            </button>

            {s.custom && (
              <button
                type="button"
                title="РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ РёР· Р±РёР±Р»РёРѕС‚РµРєРё"
                onClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  deleteStamp(s);
                }}
                style={{
                  position: 'absolute',
                  top: 2,
                  right: 2,
                  width: 18,
                  height: 18,
                  padding: 0,
                  fontSize: 11,
                  lineHeight: '16px',
                  border: '1px solid var(--border)',
                  borderRadius: 3,
                  background: 'var(--panel)',
                  color: '#dc2626',
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                }}
              >
                Г—
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {editing && (
        <StampEditor
          stamp={editing.stamp}
          isOpen
          onClose={() => setEditing(null)}
          onSave={handleSave}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

/* ============================================================
   presentation/components/stamps/CompositeFillLayer.tsx
   РћС‚РѕР±СЂР°Р¶Р°РµС‚ СЂР°СЃС‚СЂ anchor-Р·Р°Р»РёРІРѕРє. Р›РѕРіРёРєР° вЂ” РІ FillRasterizer.
   ============================================================ */

import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { Image as KImage } from 'react-konva';
import type { StampShapeItem } from '../../../shared/types';
import type { FillSpec } from '../../../lib/stamps/rasterFill';
import { useFillRasterizer } from '../../hooks/useFillRasterizer';

interface Props {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  enabled?: boolean;
}

function anchorToWorld(
  target: StampShapeItem,
  local: { x: number; y: number },
): { x: number; y: number } {
  const lx = target.x + local.x * target.width;
  const ly = target.y + local.y * target.height;
  const cx = target.x + target.width / 2;
  const cy = target.y + target.height / 2;
  const rot = ((target.rotation || 0) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const dx = lx - cx;
  const dy = ly - cy;
  return {
    x: cx + dx * Math.cos(rot) - dy * Math.sin(rot),
    y: cy + dx * Math.sin(rot) + dy * Math.cos(rot),
  };
}

const CompositeFillLayerImpl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  width,
  height,
  shapes,
  enabled = true,
}) => {
  const fills: FillSpec[] = useMemo(() => {
    const out: FillSpec[] = [];
    for (const s of shapes) {
      if (s.kind !== 'fill') continue;
      if (s.hidden) continue;
      if (!s.fillTargetShapeId) continue;

      const target = shapes.find((t) => t.id === s.fillTargetShapeId);
      if (!target) continue;

      const local = s.fillAnchorLocal ?? { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 };
      const { x, y } = anchorToWorld(target, local);

      out.push({ px: x, py: y, color: s.fill || '#fbbf24', target });
    }
    return out;
  }, [shapes]);

  const signature = useMemo(() => {
    const geo = shapes
      .filter((s) => s.kind !== 'fill' && !s.hidden)
      .map((s) =>
        `${s.kind}:${Math.round(s.x)}|${Math.round(s.y)}|${Math.round(s.width)}|${Math.round(s.height)}|${Math.round(s.rotation || 0)}|${(s.points || []).map((v) => Math.round(v)).join(',')}|${s.strokeWidth}`,
      )
      .join('||');
    const f = fills
      .map((x) => `${Math.round(x.px)},${Math.round(x.py)},${x.color},${x.target?.id ?? '-'}`)
      .join(';');
    return geo + '###' + f;
  }, [shapes, fills]);

  const entry = useFillRasterizer({
    key: signature,
    width,
    height,
    shapes,
    fills,
    enabled,
  });

  if (!enabled || !fills.length) return null;
  if (!entry || !entry.image) return null;

  return (
    <KImage
      x={0}
      y={0}
      width={width}
      height={height}
      image={entry.image}
      listening={false}
    />
  );
};

export const CompositeFillLayer = React.memo(CompositeFillLayerImpl);

import React from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../shared/store/store';
import { useDocumentStore } from '../providers/EditorProvider';

export const Toolbar: React.FC = () => {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const slides = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.slides);
  const data = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.data);

  const handleExport = () => {
    const payload = { version: '2.0', exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(), slides, data };
    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url; a.download = `ticket-editor-${Date.now()}.json`; a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const handleImport = () => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file'; input.accept = '.json';
    input.onchange = () => {
      const file = input.files?.[0]; if (!file) return;
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = () => {
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(reader.result as string);
          if (!Array.isArray(parsed.slides)) throw new Error('РќРµРІРµСЂРЅС‹Р№ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚');
          docStore.commit((draft) => {
            draft.slides = parsed.slides;
            draft.activeId = parsed.slides[0].id;
            draft.data = parsed.data || [];
          }, 'import-json');
        } catch (err) {
          alert('РћС€РёР±РєР° РёРјРїРѕСЂС‚Р°: ' + (err as Error).message);
        }
      };
      reader.readAsText(file);
    };
    input.click();
  };

  return (
    <header className="toolbar">
      <h1>Р РµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ С€Р°Р±Р»РѕРЅРѕРІ</h1>
      <div className="toolbar-actions">
        <button className="toolbar-btn" onClick={() => docStore.undo()} disabled={!docStore.canUndo}>в†¶ РћС‚РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ</button>
        <button className="toolbar-btn" onClick={() => docStore.redo()} disabled={!docStore.canRedo}>в†· РџРѕРІС‚РѕСЂРёС‚СЊ</button>
        <div style={{ width: 16 }} />
        <button className="toolbar-btn" onClick={handleImport}>рџ“Ґ РРјРїРѕСЂС‚</button>
        <button className="toolbar-btn primary" onClick={handleExport}>рџ“¤ Р­РєСЃРїРѕСЂС‚</button>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
};

}

    <button onClick={() => onChange(Math.max(0.2, +(zoom - 0.1).toFixed(2)))}>в€’</button>

    <button onClick={() => onChange(Math.min(3, +(zoom + 0.1).toFixed(2)))}>+</button>

/**
 * Anchor Overlay Hook - Presentation Layer
 * Handle anchor points for connector creation
 */

import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';

export interface AnchorPoint {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  position: 'top' | 'right' | 'bottom' | 'left';
  elementId: string;
}

interface UseAnchorOverlayReturn {
  hoveredElementId: string | null;
  anchors: AnchorPoint[];
  showAnchors: (elementId: string, anchors: AnchorPoint[]) => void;
  hideAnchors: () => void;
  setHoveredElement: (id: string | null) => void;
}

export function useAnchorOverlay(): UseAnchorOverlayReturn {
  const [hoveredElementId, setHoveredElementId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [anchors, setAnchors] = useState<AnchorPoint[]>([]);

  const showAnchors = useCallback((elementId: string, newAnchors: AnchorPoint[]) => {
    setHoveredElementId(elementId);
    setAnchors(newAnchors);
  }, []);

  const hideAnchors = useCallback(() => {
    setHoveredElementId(null);
    setAnchors([]);
  }, []);

  const setHoveredElement = useCallback((id: string | null) => {
    setHoveredElementId(id);
    if (!id) {
      setAnchors([]);
    }
  }, []);

  return {
    hoveredElementId,
    anchors,
    showAnchors,
    hideAnchors,
    setHoveredElement,
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   presentation/hooks/useAutoFitZoom.ts
   ============================================================ */

import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

interface Args {
  wrapRef: React.RefObject<HTMLElement>;
  slideWidth: number;
  slideHeight: number;
  setZoom: (z: number) => void;
  padding?: number;
  topReserve?: number;
  minZoom?: number;
  maxZoom?: number;
}

export function useAutoFitZoom({
  wrapRef, slideWidth, slideHeight, setZoom,
  padding = 40, topReserve = 80, minZoom = 0.2, maxZoom = 2,
}: Args) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const wrap = wrapRef.current;
    if (!wrap || !slideWidth || !slideHeight) return;

    const fit = () => {
      const rect = wrap.getBoundingClientRect();
      // РћС‚РЅРёРјР°РµРј РїР°РґРґРёРЅРіРё Рё СЂРµР·РµСЂРІ РїРѕРґ ZoomBar
      const availableW = Math.max(100, rect.width - padding * 2);
      const availableH = Math.max(100, rect.height - padding * 2 - topReserve);

      const zoomX = availableW / slideWidth;
      const zoomY = availableH / slideHeight;
      const next = Math.max(minZoom, Math.min(maxZoom, Math.min(zoomX, zoomY)));

      setZoom(+next.toFixed(3));
    };

    fit();

    let raf = 0;
    const onResize = () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(fit);
    };

    window.addEventListener('resize', onResize);
    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
    };
  }, [wrapRef, slideWidth, slideHeight, setZoom, padding, topReserve, minZoom, maxZoom]);
}

/**
 * Auto Save Hook - Presentation Layer
 * Automatic saving to localStorage
 */

import { useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import type { Slide } from '../../domain/entities/Slide';
import type { DataRoot } from '../../shared/types';

const AUTO_SAVE_KEY = 'ticket-editor:autosave';
const AUTO_SAVE_INTERVAL_MS = 30000; // 30 seconds
const MAX_AGE_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 24 hours

interface AutoSaveData {
  slides: Slide[];
  data: DataRoot[];
  activeSlideId: string | null;
  savedAt: string;
}

export function useAutoSave(
  slides: Slide[],
  data: DataRoot[],
  activeSlideId: string | null
): void {
  // Auto save every 30 seconds
  useEffect(() => {
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      const saveData: AutoSaveData = {
        slides,
        data,
        activeSlideId,
        savedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      };
      localStorage.setItem(AUTO_SAVE_KEY, JSON.stringify(saveData));
    }, AUTO_SAVE_INTERVAL_MS);

    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [slides, data, activeSlideId]);
}

export function loadAutoSave(): AutoSaveData | null {
  try {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(AUTO_SAVE_KEY);
    if (!saved) return null;

    const data: AutoSaveData = JSON.parse(saved);
    const savedAt = new Date(data.savedAt);
    const now = new Date();

    // Check if older than 24 hours
    if (now.getTime() - savedAt.getTime() > MAX_AGE_MS) {
      clearAutoSave();
      return null;
    }

    return data;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function clearAutoSave(): void {
  localStorage.removeItem(AUTO_SAVE_KEY);
}

export function hasAutoSave(): boolean {
  return localStorage.getItem(AUTO_SAVE_KEY) !== null;
}

FILE: presentation\hooks\useConnectors.ts

/**
 * Connectors Hook - Presentation Layer
 * Handle connector creation and management
 */

import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import type { Slide } from '../../domain/entities/Slide';
import type { CanvasElement, ConnectorElement, AnchorSide } from '../../shared/types';
import { generateId } from '../../shared/utils/id';

export interface ConnectorDraft {
  fromId: string;
  fromAnchor: AnchorSide;
  toX: number;
  toY: number;
  hoverTargetId?: string;
  hoverAnchor?: AnchorSide;
}

interface UseConnectorsOptions {
  slide: Slide | null;
  addElement: (element: CanvasElement) => void;
  removeElement: (id: string) => void;
}

export interface UseConnectorsReturn {
  draft: ConnectorDraft | null;
  startDraft: (fromId: string, fromAnchor: AnchorSide, x: number, y: number) => void;
  moveDraft: (x: number, y: number) => void;
  commitDraft: () => void;
  cancelDraft: () => void;
  findNearestAnchor: (x: number, y: number) => { elementId: string; anchor: AnchorSide } | null;
}

const ANCHOR_OFFSET = 8;

export function useConnectors(options: UseConnectorsOptions): UseConnectorsReturn {
  const { slide, addElement } = options;
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<ConnectorDraft | null>(null);

  const getAnchorPosition = useCallback((
    element: CanvasElement,
    anchor: AnchorSide
  ): { x: number; y: number } => {
    const cx = element.x + element.width / 2;
    const cy = element.y + element.height / 2;

    switch (anchor) {
      case 'top':
        return { x: cx, y: element.y - ANCHOR_OFFSET };
      case 'right':
        return { x: element.x + element.width + ANCHOR_OFFSET, y: cy };
      case 'bottom':
        return { x: cx, y: element.y + element.height + ANCHOR_OFFSET };
      case 'left':
        return { x: element.x - ANCHOR_OFFSET, y: cy };
    }
  }, []);

  const findNearestAnchor = useCallback((
    x: number,
    y: number
  ): { elementId: string; anchor: AnchorSide } | null => {
    if (!slide) return null;

    let nearest: { elementId: string; anchor: AnchorSide; dist: number } | null = null;

    for (const el of slide.elements) {
      // Skip connectors and fills
      if (el.type === 'connector' || el.type === 'fill') continue;

      const anchors: AnchorSide[] = ['top', 'right', 'bottom', 'left'];
      
      for (const anchor of anchors) {
        const pos = getAnchorPosition(el, anchor);
        const dist = Math.hypot(pos.x - x, pos.y - y);
        
        if (dist < 30 && (!nearest || dist < nearest.dist)) {
          nearest = { elementId: el.id, anchor, dist };
        }
      }
    }

    return nearest ? { elementId: nearest.elementId, anchor: nearest.anchor } : null;
  }, [slide, getAnchorPosition]);

  const startDraft = useCallback((
    fromId: string,
    fromAnchor: AnchorSide,
    x: number,
    y: number
  ) => {
    setDraft({
      fromId,
      fromAnchor,
      toX: x,
      toY: y,
    });
  }, []);

  const moveDraft = useCallback((x: number, y: number) => {
    if (!draft) return;

    const nearest = findNearestAnchor(x, y);
    
    setDraft({
      ...draft,
      toX: x,
      toY: y,
      hoverTargetId: nearest?.elementId,
      hoverAnchor: nearest?.anchor,
    });
  }, [draft, findNearestAnchor]);

  const commitDraft = useCallback(() => {
    if (!draft || !slide) return;

    // Don't create if no target
    if (!draft.hoverTargetId || !draft.hoverAnchor) {
      setDraft(null);
      return;
    }

    // Don't connect to self
    if (draft.fromId === draft.hoverTargetId) {
      setDraft(null);
      return;
    }

    const connector: ConnectorElement = {
      id: generateId(),
      type: 'connector',
      x: 0,
      y: 0,
      width: 0,
      height: 0,
      rotation: 0,
      fromId: draft.fromId,
      fromAnchor: draft.fromAnchor,
      toId: draft.hoverTargetId,
      toAnchor: draft.hoverAnchor,
      stroke: '#374151',
      strokeWidth: 2,
      dash: 'solid',
      isArrow: true,
      routing: 'straight',
    };

    addElement(connector);
    setDraft(null);
  }, [draft, slide, addElement]);

  const cancelDraft = useCallback(() => {
    setDraft(null);
  }, []);

  return {
    draft,
    startDraft,
    moveDraft,
    commitDraft,
    cancelDraft,
    findNearestAnchor,
  };
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: presentation\hooks\useEditorShortcuts.ts
SIZE: 8966 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore, DocumentState } from '../../domain/document/DocumentStore';
import type { SelectionStore } from '../stores/SelectionStore';
import type { ModalsStore } from '../stores/ModalsStore';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';
import { generateId } from '../../shared/utils/id';

interface Args {
  docStore: DocumentStore;
  selStore: SelectionStore;
  modalsStore: ModalsStore;
}

const isEditable = (el: EventTarget | null): boolean => {
  const h = el as HTMLElement | null;
  if (!h) return false;
  return (
    h.isContentEditable ||
    h.tagName === 'INPUT' ||
    h.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' ||
    h.tagName === 'SELECT'
  );
};

/**
 * Р“Р»РѕР±Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ С€РѕСЂС‚РєР°С‚С‹ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂР° (В§38).
 *
 * - РќРµ СЃСЂР°Р±Р°С‚С‹РІР°РµС‚ РїСЂРё РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚С‹С… РјРѕРґР°Р»РєР°С… (В§F-STMP-E-17, В§F-DG вЂ” СЃР°РјРё РѕР±СЂР°Р±Р°С‚С‹РІР°СЋС‚).
 * - РќРµ СЃСЂР°Р±Р°С‚С‹РІР°РµС‚, РєРѕРіРґР° С„РѕРєСѓСЃ РІ РїРѕР»Рµ РІРІРѕРґР° вЂ” РєСЂРѕРјРµ СЃР»СѓС‡Р°СЏ, РєРѕРіРґР° РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚
 *   inline-СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ (С‚РѕРіРґР° Escape РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ РµРіРѕ Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ вЂ” В§F-IE-07).
 * - MergeKey РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґР»СЏ continuous-update (`update-<id>`), С‡С‚РѕР±С‹
 *   РЅРµ СЃРєР»РµРёС‚СЊ РїРѕРґСЂСЏРґ РёРґСѓС‰РёРµ delete/paste/duplicate РІ РѕРґРЅСѓ Р·Р°РїРёСЃСЊ (В§I-13, В§I-14).
 */
export function useEditorShortcuts({ docStore, selStore, modalsStore }: Args) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const ms = modalsStore.getState();

      /* РњРѕРґР°Р»РєРё (StampEditor, DiagramEditor) СЃР°РјРё РІРµС€Р°СЋС‚ СЃРІРѕРё РѕР±СЂР°Р±РѕС‚С‡РёРєРё. */
      if (ms.stampEditorTarget || ms.diagramEditorTarget) return;

      /* Р¤РѕРєСѓСЃ РІ РїРѕР»Рµ РІРІРѕРґР° Рё inline-СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ РЅРµ Р°РєС‚РёРІРµРЅ вЂ” РЅРµ РјРµС€Р°РµРј. */
      if (isEditable(e.target) && !ms.editing) return;

      const ctrl = e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey;
      const code = e.code;

      /* -------- Escape (В§F-IE-07, В§F-FM-06) -------- */
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        if (ms.editing) {
          modalsStore.closeEditor();
          return;
        }
        if (ms.fillPoints.length) {
          modalsStore.setFillPoints([]);
          return;
        }
        selStore.clear();
        return;
      }

      /* -------- Enter: Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РїРѕР»РёРіРѕРЅ fill (В§F-FM-05) -------- */
      if (e.key === 'Enter' && !ms.editing && ms.fillPoints.length >= 6) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const pts = ms.fillPoints.slice();
        docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
          if (!s) return;
          const xs: number[] = [];
          const ys: number[] = [];
          for (let i = 0; i < pts.length; i += 2) {
            xs.push(pts[i]);
            ys.push(pts[i + 1]);
          }
          const minX = Math.min(...xs);
          const minY = Math.min(...ys);
          const maxX = Math.max(...xs);
          const maxY = Math.max(...ys);
          const fillEl: CanvasElement = {
            id: generateId(),
            type: 'fill',
            x: minX,
            y: minY,
            width: Math.max(1, maxX - minX),
            height: Math.max(1, maxY - minY),
            rotation: 0,
            points: pts.map((v, i) => (i % 2 === 0 ? v - minX : v - minY)),
            fill: ms.fillColor,
            stroke: '',
            strokeWidth: 0,
            opacity: 1,
          } as CanvasElement;
          s.elements.push(fillEl);
        });
        modalsStore.setFillPoints([]);
        return;
      }

      /* Р”Р°Р»СЊС€Рµ вЂ” С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РєРѕРіРґР° С„РѕРєСѓСЃ РЅРµ РІ РїРѕР»Рµ РІРІРѕРґР°. */
      if (isEditable(e.target)) return;

      /* -------- Delete / Backspace (В§38) -------- */
      if (e.key === 'Delete' || e.key === 'Backspace') {
        const ids = selStore.getState().selectedIds;
        if (!ids.length) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
          if (!s) return;
          s.elements = s.elements.filter((el) => !ids.includes(el.id));
        });
        selStore.clear();
        return;
      }

      /* -------- Undo / Redo (В§38) -------- */
      if (ctrl && code === 'KeyZ' && !e.shiftKey) {
        e.preventDefault();
        docStore.undo();
        return;
      }
      if (ctrl && (code === 'KeyY' || (code === 'KeyZ' && e.shiftKey))) {
        e.preventDefault();
        docStore.redo();
        return;
      }

      /* -------- Copy / Cut / Paste / Duplicate (В§38) -------- */
      if (ctrl && code === 'KeyC') {
        e.preventDefault();
        const state = docStore.getState();
        const slide = state.slides.find((x) => x.id === state.activeId);
        if (!slide) return;
        const ids = selStore.getState().selectedIds;
        const els = slide.elements.filter(
          (el) => ids.includes(el.id) && el.type !== 'connector',
        );
        if (els.length) modalsStore.copy(els);
        return;
      }

      if (ctrl && code === 'KeyX') {
        e.preventDefault();
        const state = docStore.getState();
        const slide = state.slides.find((x) => x.id === state.activeId);
        if (!slide) return;
        const ids = selStore.getState().selectedIds;
        const els = slide.elements.filter(
          (el) => ids.includes(el.id) && el.type !== 'connector',
        );
        if (!els.length) return;
        modalsStore.copy(els);
        docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
          if (!s) return;
          s.elements = s.elements.filter((el) => !ids.includes(el.id));
        });
        selStore.clear();
        return;
      }

      if (ctrl && code === 'KeyV') {
        e.preventDefault();
        const buf = modalsStore.getClipboard();
        if (!buf.length) return;
        const newIds: string[] = [];
        docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
          if (!s) return;
          for (const el of buf) {
            const cloned = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(el)) as CanvasElement;
            cloned.id = generateId();
            (cloned as any).x += 20;
            (cloned as any).y += 20;
            s.elements.push(cloned);
            newIds.push(cloned.id);
          }
        });
        selStore.setIds(newIds);
        return;
      }

      if (ctrl && code === 'KeyD') {
        e.preventDefault();
        const state = docStore.getState();
        const slide = state.slides.find((x) => x.id === state.activeId);
        if (!slide) return;
        const ids = selStore.getState().selectedIds;
        const els = slide.elements.filter(
          (el) => ids.includes(el.id) && el.type !== 'connector',
        );
        if (!els.length) return;
        const newIds: string[] = [];
        docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
          if (!s) return;
          for (const el of els) {
            const cloned = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(el)) as CanvasElement;
            cloned.id = generateId();
            (cloned as any).x += 20;
            (cloned as any).y += 20;
            s.elements.push(cloned);
            newIds.push(cloned.id);
          }
        });
        selStore.setIds(newIds);
        return;
      }

      /* -------- Copy / Apply style (В§38) -------- */
      if (ctrl && e.altKey && code === 'KeyC') {
        e.preventDefault();
        const state = docStore.getState();
        const slide = state.slides.find((x) => x.id === state.activeId);
        if (!slide) return;
        const selId = selStore.getState().selectedIds[0];
        const el = slide.elements.find((x) => x.id === selId);
        if (el) modalsStore.copyStyle(el);
        return;
      }
      if (ctrl && e.altKey && code === 'KeyV') {
        e.preventDefault();
        const style = modalsStore.getStyleClipboard();
        if (!style) return;
        const selId = selStore.getState().selectedIds[0];
        if (!selId) return;
        docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
          const el = s?.elements.find((x) => x.id === selId);
          if (el) Object.assign(el, style);
        });
        modalsStore.clearStyle();
        return;
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  }, [docStore, selStore, modalsStore]);
}

/**
 * Fill Mode Hook - Presentation Layer
 * Handles fill polygon creation and shape filling
 */

import { useState, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import type { Slide } from '../../domain/entities/Slide';
import type { CanvasElement, FillElement } from '../../shared/types';
import { generateId } from '../../shared/utils/id';

interface UseFillModeOptions {
  slide: Slide | null;
  addElement: (element: CanvasElement) => void;
  updateElement: (id: string, patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => void;
}

export interface UseFillModeReturn {
  isActive: boolean;
  color: string;
  points: readonly number[];
  activate: () => void;
  deactivate: () => void;
  setColor: (color: string) => void;
  handleClick: (pointerPos: { x: number; y: number }) => boolean;
  closePolygon: () => void;
  cancel: () => void;
  reset: () => void;
}

/**
 * Check if point hits a shape element
 */
function hitShapeElement(el: CanvasElement, px: number, py: number): boolean {
  // Shape element hit test
  if (el.type === 'shape') {
    if (px < el.x || py < el.y || px > el.x + el.width || py > el.y + el.height) {
      return false;
    }
    if (el.shape === 'circle') {
      const cx = el.x + el.width / 2;
      const cy = el.y + el.height / 2;
      const r = Math.min(el.width, el.height) / 2;
      return Math.hypot(px - cx, py - cy) <= r;
    }
    return true;
  }

  // Fill element hit test (point-in-polygon)
  if (el.type === 'fill' && el.points && el.points.length >= 6) {
    const pts = el.points;
    const n = pts.length / 2;
    let inside = false;

    for (let i = 0, j = n - 1; i < n; j = i++) {
      const xi = el.x + pts[i * 2];
      const yi = el.y + pts[i * 2 + 1];
      const xj = el.x + pts[j * 2];
      const yj = el.y + pts[j * 2 + 1];

      const intersect =
        yi > py !== yj > py &&
        px < ((xj - xi) * (py - yi)) / (yj - yi + 0.000001) + xi;

      if (intersect) inside = !inside;
    }
    return inside;
  }

  return false;
}

/**
 * Find shape element at position
 */
function findShapeAt(
  slide: Slide | null,
  x: number,
  y: number
): CanvasElement | null {
  if (!slide) return null;
  
  for (let i = slide.elements.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const el = slide.elements[i];
    if (hitShapeElement(el, x, y)) {
      return el;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export function useFillMode(options: UseFillModeOptions): UseFillModeReturn {
  const { slide, addElement, updateElement } = options;

  const [isActive, setIsActive] = useState(false);
  const [color, setColor] = useState('#fbbf24');
  const [points, setPoints] = useState<number[]>([]);

  const activate = useCallback(() => {
    setIsActive(true);
  }, []);

  const deactivate = useCallback(() => {
    setIsActive(false);
    setPoints([]);
  }, []);

  const cancel = useCallback(() => {
    setPoints([]);
  }, []);

  const reset = useCallback(() => {
    setIsActive(false);
    setPoints([]);
    setColor('#fbbf24');
  }, []);

  const handleClick = useCallback((pointerPos: { x: number; y: number }): boolean => {
    if (!isActive) return false;

    const target = findShapeAt(slide, pointerPos.x, pointerPos.y);

    // If clicked on shape/fill - fill it
    if (target && (target.type === 'shape' || target.type === 'fill')) {
      updateElement(target.id, { fill: color });
      return true;
    }

    // Otherwise add point to polygon
    setPoints((prev) => [...prev, pointerPos.x, pointerPos.y]);
    return true;
  }, [isActive, color, slide, updateElement]);

  const closePolygon = useCallback(() => {
    if (points.length < 6) {
      setPoints([]);
      return;
    }

    // Calculate bounding box
    const xs: number[] = [];
    const ys: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < points.length; i += 2) {
      xs.push(points[i]);
      ys.push(points[i + 1]);
    }

    const minX = Math.min(...xs);
    const minY = Math.min(...ys);
    const maxX = Math.max(...xs);
    const maxY = Math.max(...ys);

    // Convert to relative coordinates
    const relativePoints = points.map((v, i) =>
      i % 2 === 0 ? v - minX : v - minY
    );

    const fillElement: FillElement = {
      id: generateId(),
      type: 'fill',
      x: minX,
      y: minY,
      width: Math.max(1, maxX - minX),
      height: Math.max(1, maxY - minY),
      rotation: 0,
      points: relativePoints,
      fill: color,
      stroke: '',
      strokeWidth: 0,
      opacity: 1,
    };

    addElement(fillElement);
    setPoints([]);
  }, [points, color, addElement]);

  return {
    isActive,
    color,
    points,
    activate,
    deactivate,
    setColor,
    handleClick,
    closePolygon,
    cancel,
    reset,
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   presentation/hooks/useFillRasterizer.ts
   РўРѕРЅРєР°СЏ РѕР±С‘СЂС‚РєР° РЅР°Рґ singleton FillRasterizer:
   РїРѕРґРїРёСЃРєР° РЅР° СЂРµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚ + Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ РїСЂРё РјРѕРЅС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРё/СЃРјРµРЅРµ key.
   ============================================================ */

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem } from '../../shared/types';
import type { FillSpec } from '../../lib/stamps/rasterFill';
import {
  getFillRasterizer,
  type FillCacheEntry,
} from '../../lib/stamps/fillRasterizer';

export interface UseFillRasterizerArgs {
  key: string;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  fills: FillSpec[];
  enabled: boolean;
}

export function useFillRasterizer(args: UseFillRasterizerArgs): FillCacheEntry | null {
  const { key, width, height, shapes, fills, enabled } = args;

  const [entry, setEntry] = useState<FillCacheEntry | null>(
    () => getFillRasterizer().get(key) ?? null,
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const rasterizer = getFillRasterizer();
    const cached = rasterizer.get(key);
    if (cached) setEntry(cached);
    return rasterizer.subscribe((changedKey, changedEntry) => {
      if (changedKey === key) setEntry(changedEntry);
    });
  }, [key]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return;
    const rasterizer = getFillRasterizer();
    if (rasterizer.get(key)) return;
    if (rasterizer.isInflight(key)) return;
    rasterizer.request({ key, width, height, shapes, fills });
  }, [key, width, height, shapes, fills, enabled]);

  return entry;
}

/**
 * Group Drag Hook - Presentation Layer
 * Handle dragging multiple selected elements
 */

import { useState, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';

interface DragState {
  isDragging: boolean;
  startX: number;
  startY: number;
}

interface UseGroupDragReturn {
  dragState: DragState;
  startDrag: (x: number, y: number) => void;
  updateDrag: (x: number, y: number) => { dx: number; dy: number } | null;
  endDrag: () => void;
}

export function useGroupDrag(): UseGroupDragReturn {
  const [dragState, setDragState] = useState<DragState>({
    isDragging: false,
    startX: 0,
    startY: 0,
  });
  
  const lastPosRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number }>({ x: 0, y: 0 });

  const startDrag = useCallback((x: number, y: number) => {
    setDragState({
      isDragging: true,
      startX: x,
      startY: y,
    });
    lastPosRef.current = { x, y };
  }, []);

  const updateDrag = useCallback((x: number, y: number) => {
    if (!dragState.isDragging) return null;
    
    const dx = x - lastPosRef.current.x;
    const dy = y - lastPosRef.current.y;
    
    lastPosRef.current = { x, y };
    
    return { dx, dy };
  }, [dragState.isDragging]);

  const endDrag = useCallback(() => {
    setDragState({
      isDragging: false,
      startX: 0,
      startY: 0,
    });
  }, []);

  return {
    dragState,
    startDrag,
    updateDrag,
    endDrag,
  };
}

/**
 * History hook - Presentation Layer
 * Undo/redo functionality with Immer
 */

import { useState, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import { produce } from 'immer';
import type { Slide } from '../../domain/entities/Slide';
import { MAX_HISTORY } from '../../shared/constants';

export interface HistoryEntry {
  readonly slides: Slide[];
  readonly activeSlideId: string | null;
}

export interface UseHistoryReturn {
  canUndo: boolean;
  canRedo: boolean;
  push: (slides: Slide[], activeSlideId: string | null) => void;
  undo: () => HistoryEntry | null;
  redo: () => HistoryEntry | null;
  reset: () => void;
}

export function useHistory(): UseHistoryReturn {
  const [entries, setEntries] = useState<HistoryEntry[]>([]);
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(-1);
  const lastCommittedRef = useRef<Slide[] | null>(null);

  const push = useCallback(
    (slides: Slide[], activeSlideId: string | null) => {
      const last = lastCommittedRef.current;

      // Skip if same reference
      if (last === slides) return;

      // Structural sharing with Immer
      const snapshot = last
        ? produce(last, (draft) => {
            if (draft.length !== slides.length) {
              draft.length = slides.length;
            }
            for (let i = 0; i < slides.length; i++) {
              if (draft[i] !== slides[i]) draft[i] = slides[i];
            }
          })
        : slides;

      lastCommittedRef.current = snapshot;

      setEntries((prev) => {
        const trimmed = prev.slice(0, index + 1);
        trimmed.push({ slides: snapshot, activeSlideId });
        while (trimmed.length > MAX_HISTORY) trimmed.shift();
        return trimmed;
      });

      setIndex((prev) => {
        const next = prev + 1;
        return next >= MAX_HISTORY ? MAX_HISTORY - 1 : next;
      });
    },
    [index]
  );

  const undo = useCallback((): HistoryEntry | null => {
    if (index <= 0) return null;
    const prev = entries[index - 1];
    lastCommittedRef.current = prev.slides;
    setIndex(index - 1);
    return prev;
  }, [entries, index]);

  const redo = useCallback((): HistoryEntry | null => {
    if (index >= entries.length - 1) return null;
    const next = entries[index + 1];
    lastCommittedRef.current = next.slides;
    setIndex(index + 1);
    return next;
  }, [entries, index]);

  const reset = useCallback(() => {
    setEntries([]);
    setIndex(-1);
    lastCommittedRef.current = null;
  }, []);

  return {
    canUndo: index > 0,
    canRedo: index < entries.length - 1,
    push,
    undo,
    redo,
    reset,
  };
}

/**
 * Import/Export Hook - Presentation Layer
 * Handle file operations
 */

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { Slide } from '../../domain/entities/Slide';
import type { DataRoot } from '../../shared/types';

interface ExportData {
  version: string;
  slides: Slide[];
  data: DataRoot[];
}

export function useIO() {
  const exportJSON = useCallback((slides: Slide[], data: DataRoot[]) => {
    const exportData: ExportData = {
      version: '2.0',
      slides,
      data,
    };

    const json = JSON.stringify(exportData, null, 2);
    const blob = new Blob([json], { type: 'application/json' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = url;
    link.download = `ticket-editor-${Date.now()}.json`;
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }, []);

  const importJSON = useCallback((
    file: File,
    onSuccess: (slides: Slide[], data: DataRoot[]) => void,
    onError: (error: string) => void
  ) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    
    reader.onload = (e) => {
      try {
        const content = e.target?.result as string;
        const data = JSON.parse(content);
        
        // Validate structure
        if (!data.slides || !Array.isArray(data.slides)) {
          throw new Error('Invalid file format: missing slides');
        }
        
        // Migrate if needed
        const version = data.version || '1.0';
        const slides = migrateSlides(data.slides, version);
        const dataRoots = data.data || [];
        
        onSuccess(slides, dataRoots);
      } catch (err) {
        onError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to import file');
      }
    };
    
    reader.onerror = () => {
      onError('Failed to read file');
    };
    
    reader.readAsText(file);
  }, []);

  const exportSVG = useCallback((slide: Slide) => {
    // Generate SVG from slide
    const svg = generateSVG(slide);
    const blob = new Blob([svg], { type: 'image/svg+xml' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = url;
    link.download = `slide-${slide.name}-${Date.now()}.svg`;
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }, []);

  const exportServerSVG = useCallback(async (
    slides: Slide[],
    onSuccess: () => void,
    onError: (error: string) => void
  ) => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/export/svg', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ slides }),
      });
      
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error('Server export failed');
      }
      
      const blob = await response.blob();
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      
      const link = document.createElement('a');
      link.href = url;
      link.download = `export-${Date.now()}.svg`;
      document.body.appendChild(link);
      link.click();
      document.body.removeChild(link);
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      
      onSuccess();
    } catch (err) {
      onError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Export failed');
    }
  }, []);

  const exportServerPDF = useCallback(async (
    slides: Slide[],
    onSuccess: () => void,
    onError: (error: string) => void
  ) => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/export/pdf', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ slides }),
      });
      
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error('Server export failed');
      }
      
      const blob = await response.blob();
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      
      const link = document.createElement('a');
      link.href = url;
      link.download = `export-${Date.now()}.pdf`;
      document.body.appendChild(link);
      link.click();
      document.body.removeChild(link);
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      
      onSuccess();
    } catch (err) {
      onError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Export failed');
    }
  }, []);

  return {
    exportJSON,
    importJSON,
    exportSVG,
    exportServerSVG,
    exportServerPDF,
  };
}

// Migration helpers
function migrateSlides(slides: unknown[], version: string): Slide[] {
  // Simple migration - in real app would have complex logic
  if (version === '1.0') {
    // Migrate from v1.0 to v2.0
    return slides.map((slide: Record<string, unknown>) => ({
      ...slide,
      elements: (slide.elements as unknown[] || []).map((el: Record<string, unknown>) => ({
        ...el,
        rotation: el.rotation || 0,
      })),
    })) as Slide[];
  }
  
  return slides as Slide[];
}

// SVG generation
function generateSVG(slide: Slide): string {
  const elements = slide.elements.map((el) => {
    switch (el.type) {
      case 'rect':
      case 'shape':
        return `<rect x="${el.x}" y="${el.y}" width="${el.width}" height="${el.height}" fill="${(el as { fill?: string }).fill || 'none'}" />`;
      case 'text':
        return `<text x="${el.x}" y="${el.y + (el as { fontSize?: number }).fontSize || 16}">${(el as { text?: string }).text || ''}</text>`;
      default:
        return '';
    }
  }).join('\n');

  return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<svg width="${slide.width}" height="${slide.height}" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
  <rect width="100%" height="100%" fill="${slide.background}" />
  ${elements}
</svg>`;
}

/**
 * Rubber Band (Lasso) Hook - Presentation Layer
 * Handle rectangular selection on canvas
 */

import { useState, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';

interface Point { x: number; y: number }

interface RubberBandState {
  isActive: boolean;
  start: Point | null;
  current: Point | null;
}

interface UseRubberBandReturn {
  rubberBand: RubberBandState;
  handleMouseDown: (pos: Point) => void;
  handleMouseMove: (pos: Point) => void;
  handleMouseUp: () => { start: Point; end: Point } | null;
  reset: () => void;
}

export function useRubberBand(): UseRubberBandReturn {
  const [state, setState] = useState<RubberBandState>({
    isActive: false,
    start: null,
    current: null,
  });
  
  const startRef = useRef<Point | null>(null);

  const handleMouseDown = useCallback((pos: Point) => {
    startRef.current = pos;
    setState({
      isActive: true,
      start: pos,
      current: pos,
    });
  }, []);

  const handleMouseMove = useCallback((pos: Point) => {
    if (!state.isActive) return;
    setState(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      current: pos,
    }));
  }, [state.isActive]);

  const handleMouseUp = useCallback(() => {
    if (!state.isActive || !state.start || !state.current) {
      return null;
    }
    
    const result = {
      start: state.start,
      end: state.current,
    };
    
    setState({
      isActive: false,
      start: null,
      current: null,
    });
    startRef.current = null;
    
    return result;
  }, [state.isActive, state.start, state.current]);

  const reset = useCallback(() => {
    setState({
      isActive: false,
      start: null,
      current: null,
    });
    startRef.current = null;
  }, []);

  return {
    rubberBand: state,
    handleMouseDown,
    handleMouseMove,
    handleMouseUp,
    reset,
  };
}

import { useStore } from '../../shared/store/store';
import { useDocumentStore, useSelectionStore } from '../providers/EditorProvider';

export function useSelectedElement() {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const selStore = useSelectionStore();

  const activeId = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.activeId);
  const slides = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.slides);
  const selectedIds = useStore(selStore, (s) => s.selectedIds);

  if (selectedIds.length !== 1) return null;
  const slide = slides.find((s) => s.id === activeId);
  return slide?.elements.find((e) => e.id === selectedIds[0]) ?? null;
}

import { useState, useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';

export interface UseSelectionReturn {
  selectedIds: readonly string[];
  selectedId: string | null;
  selectedElements: CanvasElement[];
  isSelected: (id: string) => boolean;
  select: (id: string, multi?: boolean) => void;
  selectMany: (ids: string[]) => void;
  clearSelection: () => void;
  toggleSelection: (id: string) => void;
}

export function useSelection(elements: CanvasElement[]): UseSelectionReturn {
  const [selectedIds, setSelectedIds] = useState<string[]>([]);

  const selectedId = useMemo(() => selectedIds[0] ?? null, [selectedIds]);

  const selectedElements = useMemo(() => {
    return elements.filter((el) => selectedIds.includes(el.id));
  }, [elements, selectedIds]);

  const isSelected = useCallback(
    (id: string) => selectedIds.includes(id),
    [selectedIds],
  );

  const select = useCallback((id: string, multi = false) => {
    if (!id) {
      setSelectedIds([]);
      return;
    }
    setSelectedIds((prev) => {
      if (!multi) return [id];
      return prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter((x) => x !== id) : [...prev, id];
    });
  }, []);

  const selectMany = useCallback((ids: string[]) => {
    setSelectedIds(ids);
  }, []);

  const clearSelection = useCallback(() => {
    setSelectedIds([]);
  }, []);

  const toggleSelection = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setSelectedIds((prev) =>
      prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter((x) => x !== id) : [...prev, id],
    );
  }, []);

  return {
    selectedIds,
    selectedId,
    selectedElements,
    isSelected,
    select,
    selectMany,
    clearSelection,
    toggleSelection,
  };
}

/**
 * React hook for slides - Presentation Layer
 * Clean interface for components
 */

import { useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';
import { Slide } from '../../domain/entities/Slide';
import { slideStore, useSlideStore, useActiveSlide } from '../../infrastructure/store/SlideStoreImpl';
import { createSlideUseCases } from '../../application/use-cases/SlideUseCases';
import type { DocFormat, Orientation, CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';

const useCases = createSlideUseCases(slideStore);

export interface UseSlidesReturn {
  // State
  slides: Slide[];
  activeSlide: Slide | null;
  activeSlideId: string | null;

  // Actions
  addSlide: () => Slide;
  removeSlide: (id: string) => void;
  duplicateSlide: (id: string) => Slide | null;
  setActiveSlide: (id: string) => void;
  setSlides: (slides: Slide[]) => void;
  changeFormat: (format: DocFormat) => void;
  changeOrientation: (orientation: Orientation) => void;
  changeCustomSize: (width: number, height: number) => void;

  // Element actions
  addElement: (element: CanvasElement) => void;
  updateElement: (elementId: string, patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => void;
  removeElement: (elementId: string) => void;
  moveElementLayer: (elementId: string, direction: 1 | -1) => void;
}

export function useSlides(): UseSlidesReturn {
  const state = useSlideStore();
  const activeSlide = useActiveSlide();

  const slides = useMemo(() => state.slides, [state.slides]);
  const activeSlideId = useMemo(() => state.activeSlideId, [state.activeSlideId]);

  const addSlide = useCallback(() => {
    return useCases.createSlide({
      format: activeSlide?.format,
      orientation: activeSlide?.orientation,
    });
  }, [activeSlide]);

  const removeSlide = useCallback((id: string) => {
    useCases.removeSlide(id);
  }, []);

  const duplicateSlide = useCallback((id: string) => {
    return useCases.duplicateSlide(id);
  }, []);

  const setActiveSlide = useCallback((id: string) => {
    useCases.setActiveSlide(id);
  }, []);

  const setSlides = useCallback((newSlides: Slide[]) => {
    slideStore.setSlides(newSlides);
  }, []);

  const changeFormat = useCallback((format: DocFormat) => {
    if (!activeSlide) return;
    useCases.changeFormat(activeSlide.id, format);
  }, [activeSlide]);

  const changeOrientation = useCallback((orientation: Orientation) => {
    if (!activeSlide) return;
    useCases.changeOrientation(activeSlide.id, orientation);
  }, [activeSlide]);

  const changeCustomSize = useCallback((width: number, height: number) => {
    if (!activeSlide) return;
    activeSlide.changeFormat('custom', { w: width, h: height });
    slideStore.updateSlide(activeSlide.id, activeSlide);
  }, [activeSlide]);

  const addElement = useCallback((element: CanvasElement) => {
    if (!activeSlide) return;
    useCases.addElement(activeSlide.id, element);
  }, [activeSlide]);

  const updateElement = useCallback((elementId: string, patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => {
    if (!activeSlide) return;
    useCases.updateElement({
      slideId: activeSlide.id,
      elementId,
      patch,
    });
  }, [activeSlide]);

  const removeElement = useCallback((elementId: string) => {
    if (!activeSlide) return;
    useCases.removeElement(activeSlide.id, elementId);
  }, [activeSlide]);

  const moveElementLayer = useCallback((elementId: string, direction: 1 | -1) => {
    if (!activeSlide) return;
    useCases.moveElementLayer(activeSlide.id, elementId, direction);
  }, [activeSlide]);

  return {
    slides,
    activeSlide,
    activeSlideId,
    addSlide,
    removeSlide,
    duplicateSlide,
    setActiveSlide,
    setSlides,
    changeFormat,
    changeOrientation,
    changeCustomSize,
    addElement,
    updateElement,
    removeElement,
    moveElementLayer,
  };
}

/* ============================================================

   РџРѕСЃР»Рµ РѕС‚Р»Р°РґРєРё вЂ” РІРµСЂРЅСѓС‚СЊ РІРµСЂСЃРёСЋ Р±РµР· Р»РѕРіРѕРІ.
   ============================================================ */

import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '../../shared/types';
import { findShapesContaining } from '../../lib/stamps/geometry';
import { generateId } from '../../shared/utils/id';

export interface UseStampFillModeArgs {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  texts: StampTextItem[];
  images: StampImageItem[];
  width: number;
  height: number;
  commit: (s: StampShapeItem[], t: StampTextItem[], i: StampImageItem[]) => void;
  select: (id: string | null, type: 'shape' | 'text' | 'image') => void;
}

export interface UseStampFillModeReturn {
  fillMode: boolean;
  setFillMode: (v: boolean) => void;
  fillColor: string;
  setFillColor: (v: string) => void;
  handleCanvasClick: (px: number, py: number) => void;
}

export function useStampFillMode({
  shapes,
  texts,
  images,
  width,
  height,
  commit,
  select,
}: UseStampFillModeArgs): UseStampFillModeReturn {
  const [fillMode, setFillMode] = useState(false);
  const [fillColor, setFillColor] = useState('#fbbf24');

  const handleCanvasClick = useCallback((px: number, py: number) => {
    console.log('[useStampFillMode] enter', {
      fillMode,
      px, py,
      shapesCount: shapes.length,
      kinds: shapes.map((s) => `${s.kind}:${s.id.slice(0, 6)}`),
    });

    if (!fillMode) return;

    const containing = findShapesContaining(px, py, shapes);
    console.log('[useStampFillMode] containing', containing.length,
      containing.map((s) => `${s.kind}:${s.id.slice(0, 6)}`));

    if (containing.length === 0) {
      console.warn('[useStampFillMode] no shape at', { px, py,
        candidates: shapes
          .filter((s) => s.kind !== 'fill' && !s.hidden)
          .map((s) => ({
            id: s.id.slice(0, 6), kind: s.kind,
            x: s.x, y: s.y, w: s.width, h: s.height,
          })),
      });
      return;
    }

    const target = containing.reduce((a, b) =>
      a.width * a.height <= b.width * b.height ? a : b,
    );
    console.log('[useStampFillMode] target', target.kind, target.id.slice(0, 6),
      { x: target.x, y: target.y, w: target.width, h: target.height });

    const localX = (px - target.x) / Math.max(1, target.width);
    const localY = (py - target.y) / Math.max(1, target.height);

    const fill: StampShapeItem = {
      id: generateId(),
      kind: 'fill',
      x: 0, y: 0,
      width, height,
      rotation: 0, cornerRadius: 0,
      stroke: '', strokeWidth: 0, dash: 'solid',
      fill: fillColor,
      fillTargetShapeId: target.id,
      fillAnchorLocal: { x: localX, y: localY },
      name: 'Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР° РѕР±Р»Р°СЃС‚Рё',
    };

    console.log('[useStampFillMode] committing fill', fill.id.slice(0, 6),
      'anchor', { x: localX, y: localY });

    commit([...shapes, fill], texts, images);
    select(fill.id, 'shape');
    setFillMode(false);
  }, [fillMode, fillColor, shapes, texts, images, width, height, commit, select]);

  return { fillMode, setFillMode, fillColor, setFillColor, handleCanvasClick };
}

import React, { createContext, useContext } from 'react';
import { DocumentStore } from '../../domain/document/DocumentStore';
import { SelectionStore } from '../stores/SelectionStore';
import { ModalsStore } from '../stores/ModalsStore';

export interface EditorStores {
  document: DocumentStore;
  selection: SelectionStore;
  modals: ModalsStore;
}

const EditorContext = createContext<EditorStores | null>(null);

export const EditorProvider: React.FC<{ stores: EditorStores; children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ stores, children }) => (
  <EditorContext.Provider value={stores}>{children}</EditorContext.Provider>
);

export function useStores(): EditorStores {
  const ctx = useContext(EditorContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useStores must be used within EditorProvider');
  return ctx;
}

export const useDocumentStore = () => useStores().document;
export const useSelectionStore = () => useStores().selection;
export const useModalsStore = () => useStores().modals;

  contextMenu: ContextMenuState | null;
  stampEditorTarget: string | null;
  diagramEditorTarget: string | null;

const INITIAL: ModalsState = {
  editing: null,
  floatingPos: null,
  contextMenu: null,
  stampEditorTarget: null,
  diagramEditorTarget: null,
  fillMode: false,
  fillColor: '#fbbf24',

/**
 * UI-СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ РјРѕРґР°Р»РѕРє Рё transient-СЂРµР¶РёРјРѕРІ.
 *
 * В§ARC-STATE-02: РµРґРёРЅС‹Р№ store + С‚РѕС‡РµС‡РЅС‹Рµ СЃРµР»РµРєС‚РѕСЂС‹.
 * В§ARC-STATE-06: slices editing / floating / contextMenu / stamp / diagram / fill
 *               вЂ” РІ РѕРґРЅРѕРј store, РЅРѕ СЃ СЂР°Р·РґРµР»СЊРЅС‹РјРё РјРµС‚РѕРґР°РјРё-РјСѓС‚Р°С‚РѕСЂР°РјРё.
 *
 * Р’СЃРµ РјСѓС‚Р°С†РёРё РёРґСѓС‚ С‡РµСЂРµР· `setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, ... }))`, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹
 * `SimpleStore` РЅРµ С‚РµСЂСЏР» РѕСЃС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»СЏ (РµРіРѕ `setState` вЂ” full replacement).

 * Clipboard Рё styleClipboard вЂ” РЅРµ РІ state, Р° РІ РїСЂРёРІР°С‚РЅС‹С… РїРѕР»СЏС…:

 * С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РІ СЂРµРЅРґРµСЂРµ ContextMenu / РїСЂРё commit.
 */
export class ModalsStore extends SimpleStore<ModalsState> {
  private clipboard: CanvasElement[] = [];
  private styleClipboard: Partial<CanvasElement> | null = null;

  constructor() {
    super(INITIAL);
  }

  /* ---------- editing ---------- */

  openEditor(state: EditingState) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      editing: state,
      floatingPos: null,
      contextMenu: null,
    }));
  }

  setEditing(state: EditingState | null) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, editing: state }));
  }

  closeEditor() {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, editing: null }));
  }

  /* ---------- floating ---------- */

  setFloatingPos(p: { x: number; y: number } | null) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, floatingPos: p }));
  }

  /* ---------- context menu ---------- */

  setContextMenu(m: ContextMenuState | null) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, contextMenu: m }));
  }

  /* ---------- stamp editor ---------- */

  openStampEditor(id: string) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      stampEditorTarget: id,
      floatingPos: null,
      contextMenu: null,
      editing: null,
    }));
  }

  closeStampEditor() {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, stampEditorTarget: null }));
  }

  /* ---------- diagram editor ---------- */

  openDiagramEditor(id: string) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      diagramEditorTarget: id,
      floatingPos: null,
      contextMenu: null,
      editing: null,
    }));
  }

  closeDiagramEditor() {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, diagramEditorTarget: null }));
  }

  /* ---------- fill mode ---------- */

  setFillMode(v: boolean) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      fillMode: v,
      fillPoints: v ? prev.fillPoints : [],
    }));
  }

  setFillColor(c: string) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, fillColor: c }));
  }

  setFillPoints(pts: number[]) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, fillPoints: pts }));
  }

  /* ---------- clipboard ---------- */

  copy(elements: CanvasElement[]) {
    this.clipboard = elements.map(
      (el) => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(el)) as CanvasElement,
    );
  }

  getClipboard(): CanvasElement[] {
    return this.clipboard;
  }

  copyStyle(element: CanvasElement) {
    const {
      id: _id,
      x: _x,
      y: _y,
      rotation: _r,
      type: _t,
      ...rest
    } = element as any;
    this.styleClipboard = rest as Partial<CanvasElement>;
  }

  getStyleClipboard(): Partial<CanvasElement> | null {
    return this.styleClipboard;
  }

  clearStyle() {
    this.styleClipboard = null;
  }
}

import { SimpleStore } from '../../shared/store/store';

export interface SelectionState {
  selectedIds: string[];
}

export class SelectionStore extends SimpleStore<SelectionState> {
  constructor() { super({ selectedIds: [] }); }
  select(id: string) { this.setState({ selectedIds: [id] }); }
  toggle(id: string) {
    this.setState((s) => ({
      selectedIds: s.selectedIds.includes(id)
        ? s.selectedIds.filter((x) => x !== id)
        : [...s.selectedIds, id],
    }));
  }
  setIds(ids: string[]) { this.setState({ selectedIds: ids }); }
  clear() { this.setState({ selectedIds: [] }); }
}

/**
 * Snap Service
 * Handles snapping to grid, elements, and guides
 */

import type { Slide } from '../domain/entities/Slide';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../shared/types';
import { GRID_SIZE, SNAP_THRESHOLD } from '../shared/constants';

export interface SnapResult {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  v: number[];
  h: number[];
}

export interface SnapOptions {
  snapToGrid?: boolean;
  snapToElements?: boolean;
  snapToCenter?: boolean;
  snapToEdges?: boolean;
}

/**
 * Snap position to grid
 */
export function snapToGrid(x: number, y: number): { x: number; y: number } {
  return {
    x: Math.round(x / GRID_SIZE) * GRID_SIZE,
    y: Math.round(y / GRID_SIZE) * GRID_SIZE,
  };
}

/**
 * Check if value is within threshold
 */
function isNear(value: number, target: number, threshold: number): boolean {
  return Math.abs(value - target) <= threshold;
}

/**
 * Compute snap for element drag
 */
export function computeSnap(
  element: CanvasElement,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  slide: Slide,
  options: SnapOptions = {}
): SnapResult {
  const { snapToGrid = true, snapToElements = true, snapToCenter = true, snapToEdges = true } = options;

  const result: SnapResult = {
    x,
    y,
    v: [],
    h: [],
  };

  // Snap to grid
  if (snapToGrid) {
    const gridX = Math.round(x / GRID_SIZE) * GRID_SIZE;
    const gridY = Math.round(y / GRID_SIZE) * GRID_SIZE;

    if (isNear(x, gridX, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
      result.x = gridX;
      result.v.push(gridX);
    }
    if (isNear(y, gridY, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
      result.y = gridY;
      result.h.push(gridY);
    }
  }

  // Get other elements for snapping
  const otherElements = slide.elements.filter((e) => e.id !== element.id);

  for (const other of otherElements) {
    // Snap to edges
    if (snapToEdges) {
      // Left edge to left edge
      if (isNear(x, other.x, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
        result.x = other.x;
        result.v.push(other.x);
      }
      // Left edge to right edge
      if (isNear(x, other.x + other.width, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
        result.x = other.x + other.width;
        result.v.push(other.x + other.width);
      }
      // Right edge to left edge
      if (isNear(x + element.width, other.x, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
        result.x = other.x - element.width;
        result.v.push(other.x);
      }
      // Right edge to right edge
      if (isNear(x + element.width, other.x + other.width, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
        result.x = other.x + other.width - element.width;
        result.v.push(other.x + other.width);
      }

      // Top edge to top edge
      if (isNear(y, other.y, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
        result.y = other.y;
        result.h.push(other.y);
      }
      // Top edge to bottom edge
      if (isNear(y, other.y + other.height, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
        result.y = other.y + other.height;
        result.h.push(other.y + other.height);
      }
      // Bottom edge to top edge
      if (isNear(y + element.height, other.y, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
        result.y = other.y - element.height;
        result.h.push(other.y);
      }
      // Bottom edge to bottom edge
      if (isNear(y + element.height, other.y + other.height, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
        result.y = other.y + other.height - element.height;
        result.h.push(other.y + other.height);
      }
    }

    // Snap to center
    if (snapToCenter) {
      const otherCenterX = other.x + other.width / 2;
      const otherCenterY = other.y + other.height / 2;
      const elementCenterX = x + element.width / 2;
      const elementCenterY = y + element.height / 2;

      // Horizontal center alignment
      if (isNear(elementCenterX, otherCenterX, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
        result.x = otherCenterX - element.width / 2;
        result.v.push(otherCenterX);
      }

      // Vertical center alignment
      if (isNear(elementCenterY, otherCenterY, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
        result.y = otherCenterY - element.height / 2;
        result.h.push(otherCenterY);
      }
    }
  }

  // Snap to slide center
  if (snapToCenter) {
    const slideCenterX = slide.width / 2;
    const slideCenterY = slide.height / 2;
    const elementCenterX = x + element.width / 2;
    const elementCenterY = y + element.height / 2;

    if (isNear(elementCenterX, slideCenterX, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
      result.x = slideCenterX - element.width / 2;
      result.v.push(slideCenterX);
    }
    if (isNear(elementCenterY, slideCenterY, SNAP_THRESHOLD)) {
      result.y = slideCenterY - element.height / 2;
      result.h.push(slideCenterY);
    }
  }

  return result;
}

/**
 * Get snap guides for display
 */
export function getSnapGuides(
  element: CanvasElement,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  slide: Slide
): { v: number[]; h: number[] } {
  const snap = computeSnap(element, x, y, slide);
  return {
    v: snap.v,
    h: snap.h,
  };
}

/**
 * Check if snap is active
 */
export function isSnapActive(
  element: CanvasElement,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  slide: Slide
): boolean {
  const snap = computeSnap(element, x, y, slide);
  return snap.x !== x || snap.y !== y;
}

import { useSyncExternalStore, useCallback } from 'react';

export type Listener = () => void;

export class SimpleStore<T> {
  private state: T;
  private listeners = new Set<Listener>();

  constructor(initialState: T) {
    this.state = initialState;
  }

  getState = (): T => this.state;

  setState = (nextState: T | ((prev: T) => T)) => {
    this.state = typeof nextState === 'function' 
      ? (nextState as Function)(this.state) 
      : nextState;
    this.listeners.forEach((l) => l());
  };

  subscribe = (listener: Listener) => {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => this.listeners.delete(listener);
  };
}

export function useStore<T, S>(store: SimpleStore<T>, selector: (state: T) => S): S {
  const getSnapshot = useCallback(() => selector(store.getState()), [store, selector]);
  return useSyncExternalStore(store.subscribe, getSnapshot, getSnapshot);
}

/**
 * ID generation utilities
 */

let counter = 0;

/**
 * Generate unique ID
 */
export function generateId(): string {
  const timestamp = Date.now().toString(36);
  const random = Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 8);
  const count = (counter++).toString(36);
  return `${timestamp}-${random}-${count}`;
}

/**
 * Generate ID with prefix
 */
export function generateIdWithPrefix(prefix: string): string {
  return `${prefix}-${generateId()}`;
}

/**
 * Check if string is valid ID
 */
export function isValidId(id: string): boolean {
  return typeof id === 'string' && id.length > 0;
}

/// <reference lib="webworker" />
/* ============================================================
   workers/fillWorker.ts
   РўРѕРЅРєР°СЏ РѕР±С‘СЂС‚РєР° РЅР°Рґ rasterizeCompositeFills.
   РќРёРєРѕРіРґР° РЅРµ Р±СЂРѕСЃР°РµС‚ вЂ” РЅР° Р»СЋР±РѕРµ РёСЃРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёРµ РѕС‚РґР°С‘С‚ ok:false,
   РёРЅР°С‡Рµ async onmessage Р·Р°РІРёСЃРЅРµС‚ Рё main thread Р±СѓРґРµС‚ Р¶РґР°С‚СЊ РІРµС‡РЅРѕ.
   ============================================================ */

import { rasterizeCompositeFills } from '../lib/stamps/rasterFill';
import type { FillWorkerRequest, FillWorkerResponse } from './protocol';

const ctx = self as unknown as DedicatedWorkerGlobalScope;

ctx.onmessage = async (e: MessageEvent<FillWorkerRequest>) => {
  const { seq, width, height, shapes, fills } = e.data;

  let response: FillWorkerResponse;

  try {
    const result = await rasterizeCompositeFills({ width, height, shapes, fills });
    response = result.ok
      ? { seq, ok: true, blob: result.value }
      : { seq, ok: false, reason: result.reason };
  } catch (err) {
    response = { seq, ok: false, reason: 'all-fills-rejected' };
    // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
    console.error('[fillWorker] unhandled exception', err);
  }

  ctx.postMessage(response);
};

/* ============================================================
   workers/protocol.ts
   РўРёРїС‹ СЃРѕРѕР±С‰РµРЅРёР№ main thread <-> fill worker.
   ============================================================ */

import type { FillSpec, FillError } from '../lib/stamps/rasterFill';
import type { StampShapeItem } from '../shared/types';

export interface FillWorkerRequest {
  seq: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  fills: FillSpec[];
}

export type FillWorkerResponse =
  | { seq: number; ok: true; blob: Blob }
  | { seq: number; ok: false; reason: FillError };
